PiG-Avatar: Hierarchical Neural-Field-Guided Gaussian Avatars
Authors: Julian Kaltheuner, Jan Spindler, Sina Kitz, Patrick Stotko, Reinhard Klein
First: 2026-05-19T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:59:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing Gaussian avatar methods typically parameterize geometry on a body-template surface, which entangles the avatar's representation space with the template's deformation space and limits the capture of layered, off-body, and non-rigid clothing geometry. We present PiG-Avatar, which addresses this limitation by using the parametric body model solely for kinematic transport, while representing the avatar as Gaussians anchored in a volumetric canonical space governed by a continuous neural field. This decouples representation from template topology, avoiding the geometric constraints of surface-based parameterizations. Kinematic coherence is maintained through 3D barycentric anchor transport, which guides motion without constraining geometry and allows anchors to deviate freely from the template surface, yielding dense, stable temporal surface correspondences by construction. To make this unconstrained formulation tractable, we introduce dual-level spatially coherent optimization, combining Sobolev-preconditioned neural-field updates with a novel KNN-based preconditioning of canonical anchor geometry. Together, these mechanisms induce an emergent self-organization of anchor density: anchors migrate toward regions of high curvature, appearance variation, and non-coherent motion without explicit heuristics. As a result, complex clothing geometry and layered surfaces emerge as natural, high-fidelity outputs. This single representation further supports hierarchical reconstruction across multiple levels of detail, with coarse-level supervision propagating to finer levels through the shared field and coupled anchor graph. On established benchmarks featuring subjects with complex clothing and challenging non-rigid motion, PiG-Avatar achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality, generalizes robustly to imperfect body model initialization, and renders in real time across all detail levels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PiG-Avatar：基于层次神经场引导的高斯化身
现有高斯化身方法通常在身体模板表面上参数化几何，这使化身的表示空间与模板的形变空间纠缠，并限制了对分层、体外及非刚性服装几何的捕捉。我们提出PiG-Avatar，通过仅将参数化身体模型用于运动传递，而将化身表示为锚定在由连续神经场控制的体素规范空间中的高斯点，从而解决这一限制。这使表示与模板拓扑解耦，避免了基于表面参数化的几何约束。通过3D重心锚点传递保持运动一致性，该方法引导运动而不约束几何，允许锚点自由偏离模板表面，从而自然生成密集且稳定的时序表面对应关系。为使该无约束公式可计算，我们引入双层空间一致优化，结合Sobolev预条件神经场更新与新颖的基于KNN的规范锚点几何预条件。这些机制共同诱导锚点密度的自组织：锚点自发迁移至高曲率、外观变化及非一致运动区域，无需显式启发式规则。因此，复杂服装几何与分层表面自然生成为高保真输出。该单一表示还支持多层级细节的层次重建，粗粒度监督通过共享场与耦合锚点图传播至细粒度层级。在包含复杂服装与挑战性非刚性运动的基准测试中，PiG-Avatar实现最先进渲染质量，对不完美身体模型初始化具有鲁棒泛化能力，并在所有细节层级实时渲染。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of surface-based Gaussian avatar methods that entangle geometry with template deformations, PiG-Avatar introduces a hierarchical neural-field-guided approach where Gaussians are anchored in a volumetric canonical space governed by a continuous neural field, decoupling representation from template topology. Kinematic coherence is preserved via 3D barycentric anchor transport, enabling motion without geometric constraints and yielding dense temporal correspondences. The method employs dual-level spatially coherent optimization—combining Sobolev-preconditioned neural-field updates with KNN-based anchor preconditioning—to induce self-organizing anchor density that clusters around high-curvature and motion-variant regions. This results in high-fidelity, layered clothing geometry and supports multi-level detail reconstruction with coarse-to-fine supervision. Experiments on benchmarks with complex clothing and non-rigid motion show state-of-the-art rendering quality, robust generalization to imperfect initialization, and real-time performance across all detail levels.
为了解决现有高斯头像方法因依赖体模板导致几何表示与模板形变纠缠的问题，PiG-Avatar仅用参数化体模型进行运动传输，将头像表示为受连续神经场引导的体素空间中锚定的高斯点。通过3D重心锚点传输保持运动一致性，允许锚点自由偏离模板表面，从而天然生成稳定的时间表面对应关系。为使该无约束框架可优化，引入双层空间一致优化机制，结合Sobolev预条件神经场更新与基于KNN的锚点几何预条件，促使锚点自发迁移至曲率高、外观变化大或运动不一致的区域，无需显式启发式规则。实验表明，该方法在复杂服装与非刚性运动的基准数据上达到最优渲染质量，对初始体模型误差鲁棒性强，且可在多层级细节下实时渲染。
MSAVBench: Towards Comprehensive and Reliable Evaluation of Multi-Shot Audio-Video Generation
Authors: Yujie Wei, Yujin Han, Zhekai Chen, Yongming Li, Kaixun Jiang, Zhihang Liu, Quanhao Li, Zhiwu Qing, Xiang Wang, Zhen Xing, Ruihang Chu, Lingyi Hong, Yefei He, Junjie Zhou, Junqiu Yu, Yang Shi, Difan Zou, Kai Zhu, Shiwei Zhang, Yingya Zhang, Yu Liu, Xihui Liu, Hongming Shan
First: 2026-05-19T17:59:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:59:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video generation is rapidly evolving from single-shot synthesis to complex multi-shot audio-video (MSAV) narratives to meet real-world demands. However, evaluating such frontier models remains a fundamental challenge. Existing benchmarks are limited in scope and data diversity, and rely on rigid evaluation pipelines, preventing systematic and reliable assessment of modern MSAV models. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MSAVBench, the first comprehensive benchmark and adaptive hybrid evaluation framework for multi-shot audio-video generation. Our benchmark spans four key dimensions, video, audio, shot, and reference, covering diverse task settings, varying shot counts of up to 15, and challenging non-realistic scenarios. Our evaluation framework improves robustness through an adaptive self-correction mechanism for shot segmentation, instance-wise rubrics for subjective metrics, and tool-grounded evidence extraction for complex judgments. Furthermore, MSAVBench achieves high alignment with human judgments, reaching a Spearman rank correlation of 91.5%. Our systematic evaluation of 19 state-of-the-art closed- and open-source models shows that current systems still struggle with director-level control and fine-grained audio-visual synchronization, while modular or agentic generation pipelines offer a promising path toward narrowing the gap between open- and closed-source models. We will release the benchmark data and evaluation code to facilitate future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MSAVBench：迈向全面可靠的多镜头音频视频生成评估
视频生成正从单镜头合成迅速演进为复杂的多镜头音频视频（MSAV）叙事，以满足现实需求。然而，评估此类前沿模型仍是一项根本性挑战。现有基准在范围和数据多样性上受限，且依赖刚性评估流程，阻碍了对现代MSAV模型的系统化、可靠评估。为弥合这些差距，我们提出了MSAVBench，这是首个全面的多镜头音频视频生成基准及自适应混合评估框架。我们的基准涵盖四个关键维度——视频、音频、镜头和参考，覆盖多样任务设置，支持最多15个镜头的变体，并包含具有挑战性的非现实场景。评估框架通过镜头分割的自适应自校正机制、实例级主观指标评分标准，以及基于工具的复杂判断证据提取，提升评估鲁棒性。此外，MSAVBench与人类判断高度一致，Spearman秩相关系数达91.5%。我们对19个当前最先进的闭源与开源模型进行系统评估，发现现有系统在导演级控制和细粒度音视频同步方面仍存在困难，而模块化或代理式生成流程为缩小开源与闭源模型之间的差距提供了有前景的路径。我们将发布基准数据与评估代码，以促进未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to evaluate complex multi-shot audio-video generation models in real-world scenarios, MSAVBench introduces the first comprehensive benchmark and adaptive evaluation framework. It covers four dimensions—video, audio, shot, and reference—with diverse task settings and up to 15 shots, including non-realistic challenges. The framework enhances reliability via adaptive shot segmentation, instance-wise subjective rubrics, and tool-grounded evidence extraction. Experimental results show a 91.5% Spearman correlation with human judgments and reveal that current models struggle with director-level control and fine-grained audio-visual sync, while modular or agentic pipelines show promise in reducing performance gaps between open- and closed-source models.
为应对复杂多镜头音频视频生成模型在现实场景中的评估需求，MSAVBench首次提出全面的基准与自适应评估框架，涵盖视频、音频、镜头及参考四个维度，支持多样任务与最多15镜头的非现实挑战场景。评估框架通过自适应镜头分割、实例级评分标准及工具驱动证据提取提升鲁棒性，实验表明其与人类评分相关性达91.5%，并发现当前模型在导演级控制与细粒度音视频同步方面仍存不足，而模块化或代理生成架构有望缩小开源与闭源模型间的性能差距。
Atoms of Thought: Universal EEG Representation Learning with Microstates
Authors: Xinyang Tian, Ruitao Liu, Ziyi Ye, Siyang Xue, Xin Wang, Xuesong Chen
First: 2026-05-19T17:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:59:31+00:00
Comments: Accepted by the 3rd International Workshop on Multimodal and Responsible Affective Computing (MRAC 2025). 8 pages of main text, 23 pages total, 5 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning universal representations from electroencephalogram (EEG) signals is a cutting-edge approach in the field of neuroinformatics and brain-computer interfaces (BCIs). Conventionally, EEG is treated as a multivariate temporal signal, where time- or frequency-domain features are extracted for representation learning. This paper investigates a simple yet effective EEG representation, i.e., microstates. Microstates represent the building blocks of brain activity patterns at a microscopic time scale. We build a universal microstate tokenizer from a large medical EEG dataset by clustering continuous EEG signals into sequences of discrete microstates. The microstate tokenizer is then adopted universally across a series of downstream tasks, including sleep staging, emotion recognition, and motor imagery classification. Experimental results show that EEG representation learning with microstates outperforms traditional time-domain and frequency-domain features under different models and across different tasks. Further analysis shows that microstates offer greater interpretability and scalability, thereby opening up applications in both cognitive neuroscience and clinical research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：思想的原子：基于微状态的通用脑电图表示学习
从脑电图（EEG）信号中学习通用表示是神经信息学和脑机接口（BCI）领域的前沿方法。传统上，EEG被视为多变量时序信号，从中提取时域或频域特征用于表示学习。本文研究了一种简单而有效的EEG表示方法——微状态。微状态代表了在微观时间尺度上大脑活动模式的基本构建单元。我们通过聚类连续的EEG信号，从大型医学EEG数据集中构建了一个通用微状态分词器，将其序列化为离散微状态序列。该微状态分词器随后被广泛应用于一系列下游任务，包括睡眠分期、情绪识别和运动想象分类。实验结果表明，在不同模型和不同任务中，基于微状态的EEG表示学习均优于传统的时域和频域特征。进一步分析显示，微状态具有更高的可解释性和可扩展性，从而为认知神经科学和临床研究开辟了新应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for universal EEG representations in neuroinformatics and brain-computer interfaces, this paper proposes microstates—discrete, time-scale building blocks of brain activity—as a simple yet effective representation method. The authors construct a universal microstate tokenizer by clustering continuous EEG signals from a large medical dataset into discrete sequences. This tokenizer is then applied across multiple downstream tasks including sleep staging, emotion recognition, and motor imagery classification. Experimental results demonstrate that microstate-based representations consistently outperform traditional time- and frequency-domain features across models and tasks, while also offering enhanced interpretability and scalability for both cognitive neuroscience and clinical applications.
为解决神经信息学和脑机接口中对通用EEG表示的需求，该研究提出以微状态（脑活动的离散瞬态模式）作为基础表示方法。方法通过从大型医学EEG数据集中聚类连续信号构建通用微状态分词器，并将其应用于睡眠分期、情绪识别和运动想象分类等下游任务。实验表明，基于微状态的表示在多种模型和任务中均优于传统时域与频域特征，同时具备更强的可解释性和可扩展性，适用于认知神经科学与临床研究。
TIDE: Efficient and Lossless MoE Diffusion LLM Inference with I/O-aware Expert Offload
Authors: Zhiben Chen, Youpeng Zhao, Yang Sui, Jun Wang, Yuzhang Shang
First: 2026-05-19T17:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:59:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding. However, as dLLMs continue to scale up with mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures, their deployment on resource-constrained devices remains an open challenge. Existing AR-based methods often incur either prohibitive I/O overhead or significant compute bottlenecks. In this work, we propose TIDE, a novel resource-efficient inference system that leverages the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block. Specifically, we leverage the temporal stability of expert activations during the diffusion process within the block and introduce an interval-based expert refresh strategy that updates the expert placement in an I/O-aware fashion. To ensure optimal performance, we formulate the inference scheduling as a mathematical programming problem, solving for the optimal interval that minimizes I/O traffic and CPU computation. Most importantly, TIDE is a lossless optimization that requires no model training, providing a "free lunch" acceleration for dLLM inference. In a single GPU-CPU system, we demonstrate that TIDE achieves up to 1.4$\times$ and 1.5$\times$ throughput improvements over prior baselines on LLaDA2.0-mini and LLaDA2.0-flash models, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIDE：面向I/O感知专家卸载的高效无损MoE扩散大语言模型推理
扩散式大语言模型（dLLMs）作为自回归（AR）模型的有力替代方案，通过块级并行解码实现更好的硬件利用率和双向上下文能力。然而，随着dLLMs采用混合专家（MoE）架构不断扩展，其在资源受限设备上的部署仍具挑战性。现有AR方法常面临高昂的I/O开销或显著的计算瓶颈。本文提出TIDE，一种新颖的资源高效推理系统，利用扩散过程中块内专家激活的时序稳定性，引入基于时间间隔的专家刷新策略，以I/O感知方式更新专家部署。为确保最优性能，我们将推理调度建模为数学规划问题，求解最小化I/O流量与CPU计算的最优间隔。最重要的是，TIDE是一种无损优化，无需模型训练，为dLLM推理提供“免费午餐”式加速。在单GPU-CPU系统中，TIDE在LLaDA2.0-mini和LLaDA2.0-flash模型上分别实现比现有基线最高1.4倍和1.5倍的吞吐量提升。
Summary / 总结
Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive (AR) models, offering better hardware utilization and bidirectional context through parallel block-level decoding.
为提升MoE架构扩散大语言模型在资源受限设备上的部署效率，TIDE提出一种基于专家激活时序稳定性的I/O感知专家卸载策略，通过将推理调度建模为数学优化问题，求解最优专家刷新间隔以最小化I/O与CPU开销。实验表明，在单GPU-CPU系统上，TIDE相较现有方法在LLaDA2.0-mini和LLaDA2.0-flash模型上分别实现最高1.4倍和1.5倍的吞吐量提升，且无需模型训练。
From Seeing to Thinking: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning Improves Post-Training of Vision-Language Models
Authors: Juncheng Wu, Hardy Chen, Haoqin Tu, Xianfeng Tang, Freda Shi, Hui Liu, Hanqing Lu, Cihang Xie, Yuyin Zhou
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-19T17:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:58:40+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 9 figures; Accepted to ICML 2026; Project Page: https://ucsc-vlaa.github.io/VLM-CapCurriculum/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) emphasize long chain-of-thought reasoning; yet, we find that their performance on visual tasks is primarily limited by a lack of visual perception as opposed to reasoning itself. In this work, we systematically study the interplay between perception and reasoning in VLM post-training by decomposing their capabilities into three separate training stages: visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning, incorporating specialized training data. We demonstrate that visual perception (a) requires targeted optimization with specialized data; (b) serves as a fundamental scaffold that should be solidified through staged training before refining visual reasoning; and (c) is more effectively learned via RL than caption-based SFT. Our experiments across multiple VLMs demonstrate that staged training consistently improves both visual perception and reasoning performance over merged training. Notably, models trained with our approach achieve 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy with 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, suggesting that superior perception reduces the need for excessive reasoning. Furthermore, we show that this capability-based staging represents a new curriculum dimension orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, and combining both yields further additive gains. Our staged-training models achieve superior performance among open-weight VLMs, establishing advanced results on several visual math and perception (e.g., +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA) tasks compared with the base counterpart.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从感知到思考：解耦感知与推理可提升视觉语言模型的后训练效果
近期视觉语言模型（VLMs）的进展强调长链推理能力；然而，我们发现其在视觉任务上的表现主要受限于视觉感知能力不足，而非推理本身。本研究系统性地研究了VLM后训练中感知与推理的相互作用，将其能力分解为三个独立训练阶段：视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理，并结合专用训练数据。我们证明：视觉感知（a）需通过专用数据进行针对性优化；（b）是基础支撑结构，应在强化视觉推理前通过分阶段训练巩固；（c）相比基于字幕的监督微调（SFT），通过强化学习（RL）更有效学习。多模型实验表明，分阶段训练在视觉感知与推理性能上均优于合并训练。值得注意的是，采用本方法训练的模型推理准确率提升1.5%，推理路径缩短20.8%，表明更强的感知能力可减少冗余推理。此外，我们证明该能力导向的分阶段训练代表一种与传统难度导向课程正交的新维度，二者结合可产生叠加收益。本方法训练的模型在开源VLM中表现最优，在多项视觉数学与感知任务（如WeMath提升+5.2%，RealWorldQA提升+3.7%）上超越基线模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that visual perception, not reasoning, is the primary bottleneck in vision-language models, this work decouples perception and reasoning into three distinct training stages—visual perception, visual reasoning, and textual reasoning—each with specialized data. The method employs staged training, where visual perception is first optimized using reinforcement learning rather than caption-based supervised fine-tuning, and then serves as a foundational scaffold before visual reasoning is refined. Experiments across multiple VLMs show that this staged approach consistently outperforms merged training, yielding 1.5% higher reasoning accuracy and 20.8% shorter reasoning traces, indicating that better perception reduces reliance on lengthy reasoning. The staged curriculum, orthogonal to traditional difficulty-based curricula, further boosts performance when combined, with models achieving +5.2% on WeMath and +3.7% on RealWorldQA over base models.
该论文指出，视觉语言模型在视觉任务中的性能瓶颈主要源于感知能力不足，而非推理能力缺陷。作者提出一种分阶段训练方法，将视觉感知、视觉推理和文本推理三个能力模块依次训练，并使用专门数据集进行优化。实验表明，感知能力需优先通过针对性数据优化，作为推理的基础支撑，并且强化学习比基于字幕的监督微调更有效。采用该分阶段训练方法的模型在视觉推理准确率上提升1.5%，推理路径缩短20.8%，说明更强的感知能力可减少对过度推理的依赖。此外，将该感知导向课程与传统难度导向课程结合可进一步提升性能，分阶段训练模型在开放权重VLM中表现最佳，于WeMath和RealWorldQA任务上分别提升5.2%和3.7%。
ClinSeekAgent: Automating Multimodal Evidence Seeking for Agentic Clinical Reasoning
Authors: Juncheng Wu, Letian Zhang, Yuhan Wang, Haoqin Tu, Hardy Chen, Zijun Wang, Cihang Xie, Yuyin Zhou
First: 2026-05-19T17:58:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:58:37+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 9 figures; Project Page: https://ucsc-vlaa.github.io/ClinSeekAgent/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems have shown promise for clinical decision support, but existing works largely assume that evidence has already been curated and handed to the model. Real-world clinical workflows instead require agents to actively seek, iteratively plan, and synthesize multimodal evidence from heterogeneous sources. In this paper, we introduce ClinSeekAgent, an automated agentic framework for dynamic multimodal evidence seeking that shifts the paradigm from passive evidence consumption to active evidence acquisition. Given only a clinical query and access to raw data sources, ClinSeekAgent gathers evidence by querying medical knowledge bases, navigating raw EHRs, and invoking medical imaging tools; refines its hypotheses as new information emerges; and integrates the collected evidence into grounded clinical decisions. ClinSeekAgent serves both as an inference-time agent for frontier LLMs and as a training-time pipeline for distilling high-quality agent trajectories into compact open-source models. To validate its inference-time effectiveness, we construct ClinSeek-Bench, which pairs Curated Input reasoning from fixed pre-selected evidence with Automated Evidence-Seeking over raw clinical data. On text-only EHR tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 60.0 to 63.2 overall F1 and MiniMax M2.5 from 43.1 to 47.3, with positive risk-prediction gains in 7 out of 9 evaluated host models. On multimodal tasks, ClinSeekAgent improves Claude Opus 4.6 from 47.5 to 62.6 (+15.1); all evaluated models improve across the three CXR-related task groups. We further validate ClinSeekAgent as a training pipeline by distilling agentic evidence-seeking trajectories into ClinSeek-35B-A3B, which achieves 34.0 average F1 on existing AgentEHR-Bench, improving over its Qwen3.5-35B-A3B baseline by +11.9 points and approaching Claude Opus 4.6.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ClinSeekAgent：自动化多模态证据检索以支持自主临床推理
大型语言模型（LLM）和自主系统在临床决策支持方面展现出潜力，但现有研究大多假设证据已预先整理并提供给模型。现实中的临床工作流程则要求代理主动检索、迭代规划并从异构来源综合多模态证据。本文提出ClinSeekAgent，一个动态多模态证据检索的自动化自主框架，将范式从被动证据消费转向主动证据获取。仅给定临床查询和原始数据源访问权限，ClinSeekAgent通过查询医学知识库、导航原始电子健康记录（EHR）并调用医学影像工具收集证据；随着新信息出现不断修正假设；并将收集的证据整合为基于证据的临床决策。ClinSeekAgent既可作为前沿LLM推理时的代理，也可作为训练时的管道，将高质量代理轨迹蒸馏为紧凑的开源模型。为验证其推理时有效性，我们构建ClinSeek-Bench，将固定预选证据的Curated Input推理与原始临床数据上的自动化证据检索配对。在纯文本EHR任务中，ClinSeekAgent使Claude Opus F1从60.0提升至63.2，MiniMax M2.5从43.1提升至47.3，9个评估主机模型中有7个风险预测表现提升。在多模态任务中，ClinSeekAgent使Claude Opus F1从47.5提升至62.6（+15.1）；所有评估模型在三个CXR相关任务组中均有所提升。我们进一步验证ClinSeekAgent作为训练管道的能力，将其代理证据检索轨迹蒸馏为ClinSeek-35B-A3B，该模型在AgentEHR-Bench上平均F1达34.0，较Qwen3.5-35B-A3B基线提升11.9分，接近Claude Opus 4.6表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for clinical agents to autonomously gather evidence from raw, heterogeneous sources rather than relying on pre-curated data, ClinSeekAgent introduces an agentic framework that dynamically seeks, plans, and synthesizes multimodal evidence. It queries medical knowledge bases, navigates EHRs, and invokes imaging tools to collect evidence, then refines hypotheses and integrates findings into clinical decisions. Evaluated on text-only and multimodal tasks, it boosts performance across multiple LLMs—e.g., improving Claude Opus 4.6’s F1 from 60.0 to 63.2 on text tasks and from 47.5 to 62.6 on multimodal CXR tasks. As a training pipeline, it distills agent trajectories into ClinSeek-35B-A3B, achieving 34.0 F1 on AgentEHR-Bench, a 11.9-point gain over its baseline.
为解决临床代理需主动从原始异构数据中获取并整合证据而非依赖预整理数据的问题，ClinSeekAgent提出一种动态多模态证据获取框架，通过查询知识库、导航电子病历及调用影像工具主动搜集证据，并在新信息出现时迭代优化假设，最终形成临床决策。该框架既支持推理时的代理推理，也支持训练时的轨迹蒸馏。实验表明，其在纯文本EHR任务中使Claude Opus 4.6的F1从60.0提升至63.2，MiniMax M2.5从43.1提升至47.3；在多模态任务中，Claude Opus 4.6得分从47.5跃升至62.6（+15.1），所有评估模型在CXR相关任务中均获提升。作为训练管道，蒸馏后的ClinSeek-35B-A3B在AgentEHR-Bench上平均F1达34.0，较Qwen3.5-35B-A3B基线提升11.9分，接近Claude Opus 4.6表现。
An Empirical Investigation of Reconstruction-Based Models for Seizure Prediction from ECG Signals
Authors: Mohammad Reza Chopannavaz, Foad Ghaderi
First: 2025-04-11T09:33:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:57:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Epileptic seizures are transient neurological events characterized by abnormal and excessive neuron activity in the brain, which are often associated with measurable disturbances in the cardiovascular system. Traditionally, electroencephalogram (EEG) signals have served as the primary modality for seizure prediction due to their direct measurement of brain activity and high diagnostic precision. However, their cost, sensitivity to noise, and practical deployment constraints limit their applicability outside controlled clinical environments. To overcome these challenges, recent studies have increasingly investigated electrocardiogram (ECG) signals as a practical and non-invasive alternative for seizure prediction in real-world settings. Evidence suggests that ECG-derived cardiac signatures may precede clinical seizure onset, offering a viable window for early detection. In this paper, we propose a reconstruction-based anomaly detection framework that integrates time-frequency representations with advanced deep learning models to capture deviations in heart rate dynamics associated with seizure onset. Afterward, reconstruction error is smoothed, and an adaptive thresholding strategy is applied to reduce false alarms. The method was evaluated on the Siena database, achieving a specificity of 99.16%, accuracy of 76.05%, and a false positive rate (FPR) of 0.01/h, with an average prediction horizon of 45 minutes prior to seizure onset. These results demonstrate that ECG-based prediction can provide clinically actionable early warnings while improving patient accessibility and comfort. Nevertheless, this performance reflects a trade-off favoring high specificity over sensitivity, resulting in reduced FPR and aligning with clinical requirements for reliable deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于重构模型的ECG信号癫痫发作预测的实证研究
癫痫发作是短暂的神经事件，表现为大脑中异常且过度的神经元活动，常伴有心血管系统的可测量扰动。传统上，脑电图（EEG）信号因其直接测量脑活动和高诊断精度，被作为癫痫发作预测的主要手段。然而，其成本高、易受噪声干扰及实际部署限制，使其难以在非临床环境中应用。为克服这些挑战，近期研究越来越多地探索心电图（ECG）信号作为现实世界中癫痫发作预测的实用且无创替代方案。证据表明，ECG衍生的心脏特征可能先于临床发作出现，为早期检测提供窗口。本文提出一种基于重构的异常检测框架，结合时频表示与先进深度学习模型，以捕捉与癫痫发作相关的节律动态偏差。随后，重构误差被平滑处理，并采用自适应阈值策略以降低误报。该方法在Siena数据库上评估，达到99.16%的特异性、76.05%的准确率和0.01/h的假阳性率，平均预测窗口为发作前45分钟。结果表明，ECG基预测可提供临床可操作的早期预警，同时提升患者可及性与舒适度。然而，该性能体现了以高特异性为优先的权衡，降低了假阳性率，符合临床可靠部署的需求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of EEG in real-world seizure prediction due to cost and noise sensitivity, this study explores ECG as a viable, non-invasive alternative. The method employs a reconstruction-based anomaly detection framework that combines time-frequency representations with deep learning to model normal heart rate dynamics and detect deviations preceding seizures. Evaluated on the Siena database, the model achieved 99.16% specificity, 76.05% accuracy, and a false positive rate of 0.01/h, with an average 45-minute prediction window before seizure onset, demonstrating clinical utility while prioritizing high specificity over sensitivity.
鉴于EEG在现实场景中用于癫痫预测存在成本高、易受噪声干扰等局限，本研究探索ECG作为更实用、无创的替代方案。方法采用基于重构的异常检测框架，结合时频表示与深度学习模型，捕捉癫痫发作前心率动态的异常变化，并通过自适应阈值策略降低误报。在Siena数据库上的实验表明，该方法达到99.16%的特异性、76.05%的准确率，误报率为每小时0.01次，平均预测窗口为发作前45分钟，验证了临床可行性，同时以高特异性为优先，牺牲部分敏感性以满足临床部署需求。
A first-order method for nonconvex-nonconcave minimax problems under a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition
Authors: Zhaosong Lu, Xiangyuan Wang
First: 2025-07-02T17:45:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:55:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by SIAM Journal on Optimization
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a class of nonconvex-nonconcave minimax problems in which the inner maximization problem satisfies a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) condition that may vary with the outer minimization variable. In contrast to the global KL or Polyak-Lojasiewicz (PL) conditions commonly assumed in the literature -- which are significantly stronger and often too restrictive in practice -- this local KL condition accommodates a broader range of practical scenarios. However, it also introduces new analytical challenges. In particular, as an optimization algorithm progresses toward a stationary point of the problem, the region over which the KL condition holds may shrink, resulting in a more intricate and potentially ill-conditioned landscape. To address this challenge, we show that the associated maximal function is locally generalized Hölder smooth. Leveraging this key property, we develop an inexact proximal gradient method for solving the minimax problem, where the inexact gradient of the maximal function is computed by applying a proximal gradient method to a KL-structured subproblem. Under mild assumptions, we establish complexity guarantees for computing an approximate stationary point of the minimax problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在局部Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz条件下非凸非凹极小极大问题的一阶方法
我们研究一类非凸非凹极小极大问题，其中内层最大化问题满足随外层最小化变量变化的局部Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz（KL）条件。与文献中通常假设的全局KL或Polyak-Lojasiewicz（PL）条件不同——这些条件显著更强且在实践中往往过于严格——该局部KL条件可适应更广泛的实用场景。然而，它也带来了新的分析挑战。特别是，当优化算法向问题的驻点逼近时，KL条件成立的区域可能缩小，导致更复杂且可能病态的景观。为应对这一挑战，我们证明了相关最大函数在局部具有广义Hölder光滑性。利用这一关键性质，我们开发了一种求解极小极大问题的不精确近端梯度方法，其中最大函数的不精确梯度通过在KL结构子问题上应用近端梯度方法计算。在温和假设下，我们建立了计算极小极大问题近似驻点的复杂度保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle nonconvex-nonconcave minimax problems that are too complex for global assumptions like Polyak-Lojasiewicz or global KL conditions, this paper introduces a first-order method under a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition that varies with the outer variable. The method constructs an inexact proximal gradient algorithm by approximating the maximal function’s gradient via solving a KL-structured subproblem, leveraging its local generalized Hölder smoothness. Under mild assumptions, the algorithm achieves complexity guarantees for finding an approximate stationary point, demonstrating effectiveness in scenarios where global assumptions fail.
为解决传统全局KL或PL条件过于严苛、难以适应实际非凸非凹minimax问题的局限，本文在局部Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz条件下提出一种一阶方法。该方法利用最大函数的局部广义Hölder光滑性，采用近似近端梯度法，其中梯度近似通过求解KL结构子问题获得。在较弱假设下，算法可保证计算近似驻点的复杂度，展现出对实际非光滑场景更强的适应性。
GraphPINE: Graph Importance Propagation for Interpretable Drug Response Prediction
Authors: Yoshitaka Inoue, Tianfan Fu, Augustin Luna
First: 2025-04-07T19:42:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:55:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explainability is necessary for many tasks in biomedical research. Recent explainability methods have focused on attention, gradient, and Shapley value. These do not handle data with strong associated prior knowledge and fail to constrain explainability results based on known relationships between predictive features. We propose GraphPINE, a graph neural network (GNN) architecture leveraging domain-specific prior knowledge to initialize node importance optimized during training for drug response prediction. Typically, a manual post-prediction step examines literature (i.e., prior knowledge) to understand returned predictive features. While node importance can be obtained for gradient and attention after prediction, node importance from these methods lacks complementary prior knowledge; GraphPINE seeks to overcome this limitation. GraphPINE differs from other GNN gating methods by utilizing an LSTM-like sequential format. We introduce an importance propagation layer that unifies 1) updates for feature matrix and node importance and 2) uses GNN-based graph propagation of feature values. This initialization and updating mechanism allows for informed feature learning and improved graph representation. We apply GraphPINE to cancer drug response prediction using drug screening and gene data collected for over 5,000 gene nodes included in a gene-gene graph with a drug-target interaction (DTI) graph for initial importance. The gene-gene graph and DTIs were obtained from curated sources and weighted by article count discussing relationships between drugs and genes. GraphPINE achieves a PR-AUC of 0.894 and ROC-AUC of 0.796 across 952 drugs. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GraphPINE-40DE.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GraphPINE：用于可解释药物反应预测的图重要性传播
在生物医学研究中，可解释性对许多任务至关重要。近期的可解释性方法主要关注注意力、梯度和Shapley值，但这些方法无法处理具有强关联先验知识的数据，且未能根据已知的预测特征间关系约束可解释性结果。 我们提出GraphPINE，一种利用领域特定先验知识初始化节点重要性并在此基础上进行训练优化的图神经网络（GNN）架构，用于药物反应预测。通常，预测后需手动查阅文献（即先验知识）以理解返回的预测特征。虽然梯度和注意力方法可在预测后获得节点重要性，但这些方法的节点重要性缺乏互补的先验知识；GraphPINE旨在克服这一局限。GraphPINE不同于其他GNN门控方法，采用类似LSTM的序列格式。我们引入一个重要性传播层，统一1）特征矩阵和节点重要性的更新，2）基于GNN的图传播特征值。该初始化与更新机制支持有指导的特征学习和改进的图表示。 我们将GraphPINE应用于癌症药物反应预测，使用超过5000个基因节点的基因-基因图和药物-靶点相互作用（DTI）图进行初始重要性初始化。基因-基因图和DTIs来源于人工整理的资源，并根据讨论药物与基因关系的文章数量加权。GraphPINE在952种药物上实现了PR-AUC为0.894、ROC-AUC为0.796的性能。代码可在https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GraphPINE-40DE获取。
Summary / 总结
Explainability is necessary for many tasks in biomedical research.
为提升药物反应预测的可解释性，GraphPINE提出一种结合领域先验知识的图神经网络架构，通过训练前初始化节点重要性并动态更新，使特征学习更具生物学依据。不同于依赖后处理注意力或梯度的现有方法，GraphPINE采用类似LSTM的序列结构与重要性传播层，同步更新特征矩阵与节点重要性，并结合图传播机制。在包含5000多个基因和952种药物的癌症药物响应数据集上，该方法在PR-AUC和ROC-AUC上分别达到0.894和0.796，验证了其在融合先验知识基础上提升预测性能与可解释性的有效性。
Multi-axis Analysis of Image Manipulation Localization
Authors: Keanu Nichols, Divya Appapogu, Giscard Biamby, Dina Bashkirova, Anna Rohrbach, Bryan A. Plummer
First: 2026-05-19T17:54:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:54:33+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Advanced image editing software enables easy creation of highly convincing image manipulations, which has been made even more accessible in recent years due to advances in generative AI. Manipulated images, while often harmless, could spread misinformation, create false narratives, and influence people's opinions on important issues. Despite this growing threat, there is limited research on detecting advanced manipulations across different visual domains. Thus, we introduce Analysis Under Domain-shifts, qualIty, Type, and Size (AUDITS), a comprehensive benchmark designed for studying axes of analysis in image manipulation detection. AUDITS comprises over 530K images from two distinct sources (user and news photos). We curate our dataset to support analysis across multiple axes using recent diffusion-based inpaintings, spanning a diverse range of manipulation types and sizes. We conduct experiments under different types of domain shift to evaluate robustness of existing image manipulation detection methods. Our goal is to drive further research in this area by offering new insights that would help develop more reliable and generalizable image manipulation detection methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：图像篡改定位的多轴分析
先进的图像编辑软件使创建高度逼真的图像篡改变得容易，而近年来生成式人工智能的进步进一步降低了门槛。尽管篡改图像通常无害，但可能传播虚假信息、制造错误叙事并影响人们对重要议题的看法。尽管这一威胁日益增长，但针对不同视觉领域中高级篡改检测的研究仍十分有限。因此，我们提出了“域偏移、质量、类型与尺寸分析”（AUDITS）基准，旨在研究图像篡改检测中的多维分析轴。AUDITS包含来自两个不同来源（用户照片与新闻照片）的超过53万张图像。我们精心构建数据集，支持使用近期基于扩散的图像修复技术进行多轴分析，涵盖多样化的篡改类型与尺寸。我们在不同类型的域偏移下进行实验，以评估现有图像篡改检测方法的鲁棒性。我们的目标是通过提供新见解，推动该领域进一步研究，助力开发更可靠、更具泛化能力的图像篡改检测方法。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the increasing prevalence of convincing image manipulations enabled by generative AI, this work addresses the lack of systematic evaluation for detecting such manipulations across diverse visual contexts. The authors introduce AUDITS, a large-scale benchmark comprising over 530K images from user and news sources, curated to support analysis along multiple axes including domain shift, quality, type, and size using diffusion-based inpainting. Experiments evaluate existing detection methods under varied domain shifts, revealing significant performance degradation when models encounter unseen visual domains or manipulation styles. The results highlight the need for more robust, generalizable detection systems capable of handling real-world variability in manipulated imagery.
鉴于生成式AI工具使图像篡改日益普及，本研究针对当前缺乏对多视觉域图像篡改系统性检测的研究现状，提出AUDITS基准数据集，包含超过53万张来自用户和新闻来源的图像，支持按领域偏移、质量、类型和尺寸等多维度分析。通过扩散模型修复技术，作者在不同领域偏移条件下评估现有检测方法，发现多数模型在面对未见过的视觉上下文或篡改风格时性能显著下降，凸显了构建更鲁棒、更具泛化能力的图像篡改检测方法的迫切需求。
A Methodology for Selecting and Composing Runtime Architecture Patterns for Production LLM Agents
Authors: Vasundra Srinivasan
First: 2026-05-19T17:54:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:54:21+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables. Companion repo at https://github.com/vasundras/agent-runtime-patterns
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Production LLM agents combine stochastic model outputs with deterministic software systems, yet the boundary between the two is rarely treated as a first-class architectural object. This paper names that boundary the stochastic-deterministic boundary (SDB): a four-part contract among a proposer, verifier, commit step, and reject signal that specifies how an LLM output becomes a system action. We argue that the SDB is the load-bearing primitive of production agent runtimes. Around this primitive, we organize agent runtime design into three concerns: Coordination, State, and Control. We present a catalog of six runtime patterns that compose the SDB differently across conversational, autonomous, and long-horizon agents: hierarchical delegation, scatter-gather plus saga, event-driven sequencing, shared state machine, supervisor plus gate, and human in the loop. For each pattern, we trace its lineage to distributed-systems concepts and identify what changes when the worker is stochastic. The paper contributes a five-step methodology for selecting runtime patterns, a diagnostic procedure that maps production failures to pattern weaknesses, and a failure mode called replay divergence, in which LLM-based consumers of a deterministic event log produce different downstream outputs under model-version or prompt changes. A stylized reliability decomposition separates per-call model variance from architectural momentum, motivating the claim that as model variance decreases, pattern choice and SDB strength become increasingly important levers for long-run reliability. We apply the methodology to five workloads and provide one runnable reference implementation for a 90-day contract-renewal agent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生产级大语言模型代理运行时架构模式的选择与组合方法论
生产级大语言模型代理将随机模型输出与确定性软件系统结合，但两者之间的边界通常未被视为首要的架构对象。本文将该边界命名为随机-确定性边界（SDB）：它是一个四部分契约，涉及提议者、验证者、提交步骤和拒绝信号，规定了LLM输出如何转化为系统动作。我们认为SDB是生产级代理运行时的核心承载原语。 围绕该原语，我们将代理运行时设计划分为三个关注点：协调、状态与控制。我们提出六种运行时模式，它们在对话型、自主型和长时程代理中以不同方式组合SDB：分层委派、散收加Saga、事件驱动序列化、共享状态机、监督者加门控，以及人机协同。对每种模式，我们追溯其分布式系统概念的渊源，并指出当工作者为随机时所发生的变化。 本文贡献了一种五步方法论用于选择运行时模式，一种诊断程序将生产故障映射到模式弱点，并提出一种称为“重放发散”的故障模式——即基于LLM的确定性事件日志消费者在模型版本或提示变化下产生不同的下游输出。一种风格化的可靠性分解将每次调用的模型方差与架构惯性分离，从而支持“随着模型方差降低，模式选择与SDB强度对长期可靠性的重要性日益凸显”的主张。我们对五个工作负载应用该方法论，并提供一个可运行的参考实现，用于90天合同续签代理。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the lack of formal architectural treatment for the boundary between stochastic LLM outputs and deterministic software systems in production agents, introducing the stochastic-deterministic boundary (SDB) as a core contract involving proposer, verifier, commit, and reject components. It organizes runtime design around three concerns—Coordination, State, and Control—and proposes six runtime patterns tailored to conversational, autonomous, and long-horizon agents, each grounded in distributed systems concepts adapted for stochastic workers. The methodology includes a five-step pattern selection process, a diagnostic tool mapping failures to pattern weaknesses, and identifies replay divergence as a failure mode where model version or prompt changes cause divergent downstream outputs. Experimental application across five workloads demonstrates that as model variance decreases, architectural choices and SDB strength become critical for long-term reliability, with a reference implementation provided for a contract-renewal agent.
本文针对生产级LLM代理中随机模型输出与确定性软件系统边界缺乏系统性架构处理的问题，提出“随机-确定性边界”（SDB）作为核心契约，包含提案者、验证者、提交与拒绝四个组件。围绕协调、状态与控制三大关注点，论文提出六种运行时模式，分别适用于对话、自主及长周期代理，每种模式均源自分布式系统概念并针对随机工作者进行调整。方法论包含五步模式选择流程、故障诊断工具（映射故障至模式弱点）及“重放发散”故障模式（模型版本或提示变化导致下游输出不一致）。实验在五个负载场景中验证，表明当模型方差降低时，架构选择与SDB强度对长期可靠性愈发关键，并提供一个合同续签代理的可运行参考实现。
Long-term Power Grid Planning via Answer Set Programming
Authors: Antonio Ielo, Francesco Doria, Sandra Castellanos-Paez, Marco Maratea, Francesco Percassi, Mauro Vallati
First: 2026-05-19T17:54:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:54:15+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Power grid is a critical infrastructure underpinning all aspects of modern society and its services. Maintaining its effectiveness requires continuous adaptations. In particular, addressing sustainability targets, demand patterns, and urbanisation trends requires implementing changes to the network. Actual developments can potentially span over a decade, with supply continuity and service quality that must be preserved throughout by ensuring conformance to several topological and combinatorial invariants. Long-term power grid planning deals with the above process, and although planning languages could be a natural choice, the kind of properties and invariants needed are cumbersome to express in such languages; on the contrary, they can be elegantly and succinctly encoded in Answer Set Programming (ASP). In this paper, we propose the first approach to automate and optimise the long-term power grid planning process using ASP. Experimental evaluations conducted on synthetic and real-world grid data confirm the expressive power of the proposed ASP-based approach and demonstrate its effectiveness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过答案集编程进行长期电网规划
电网是支撑现代社会各方面服务的关键基础设施，其有效性需要持续调整。特别是为应对可持续发展目标、需求模式和城市化趋势，需对网络进行调整。实际发展可能跨越十年，期间必须确保供电连续性和服务质量，这要求遵守若干拓扑和组合不变量。长期电网规划涉及上述过程，尽管规划语言可能是自然选择，但所需属性和不变量在这些语言中难以表达；相反，它们可优雅且简洁地编码于答案集编程（ASP）中。本文提出首个利用ASP自动化并优化长期电网规划的方法。在合成和真实电网数据上的实验评估证实了所提ASP方法的表达能力，并展示了其有效性。

Motivated by the need to adapt power grids over decades to meet sustainability, demand, and urbanization challenges while preserving service continuity and topological invariants, this paper introduces an Answer Set Programming (ASP) framework for automating long-term power grid planning. The method encodes complex combinatorial and topological constraints elegantly within ASP, enabling optimization of grid configurations over extended horizons. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world grid data validate the approach’s expressive power and demonstrate its effectiveness in generating feasible, optimized planning solutions.
为应对可持续性、需求变化和城市化趋势对电网长期适应的需求，同时确保服务连续性和拓扑不变性，本文提出一种基于答案集编程（ASP）的自动化电网长期规划框架。该方法将复杂的组合与拓扑约束优雅地编码于ASP中，支持长期电网配置的优化。在合成与真实电网数据上的实验验证了该方法的表达能力，并证明其在生成可行优化方案方面的有效性。
KoRe: Compact Knowledge Representations for Large Language Models
Authors: Davide Cavicchini, Fausto Giunchiglia, Jacopo Staiano
First: 2026-05-19T17:53:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:53:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown impressive performances in user-facing tasks such as question answering, as well as consistent improvements in reasoning capabilities. Still, the way these models encode knowledge seems inherently flawed: by design, LLMs encode world-knowledge within their parameters. This way of representing knowledge is inherently opaque, difficult to debug and update, and prone to hallucinations. On the other hand, Knowledge Graphs can provide human-readable and easily editable world knowledge representations, and their application in knowledge-intensive tasks has consistently proven beneficial to downstream performance. Nonetheless, current integration techniques require extensive retraining or finetuning. To overcome this issue, we introduce KoRe, a methodology to encode 1-hop sub-graphs into compact discrete knowledge tokens and inject them into a LLM backbone. We test the proposed approach on three established benchmarks, and report competitive performances coupled with a significant reduction (up to 10x) in token usage. Our results show that compact discrete KG representations can efficiently and effectively be used to ground modern LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KoRe：面向大语言模型的紧凑知识表示
现代大语言模型（LLM）在问答等面向用户任务中表现出色，且推理能力持续提升。然而，这些模型编码知识的方式存在固有缺陷：其知识内嵌于参数中，导致知识表示不透明、难以调试和更新，且易产生幻觉。相比之下，知识图谱可提供人类可读且易于编辑的世界知识表示，其在知识密集型任务中的应用已持续提升下游性能。但当前集成方法需大量重训练或微调。为解决此问题，我们提出KoRe方法，将1跳子图编码为紧凑离散知识令牌并注入LLM主干网络。我们在三个基准测试中验证该方法，结果表明其性能具有竞争力，同时令牌使用量显著降低（最高达10倍）。我们的结果表明，紧凑离散KG表示可高效有效地为现代LLM提供知识锚定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the opaque and hallucination-prone knowledge encoding in large language models, this work proposes KoRe, a method that embeds 1-hop knowledge graph subgraphs into compact discrete tokens and injects them into the LLM backbone without requiring retraining. Evaluated on three benchmarks, KoRe achieves competitive performance while reducing token usage by up to 10x, demonstrating that discrete knowledge representations can effectively ground LLMs with minimal computational overhead.
鉴于大语言模型中知识编码的不透明性和幻觉问题，本文提出KoRe方法，将知识图谱的一跳子图编码为紧凑离散令牌并注入LLM主干，无需重训练。在三个基准测试中，KoRe表现竞争力的同时将令牌使用量降低高达10倍，证明离散知识表示可高效有效支撑现代LLM运行，且计算开销极小。
HiDe: Rethinking The Zoom-IN method in High Resolution MLLMs via Hierarchical Decoupling
Authors: Xianjie Liu, Yiman Hu, Yixiong Zou, Liang Wu, Jian Xu, Bo Zheng
First: 2025-09-28T08:31:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:51:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have made significant strides in visual understanding tasks. However, their performance on high-resolution images remains suboptimal. While existing approaches often attribute this limitation to perceptual constraints and argue that MLLMs struggle to recognize small objects, leading them to use "zoom in" strategies for better detail, our analysis reveals a different cause: the main issue is not object size, but rather caused by complex background interference. We systematically analyze this "zoom in" operation through a series of decoupling experiments and propose the Hierarchical Decoupling Framework (HiDe), a training-free framework that uses Token-wise Attention Decoupling (TAD) to decouple the question tokens and identify the key information tokens, then leverages their attention weights to achieve precise alignment with the target visual regions. Subsequently, it employs Layout-Preserving Decoupling (LPD) to decouple these regions from the background and reconstructs a compact representation that preserves essential spatial layouts while eliminating background interference. HiDe sets a new SOTA on V*Bench, HRBench4K, and HRBench8K, boosting Qwen2.5-VL 7B and InternVL3 8B to SOTA (92.1% and 91.6% on V*Bench), even surpassing RL methods. After optimization, HiDe uses 75% less memory than the previous training-free approach. Code is provided in https://tennine2077.github.io/HiDe.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HiDe：通过分层解耦重新思考高分辨率MLLMs中的放大方法
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉理解任务中取得了显著进展，但在高分辨率图像上的表现仍不理想。现有方法常将此归因于感知限制，认为MLLMs难以识别小物体，因而采用“放大”策略以获取更多细节。我们的分析揭示了不同原因：问题并非物体大小，而是复杂背景干扰所致。我们通过一系列解耦实验系统分析“放大”操作，并提出分层解耦框架（HiDe），这是一种无需训练的框架，利用Token级注意力解耦（TAD）分离问题Token与关键信息Token，再通过其注意力权重实现与目标视觉区域的精准对齐；随后采用布局保持解耦（LPD）将这些区域从背景中分离，并重建保留关键空间布局、消除背景干扰的紧凑表示。HiDe在V*Bench、HRBench4K和HRBench8K上达到新SOTA，将Qwen2.5-VL 7B和InternVL3 8B的性能提升至SOTA（V*Bench上分别为92.1%和91.6%），甚至超越强化学习方法。优化后，HiDe的内存消耗比之前无训练方法减少75%。代码见https://tennine2077.github.io/HiDe.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that high-resolution image performance in MLLMs is hindered not by object size but by background interference, the authors propose HiDe, a training-free framework that decouples question tokens from visual tokens using Token-wise Attention Decoupling to identify key information, then applies Layout-Preserving Decoupling to isolate target regions while suppressing background noise. Experimental results show HiDe achieves state-of-the-art performance on V*Bench, HRBench4K, and HRBench8K, boosting Qwen2.5-VL 7B and InternVL3 8B to 92.1% and 91.6% accuracy respectively, even outperforming reinforcement learning methods, while using 75% less memory than prior training-free approaches.
针对现有方法将高分辨率图像表现不佳归因于小目标识别困难的误区，作者提出HiDe框架，通过Token-wise Attention Decoupling分离问题与视觉token以定位关键信息，再利用Layout-Preserving Decoupling从背景中解耦目标区域并重建保留空间布局的紧凑表示。实验表明，HiDe在V*Bench、HRBench4K和HRBench8K上达到SOTA，使Qwen2.5-VL 7B和InternVL3 8B分别提升至92.1%和91.6%准确率，超越强化学习方法，且内存消耗仅为前序训练免方法的75%。
HaorFloodAlert: Deseasonalized ML Ensemble for 72-Hour Flood Prediction in Bangladesh Haor Wetlands
Authors: Salma Hoque Talukdar Koli, Fahima Haque Talukder Jely, Md. Samiul Alim, Md. Zakir Hossen
First: 2026-05-19T17:51:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:51:46+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 9 figures. To be submitted to raaicon.org
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flash floods in Bangladesh's haor wetlands show up with almost no warning. They wreck the annual boro rice harvest. Current setups, built for riverine floods, miss backwater dynamics entirely. These basins are flat. Water does not behave like it does on the Brahmaputra. We built HaorFloodAlert, a deseasonalized machine learning ensemble that forecasts 72-hour flood probability for the Sunamganj Haor (approximately 8,000 km2). Temperature was acting as a seasonal cheat code - it inflated accuracy by 6.9 pp just because floods happen in warm months. We caught that. We also built an upstream Barak River Sentinel-1 SAR proxy from Silchar, Assam, giving about 36 hours of lead time. Otsu-thresholded SAR change detection validates at 84-91 percent spatial match. The operational ensemble (RF 0.5625 + XGBoost 0.4375) hits 89.6 percent LOOCV accuracy, 87.5 percent recall, and 0.943 AUC-ROC on 77 real Sentinel-1 events. A three-tier alert pipeline and a BRRI-calibrated boro rice damage estimator are included.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HaorFloodAlert：用于孟加拉国哈尔湿地72小时洪水预测的去季节化机器学习集成模型
孟加拉国哈尔湿地的突发性洪水几乎毫无预警，严重破坏每年的boro稻谷收成。现有系统专为河流洪水设计，完全忽略了背水区的动态。这些流域地势平坦，水流行为不同于布拉马普特拉河。我们构建了HaorFloodAlert，一个去季节化的机器学习集成模型，用于预测孙纳甘杰哈尔（约8000平方公里）72小时内的洪水概率。温度充当了季节性作弊码——仅因洪水多发生在温暖月份，就使准确率提升了6.9个百分点。我们已识别并纠正了这一问题。同时，我们还从阿萨姆邦锡尔查尔建立了上游布拉克河Sentinel-1 SAR代理，提供约36小时的预警时间。基于Otsu阈值的SAR变化检测空间匹配度验证达84-91%。该操作集成模型（随机森林0.5625 + XGBoost 0.4375）在77个真实Sentinel-1事件上达到89.6%的留一交叉验证准确率、87.5%的召回率和0.943的AUC-ROC值。系统包含三级警报管道和经BRRI校准的boro稻谷损失评估器。

CaMo: Camera Motion Grounded Evaluation and Training for Vision-Language Models
Authors: Hsiang-Wei Huang, Junbin Lu, Kuang-Ming Chen, Jianxu Shangguan, Cheng-Yen Yang, Jenq-Neng Hwang
First: 2026-05-19T17:50:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:50:25+00:00
Comments: Code and model available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/CaMo
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on spatial question answering benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether such gains reflect genuine spatial intelligence. We show that existing spatial VLMs lack basic camera motion understanding, a key component of spatial cognition. We propose the Spatial Narrative Score (SNS), an evaluation framework that requires VLMs to generate explicit spatial narratives capturing both scene semantics and camera motion, followed by reasoning with a frozen proxy LLM. Under SNS, state-of-the-art spatial VLMs exhibit significant performance degradation despite high direct question answering accuracy. To address this gap, we introduce CaMo, a camera motion grounded VLM that achieves consistent performance across SNS evaluation and direct spatial question answering accuracy. Our results highlight the importance of explicit spatial narrative externalization for evaluating VLMs with transferable 3D spatial understanding. Our code, data, and model is available at https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/CaMo
中文标题/摘要
标题：CaMo：面向视觉-语言模型的相机运动接地评估与训练
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在空间问答基准测试中表现优异，但尚不清楚这种提升是否反映了真正的空间智能。我们表明，现有空间VLMs缺乏对基本相机运动的理解，这是空间认知的关键组成部分。我们提出了空间叙事评分（SNS），一种要求VLMs生成明确空间叙事的评估框架，该叙事需同时捕捉场景语义与相机运动，并由冻结的代理大语言模型进行推理。在SNS下，尽管直接问答准确率高，当前最先进的空间VLMs表现显著下降。为解决这一差距，我们引入了CaMo——一种相机运动接地的VLM，在SNS评估和直接空间问答准确率上均保持一致表现。我们的结果强调了显式空间叙事外化对于评估具备可迁移3D空间理解能力的VLMs的重要性。代码、数据和模型可在https://github.com/hsiangwei0903/CaMo获取。

Not Every Rubric Teaches Equally: Policy-Aware Rubric Rewards for RLVR
Authors: Utkarsh Tyagi, Xingang Guo, MohammadHossein Rezaei, Daniel George, Anas Mahmoud, Jackson Lee, Bing Liu, Yunzhong He
First: 2026-05-19T17:50:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:50:18+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 7 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has made post-training highly effective when correctness can be checked automatically. However, many important model behaviors require satisfying several qualitative criteria at once. Rubric-based rewards address this setting by grading prompt-specific criteria and aggregating them into a scalar reward. Yet standard static aggregations conflate a criterion's human-assigned importance with its current usefulness as an optimization signal. We show that this assumption breaks down in rubric RL: many important criteria are already saturated or currently unreachable, while criteria that distinguish rollouts are not necessarily those with the largest human weights. We introduce POW3R, a policy-aware rubric reward framework that preserves human weights and category balance as the rubric objective while adapting criterion-level reward weights during training. POW3R uses rollout-level contrast to emphasize criteria that currently separate the policy's outputs, making the GRPO reward more informative without changing the underlying evaluation target. Across three base policies on two datasets spanning multimodal and text-only settings, POW3R wins $24$ of $30$ base-policy/metric comparisons, improving both mean rubric reward and strict completion (the fraction of prompts whose response satisfies every required rubric criterion) over vanilla GRPO with rubric rewards, and reaches the same plateau in $2.5$--$4\times$ fewer training steps. Rubric rewards should therefore distinguish what should matter in the final answer from what can teach the current policy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：并非每个评分标准都能同等教学：面向策略感知的评分标准奖励用于可验证强化学习
可验证奖励的强化学习在训练后验证正确性可自动检查时效果显著。然而，许多重要模型行为需同时满足多个定性标准。基于评分标准的奖励通过评估特定提示的多个标准并将其聚合为标量奖励来应对这一场景。但标准静态聚合混淆了标准的人类重要性与其当前作为优化信号的效用。我们证明，在评分标准强化学习中，这一假设失效：许多重要标准已饱和或当前不可达，而能区分策略输出的标准未必是人类权重最大的标准。我们提出POW3R，一种策略感知的评分标准奖励框架，它在保持评分标准目标中人类权重和类别平衡的同时，训练期间自适应调整标准级奖励权重。POW3R利用 rollout 级对比，强调当前区分策略输出的标准，使 GRPO 奖励更具信息量，而不改变底层评估目标。在两个涵盖多模态和纯文本设置的数据集上，针对三种基础策略，POW3R 在30次基础策略/指标比较中胜出24次，同时提升平均评分奖励和严格完成率（满足所有必需评分标准的提示比例），相比基础 GRPO 评分奖励，训练步数减少2.5至4倍。因此，评分奖励应区分最终答案中哪些内容应被重视，哪些内容能有效指导当前策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of static rubric aggregation in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where human-assigned criterion weights often misalign with current optimization needs, the paper introduces POW3R, a policy-aware rubric reward framework. POW3R preserves human-specified criterion weights and category balance while dynamically adjusting criterion-level reward weights based on rollout-level contrast to highlight currently discriminative criteria. Experiments across three base policies on two datasets show POW3R outperforms vanilla GRPO with rubric rewards in 24 of 30 comparisons, improving both mean rubric reward and strict completion rate, and reaching performance plateaus 2.5–4 times faster.
针对强化学习中静态评分标准聚合无法区分当前优化信号与人类权重的问题，作者提出POW3R框架，该框架在保留人类指定评分项权重和类别平衡的前提下，通过 rollout 级对比动态调整评分项权重，以突出当前区分策略输出的评分项。实验表明，在两个数据集上的三组基线策略中，POW3R在30次比较中有24次胜出，同时提升平均评分奖励和严格完成率，并在2.5至4倍更少的训练步数内达到性能平台期。
Learned Static Function Data Structures
Authors: Stefan Hermann, Hans-Peter Lehmann, Giorgio Vinciguerra, Stefan Walzer
Venue: PVLDB, 19(5): 917-930, 2026
First: 2025-10-31T16:09:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:47:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the task of constructing a data structure for associating a static set of keys with values, while allowing arbitrary output values for queries involving keys outside the set. Compared to hash tables, these so-called static function data structures do not need to store the key set and thus use significantly less memory. Several techniques are known, with compressed static functions approaching the zero-order empirical entropy of the value sequence. In this paper, we introduce learned static functions, which use machine learning to capture correlations between keys and values. For each key, a model predicts a probability distribution over the values, from which we derive a key-specific prefix code to compactly encode the true value. The resulting codeword is stored in a classic static function data structure. This design allows learned static functions to break the zero-order entropy barrier while still supporting point queries. Our experiments show substantial space savings: up to one order of magnitude on real data, and up to three orders of magnitude on synthetic data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习型静态函数数据结构
我们研究构建一种数据结构，用于将静态键集与值关联起来，同时允许对集合外的键进行任意输出值的查询。与哈希表相比，这类所谓的静态函数数据结构无需存储键集，因此内存使用显著减少。目前已知多种技术，压缩静态函数可接近值序列的零阶经验熵。本文引入学习型静态函数，利用机器学习捕捉键与值之间的相关性。对每个键，模型预测值的概率分布，从而推导出针对该键的前缀码，以紧凑方式编码真实值。生成的码字存储于经典静态函数数据结构中。该设计使学习型静态函数突破零阶熵限制，同时仍支持点查询。实验表明，其空间节省显著：在真实数据上可达一个数量级，在合成数据上可达三个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce memory usage in static key-value mappings while supporting arbitrary query outputs, this paper proposes learned static functions that leverage machine learning to model key-value correlations. For each key, a learned model predicts a probability distribution over possible values, enabling the construction of a key-specific prefix code that compactly encodes the actual value. This codeword is then stored in a traditional static function structure, allowing efficient point queries while surpassing the zero-order entropy limit. Experiments demonstrate significant space savings—up to one order of magnitude on real datasets and up to three orders of magnitude on synthetic data—compared to compressed static functions.
为降低静态函数数据结构的内存开销并支持任意键值查询，本文提出一种基于机器学习的静态函数方法。对每个键，模型预测其对应值的概率分布，从而生成键特异性的前缀编码以紧凑存储真实值，再存入传统静态函数结构中，支持高效点查询并突破零阶熵限制。实验表明，该方法在真实数据上节省空间达一个数量级，在合成数据上可达三个数量级，显著优于现有压缩静态函数方法。
Interpretable Computer Vision for Defect Detection in X-ray Tomography of Aerospace SiC/SiC Composites
Authors: Antonio Peña Corredor, Julien Lesseur, Romain Nunez, Paul Rivalland, Thomas Philippe
First: 2026-05-19T17:46:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:46:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Non-destructive testing of aerospace SiC/SiC composites via X-ray computed tomography (XCT) relies on expert visual assessment, with current workflows offering limited traceability for accept/reject decisions. Deep convolutional networks can automate defect detection, yet their black-box nature conflicts with the transparency that industrial inspection practice demands. To close this gap, we introduce p-ResNet-50, a convolutional framework extended with a prototype layer that couples high detection accuracy with case-based explanations. Six learned prototypes are explicitly aligned with expert-defined semantic categories-healthy matrix, matrix--air interfaces, pores, line-like defects, and mixed morphologies-so that every classification is traceable to a physically meaningful reference. Two novel regularisation terms, anchor-based and medoid-based, tether prototypes to expert-selected patches and prevent prototype collapse, addressing a known limitation of prototype networks. Latent-space analysis via UMAP delineates semantically coherent sub-domains and maps zones of uncertainty where misclassifications concentrate, giving inspectors an explicit picture of where the model is-and is not-reliable. The framework is validated on an XCT patch dataset of approximately 12,000 patches extracted from four defect-rich SiC/SiC laboratory specimens. Taking a black-box ResNet-50 as a baseline (ROC-AUC = 0.991), the prototype extension achieves comparable performance (accuracy 0.957 vs. 0.959; ROC-AUC 0.994 vs. 0.993) while trading a slight reduction in sensitivity for higher precision and specificity. Each decision is backed by representative evidence patches, and the model explicitly flags its uncertainty regions. Beyond defect mapping, the framework establishes a reusable methodology for embedding domain-expert knowledge into prototype networks, applicable to other XCT inspection scenarios requiring traceable, auditable decisions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于航空航天SiC/SiC复合材料X射线断层扫描缺陷检测的可解释计算机视觉
航空航天SiC/SiC复合材料的无损检测依赖X射线计算机断层扫描（XCT）和专家视觉评估，当前工作流程难以追溯接受/拒收决策。深度卷积网络可自动化缺陷检测，但其黑箱特性与工业检测所需的透明性相冲突。为弥合此差距，我们引入p-ResNet-50，一种扩展了原型层的卷积框架，兼具高检测精度与基于案例的解释能力。六个学习到的原型明确与专家定义的语义类别对齐——健康基体、基体-空气界面、孔隙、线状缺陷及混合形态，使每个分类均可追溯至物理意义明确的参考。两种新颖正则化项——基于锚点和基于中位点——将原型与专家选定的图像块绑定，防止原型坍缩，解决了原型网络的已知局限。通过UMAP进行潜在空间分析，可划分语义连贯的子域，并映射误分类集中区域，为检测人员提供模型可靠与不可靠区域的明确图示。该框架在约12,000个从四个富含缺陷的SiC/SiC实验室样品中提取的XCT图像块数据集上得到验证。以黑箱ResNet-50为基线（ROC-AUC=0.991），原型扩展版本性能相当（准确率0.957 vs. 0.959；ROC-AUC 0.994 vs. 0.993），以略低的敏感性换取更高的特异性和精确度。每个决策均附有代表性证据图像块，模型明确标示其不确定性区域。除缺陷映射外，该框架建立了一种可复用的方法，将领域专家知识嵌入原型网络，适用于其他需可追溯、可审计决策的XCT检测场景。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for transparent, auditable defect detection in aerospace SiC/SiC composites inspected via X-ray tomography, this work introduces p-ResNet-50, a modified ResNet-50 with a prototype layer that links classification decisions to physically meaningful defect categories. The method employs two novel regularization terms to stabilize prototype learning and align them with expert-selected patches, preventing collapse and enhancing interpretability. Evaluated on 12,000 XCT patches from four specimens, the model achieves performance nearly matching a black-box baseline (accuracy 0.957 vs. 0.959; ROC-AUC 0.994 vs. 0.993) while offering higher precision and specificity, and explicitly provides evidence patches and uncertainty maps for each decision, enabling traceable, expert-aligned inspections.
为解决航空航天SiC/SiC复合材料X射线断层扫描缺陷检测中缺乏可追溯性的问题，该研究提出p-ResNet-50模型，通过原型层将分类结果与专家定义的物理缺陷类别（如健康基体、气孔、线状缺陷等）直接关联。该方法引入两种新正则化项——基于锚点和中位原型的约束，防止原型坍缩并提升可解释性。在12,000个来自四块缺陷样本的XCT图像块上测试，模型准确率（0.957）与ROC-AUC（0.994）接近黑盒ResNet-50基线（0.959, 0.993），同时提升精确率与特异率，并通过UMAP可视化不确定性区域，为每项决策提供证据图像，实现可审计的检测过程。
Rethinking Visual Attribution for Chest X-ray Reasoning in Large Vision Language Models
Authors: Guangzhi Xiong, Qiao Jin, Sanchit Sinha, Zhiyong Lu, Aidong Zhang
First: 2026-05-19T17:46:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:46:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs) show promise in medical applications, but their inability to faithfully ground responses in visual evidence raises serious concerns about clinical trustworthiness. While visual attribution methods are widely used to explain LVLM predictions, whether these explanations actually reflect the visual evidence underlying the model's decision is largely unverified, since ground-truth annotations for internal model reasoning are typically unavailable. We address this question for chest X-ray (CXR) reasoning by developing a causal evaluation framework that retains only CXR-VQA samples for which the expert-annotated region is verified, via counterfactual editing, to be causally responsible for the model's prediction. Using this framework across 11 attribution methods, six open-source LVLMs, and two output modes (direct answer and step-by-step reasoning), we find that existing attribution methods often fail to identify the evidence used by LVLMs. To address this failure, we propose MedFocus, a concept-based attribution method that localizes clinically meaningful anatomical regions via unbalanced optimal transport and measures their causal effect on model outputs through targeted interventions. MedFocus produces spatial, concept-level, and token-level attributions and substantially outperforms prior methods, taking a step toward more trustworthy attribution for medical LVLMs. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/gzxiong/medfocus/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重新思考大型视觉语言模型中胸部X光图像推理的视觉归因
大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）在医学应用中展现出潜力，但其无法准确将响应与视觉证据关联，引发了对临床可信度的严重担忧。尽管视觉归因方法广泛用于解释LVLM预测，但这些解释是否真实反映模型决策背后的视觉证据，尚缺乏验证，因为模型内部推理的真值标注通常不可得。我们针对胸部X光（CXR）推理问题，开发了一种因果评估框架，仅保留经反事实编辑验证专家标注区域对模型预测具有因果责任的CXR-VQA样本。在11种归因方法、6个开源LVLMs及两种输出模式（直接回答与逐步推理）上应用该框架，发现现有归因方法常无法识别LVLMs所使用的证据。为解决此问题，我们提出MedFocus，一种基于概念的归因方法，通过不平衡最优传输定位具有临床意义的解剖区域，并通过针对性干预测量其对模型输出的因果效应。MedFocus生成空间、概念级和词元级归因，在性能上显著优于现有方法，为医学LVLMs的可信归因迈出一步。我们的数据与代码可在https://github.com/gzxiong/medfocus/获取。

MedFocus delivers spatial, concept-level, and token-level attributions and significantly outperforms existing approaches, advancing trustworthy medical LVLM explanations.
鉴于现有视觉归因方法在胸片推理中难以验证其与真实视觉证据的一致性，该研究提出一种因果评估框架，通过反事实编辑专家标注区域来验证其对模型预测的因果贡献。在11种归因方法、6个开源LVLM及两种输出模式下测试发现，多数现有方法未能准确捕捉模型实际依赖的视觉证据。为此，作者提出MedFocus方法，利用不平衡最优传输定位临床意义显著的解剖区域，并通过针对性干预评估其对模型输出的因果影响，在空间、概念与词元层面均显著优于现有方法。
SAGE: Scalable Automatic Gating Ensemble for Confident Negative Harvesting in Fraud Detection
Authors: Sudheer Tubati, Amit Goyal
Venue: WSDM
First: 2026-05-19T17:46:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:46:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Music streaming fraud, where bad actors artificially inflate stream counts to manipulate chart rankings and royalty payments, poses a significant threat to streaming services and legitimate content creators. Traditional fraud detection approaches struggle with a critical challenge: many legitimate edge cases, including super-fans and sleep-music sessions, exhibit activity patterns that closely mimic those of coordinated fraud. We present SAGE, a novel counterfactual-aware negative harvesting approach that combines SimHash-based stratified sampling with a modular gating ensemble for confident negative identification from unlabeled data. Our ensemble architecture employs pluggable statistical gates (currently instantiated with Mahalanobis distance and k-NN density) with configurable voting thresholds enabling adaptive precision-recall trade-offs. This addresses the representation bias problem in Positive-Unlabeled learning by ensuring comprehensive coverage of rare behavioral cohorts through floor-constrained sampling. Evaluation demonstrates strong precision and recall on held-out data. The approach generalizes across fraud detection domains, achieving strong performance on both customer-level and artist-level fraud without modification to the core methodology.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAGE：用于欺诈检测中自信负样本采集的可扩展自动门控集成方法
音乐流媒体欺诈行为，即恶意用户人为抬高播放次数以操纵排行榜和版税支付，对流媒体服务及合法内容创作者构成重大威胁。传统欺诈检测方法面临关键挑战：许多合法边缘案例（如超级粉丝和睡眠音乐播放）的行为模式与有组织欺诈高度相似。我们提出SAGE，一种新颖的反事实感知负样本采集方法，结合基于SimHash的分层抽样与模块化门控集成，从无标签数据中自信识别负样本。该集成架构采用可插拔统计门控（当前实例化为马氏距离和k-NN密度），并支持可配置投票阈值，实现自适应的精确率-召回率权衡。该方法通过下限约束抽样，解决了正负无标签学习中的表示偏差问题，确保对罕见行为群体的全面覆盖。评估表明在保留数据上具有高精确率和召回率。该方法可泛化至不同欺诈检测领域，在客户级和艺人级欺诈检测中均无需修改核心方法即可取得优异性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting music streaming fraud while avoiding false negatives from legitimate users like super-fans, the paper introduces SAGE, a counterfactual-aware negative harvesting method that uses SimHash stratified sampling and a modular gating ensemble to identify confident negatives from unlabeled data. 为解决音乐流媒体欺诈检测中误判合法用户（如超级粉丝）的问题，SAGE提出一种基于反事实感知的负样本采样方法，结合SimHash分层采样与模块化门控集成。该集成采用可插拔统计门（如马氏距离和k-NN密度）及可配置投票阈值，实现精度与召回率的自适应平衡。实验表明，该方法在客户级与艺人级欺诈检测中均表现优异，无需调整核心架构，有效缓解了正负样本不平衡学习中的表征偏差问题。
LongLive-2.0: An NVFP4 Parallel Infrastructure for Long Video Generation
Authors: Yukang Chen, Luozhou Wang, Wei Huang, Shuai Yang, Bohan Zhang, Yicheng Xiao, Ruihang Chu, Weian Mao, Qixin Hu, Shaoteng Liu, Yuyang Zhao, Huizi Mao, Ying-Cong Chen, Enze Xie, Xiaojuan Qi, Song Han
First: 2026-05-18T17:57:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:46:12+00:00
Comments: Code, model, and demos are available at https://github.com/NVlabs/LongLive
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present LongLive-2.0, an NVFP4-based parallel infrastructure throughout the full training and inference workflow of long video generation, addressing speed and memory bottlenecks. For training, we introduce sequence-parallel autoregressive (AR) training, instantiated as Balanced SP, which co-designs the efficient teacher-forcing layout with SP execution by pairing clean-history and noisy-target temporal chunks on each rank, enabling a natural teacher-forcing mask with SP-aware chunked VAE encoding. Combined with NVFP4 precision, it reduces GPU memory cost and accelerates GEMM computation during training, the proportion of which increases as video length grows. Moreover, we show that a high-quality infrastructure and dataset enable a remarkably clean training pipeline. Unlike existing Self-Forcing series methods that rely on ODE initialization and subsequent distribution matching distillation (DMD), LongLive-2.0 directly tunes a diffusion model into a long, multi-shot, interactive auto-regressive (AR) diffusion model. It can be further converted to real-time generation (4 to 2 denoising steps) with standalone LoRA weights. For inference on Blackwell GPUs, we enable W4A4 NVFP4 inference, quantize KV cache into NVFP4 for memory savings, and boost end-to-end throughput with asynchronous streaming VAE decoding. On non-Blackwell GPU architectures, we deploy SP inference to match the speed on Blackwell GPUs, while the quantized KV cache can lower inter-GPU communication of SP. Experiments show up to 2.15x speedup in training, and 1.84x in inference. LongLive-2.0-5B achieves 45.7 FPS inference while attaining strong performance on benchmarks. To our knowledge, LongLive-2.0 is the first NVFP4 training and inference system for long video generation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to overcome speed and memory bottlenecks in long video generation, LongLive-2.0 introduces an NVFP4-based parallel infrastructure covering both training and inference. Its training method, Balanced SP, co-designs teacher-forcing layouts with sequence-parallel execution by pairing clean-history and noisy-target chunks per GPU rank, enabling efficient VAE encoding and reducing memory usage while accelerating GEMM operations—especially as video length increases. The system directly tunes a diffusion model into a multi-shot, interactive autoregressive diffusion model without relying on ODE initialization or distillation, and supports real-time generation via LoRA weights. For inference, it leverages W4A4 NVFP4 on Blackwell GPUs, quantizes KV cache, and uses asynchronous streaming VAE decoding to boost throughput; on non-Blackwell GPUs, SP inference and quantized KV cache reduce inter-GPU communication. Experiments show 2.15x training speedup and 1.84x inference speedup, with LongLive-2.0-5B achieving 45.7 FPS while maintaining strong benchmark performance, marking the first NVFP4 system for long video generation.
为解决长视频生成中的速度与内存瓶颈，LongLive-2.0 提出了一套覆盖训练与推理全流程的 NVFP4 并行基础设施。其训练方法 Balanced SP 通过在每个 GPU 上配对干净历史与噪声目标时间片段，协同设计教师强制布局与序列并行执行，实现高效 VAE 编码并降低显存占用，同时加速 GEMM 计算——视频越长，GEMM 加速比例越高。该系统直接将扩散模型调优为多段交互式自回归扩散模型，无需依赖 ODE 初始化或分布匹配蒸馏。推理阶段在 Blackwell GPU 上支持 W4A4 NVFP4，KV 缓存量化为 NVFP4 以节省显存，并通过异步流式 VAE 解码提升端到端吞吐；在非 Blackwell 架构上采用 SP 推理配合量化 KV 缓存以降低 GPU 间通信。实验显示训练速度提升 2.15 倍，推理速度提升 1.84 倍，LongLive-2.0-5B 达到 45.7 FPS 并保持强基准性能，是首个面向长视频生成的完整 NVFP4 训练与推理系统。
Addressing prior dependence in hierarchical Bayesian modeling for PTA data analysis II: Noise and SGWB inference through parameter decorrelation
Authors: Eleonora Villa, Luigi D'Amico, Aldo Barca, Fatima Modica Bittordo, Francesco Alì, Massimo Meneghetti, Luca Naso
First: 2025-11-03T17:54:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:43:22+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 5 figures. Extended analysis and appendix added. Submitted to the Astronomy and Computing special issue HPC in Cosmology and Astrophysics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pulsar Timing Arrays (PTA) provide a powerful framework to measure low-frequency gravitational waves, but accuracy and robustness of the results are challenged by complex noise processes that must be accurately modeled. Standard PTA analyses assign fixed uniform noise priors to each pulsar, an approach that can introduce systematic biases when combining the array. To overcome this limitation, we adopt a hierarchical Bayesian modeling strategy in which noise priors are parametrized by higher-level hyperparameters. To mitigate the sensitivity of the inferred parameters to the choice of noise hyperprior, we introduce a reparametrization of the hierarchical model based on the orthogonal projection of hyperparameters onto the physical parameter subspace. The transformation is implemented through Normalizing Flows (NFs), which provide an invertible, tractable representation and preserve shrinkage and inter-pulsar information pooling in the reparametrized model. We also employ i-nessai, a flow-guided nested sampler, to efficiently explore the resulting higher-dimensional parameter space. We apply our method to a minimal 3-pulsar case study, performing a simultaneous inference of noise and stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) parameters. Despite the limited dataset, the results consistently show that the reparametrized hierarchical treatment constrains the noise parameters more tightly and partially alleviates the red-noise-SGWB degeneracy, while the orthogonal reparametrization further enhances parameter independence without affecting the correlations intrinsic to the power-law modeling of the physical processes involved.
中文标题/摘要
标题：脉冲星定时阵列数据的分层贝叶斯建模中先验依赖性的处理 II：通过参数解相关实现噪声与随机引力波背景推断
脉冲星定时阵列（PTA）为测量低频引力波提供了强大框架，但其结果的准确性和稳健性受复杂噪声过程挑战，必须精确建模。标准PTA分析为每个脉冲星分配固定均匀噪声先验，该方法在组合阵列时可能引入系统性偏差。为克服此局限，我们采用分层贝叶斯建模策略，将噪声先验参数化为更高层超参数。为降低推断参数对噪声超先验选择的敏感性，我们基于超参数在物理参数子空间上的正交投影，对分层模型进行重参数化。该变换通过归一化流（NFs）实现，提供可逆、可计算的表示，并在重参数化模型中保留收缩性和脉冲星间信息聚合。我们还采用i-nessai（基于流引导的嵌套采样器）高效探索所得高维参数空间。我们以最小3脉冲星案例应用该方法，同时推断噪声与随机引力波背景（SGWB）参数。尽管数据量有限，结果一致表明，重参数化分层处理更严格约束噪声参数，并部分缓解红噪声-SGWB退化，而正交重参数化进一步增强参数独立性，不影响物理过程幂律建模固有的相关性。
Summary / 总结
Pulsar Timing Arrays (PTA) provide a powerful framework to measure low-frequency gravitational waves, but accuracy and robustness of the results are challenged by complex noise processes that must be accurately modeled.
为解决传统PTA分析中固定均匀噪声先验导致的系统偏差，本文提出一种层次贝叶斯模型，将噪声先验参数化为高阶超参数，并通过正交投影至物理参数子空间，结合归一化流（Normalizing Flows）进行重参数化以降低对超先验选择的敏感性。采用i-nessai流引导嵌套采样器高效探索高维参数空间。在最小3脉冲星案例中，该方法显著收紧了噪声参数约束，部分缓解了红噪声与随机引力波背景（SGWB）的退化问题，同时保留了物理过程幂律建模所固有的相关性。
LLM-MC-Affect: LLM-Based Monte Carlo Modeling of Affective Trajectories and Latent Ambiguity for Interpersonal Dynamic Insight
Authors: Yu-Zheng Lin, Bono Po-Jen Shih, John Paul Martin Encinas, Elizabeth Victoria Abraham Achom, Karan Himanshu Patel, Jesus Horacio Pacheco, Sicong Shao, Jyotikrishna Dass, Soheil Salehi, Pratik Satam
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-07T06:50:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:43:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 64th Annual Meeting of the Association for Computational Linguistics (ACL 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Emotional coordination is a core property of human interaction that shapes how relational meaning is constructed in real time. While text-based affect inference has become increasingly feasible, prior approaches often treat sentiment as a deterministic point estimate for individual speakers, failing to capture the inherent subjectivity, latent ambiguity, and sequential coupling found in mutual exchanges. We introduce LLM-MC-Affect, a probabilistic framework that characterizes emotion not as a static label, but as a continuous latent probability distribution defined over an affective space. By leveraging stochastic LLM decoding and Monte Carlo estimation, the methodology approximates these distributions to derive high-fidelity sentiment trajectories that explicitly quantify both central affective tendencies and perceptual ambiguity. These trajectories enable a structured analysis of interpersonal coupling through sequential cross-correlation and slope-based indicators, identifying leading or lagging influences between interlocutors. To validate the interpretive capacity of this approach, we utilize teacher-student instructional dialogues as a representative case study, where our quantitative indicators successfully distill high-level interaction insights such as effective scaffolding. This work establishes a scalable and deployable pathway for understanding interpersonal dynamics, offering a generalizable solution that extends beyond education to broader social and behavioral research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LLM-MC-Affect：基于大语言模型的蒙特卡洛建模，用于情感轨迹与潜在模糊性的建模，以洞察人际动态
情感协调是人类互动的核心属性，塑造了关系意义在实时中的构建方式。尽管基于文本的情感推断已变得越来越可行，但以往方法常将情感视为个体发言者确定性的点估计，未能捕捉相互交流中固有的主观性、潜在模糊性及序列耦合。我们提出LLM-MC-Affect，一种概率框架，将情感视为情感空间上的连续潜在概率分布，而非静态标签。通过利用随机LLM解码与蒙特卡洛估计，该方法近似这些分布，从而推导出高保真情感轨迹，明确量化中心情感倾向与感知模糊性。这些轨迹通过序列互相关与斜率指标，实现人际耦合的结构化分析，识别对话者间的领先或滞后影响。为验证该方法的解释能力，我们以师生教学对话作为代表性案例研究，其量化指标成功提炼出如有效支架等高层次互动洞见。本研究建立了一条可扩展、可部署的路径，用于理解人际动态，提供超越教育领域、适用于更广泛社会与行为研究的通用解决方案。

This enables the derivation of high-fidelity sentiment trajectories that capture both central affective tendencies and perceptual ambiguity. Applied to teacher-student dialogues, the method successfully identifies interpersonal coupling patterns and quantifies interaction dynamics such as scaffolding, demonstrating its utility for scalable analysis of interpersonal dynamics across domains.
为突破传统情感分析中将情绪视为确定性标签的局限，LLM-MC-Affect提出将情绪建模为情感空间上的连续概率分布。通过结合随机LLM解码与蒙特卡洛估计，该方法生成高保真情感轨迹，同时量化核心情感倾向与感知模糊性。在教师-学生对话数据上，该方法通过序列交叉相关与斜率指标有效识别如支架式教学等互动模式，验证了其在多领域人际关系分析中的可扩展性与普适性。
When Does Model Collapse Occur in Structured Interactive Learning?
Authors: Yuchen Wu, Kangjie Zhou, Weijie Su
First: 2026-05-19T17:41:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:41:09+00:00
Comments: 57 pages, 12 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has given rise to an interactive learning environment, where model parameters are continuously updated using not only data generated by natural processes, but also synthetic outputs produced by other models. This paradigm introduces two major challenges: (1) training data are no longer drawn exclusively from the target population, undermining a core assumption of classical statistical learning, and (2) model training processes become inherently correlated, as models interact with one another through repeated exposure to each other's synthetic outputs in a potentially complex manner. Establishing reliable statistical inference in such structured interactive learning environments therefore remains an important open problem. In particular, there is growing concern about model collapse, a phenomenon in which the performance of generative models progressively degrades as they are trained on synthetic data produced by earlier model generations. Prior work on model collapse primarily focuses on a single model trained on its own output, failing to capture model performance in multi-model interactive settings. In this work, we fill this gap by investigating the performance of generative models in an interactive learning environment with general interaction patterns. In particular, we formalize model interactions using directed graphs and show that the occurrence of model collapse depends critically on the topology of the interaction graph. We further derive an explicit necessary and sufficient condition characterizing when model collapse occurs, and establish finite-sample results for linear regression and asymptotic guarantees for general M-estimators. We support our theoretical findings through extensive numerical experiments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结构化交互学习中模型崩溃何时发生？
生成式人工智能的普及催生了交互式学习环境，其中模型参数不仅通过自然过程生成的数据更新，还通过其他模型产生的合成输出更新。这一范式带来两大挑战：（1）训练数据不再仅来自目标群体，破坏了经典统计学习的核心假设；（2）模型训练过程因相互交互而内在相关，模型通过反复接触彼此的合成输出以复杂方式互动。因此，在此类结构化交互学习环境中建立可靠的统计推断仍是一个重要开放问题。尤其令人担忧的是模型崩溃现象——即生成模型在训练由早期模型生成的合成数据时，其性能逐步退化。 以往关于模型崩溃的研究主要聚焦于单模型训练其自身输出，未能涵盖多模型交互场景中的模型表现。本文填补这一空白，通过研究具有通用交互模式的交互式学习环境中生成模型的表现，我们使用有向图形式化模型交互，并证明模型崩溃的发生高度依赖于交互图的拓扑结构。我们进一步推导出刻画模型崩溃发生条件的显式充要条件，并为线性回归建立有限样本结果，为一般M估计量建立渐近保证。我们通过大量数值实验支持理论发现。
