The authors model interactions between multiple models using directed graphs and derive a necessary and sufficient condition for collapse based on graph topology. They establish theoretical guarantees for linear regression and general M-estimators, and validate their findings through extensive numerical experiments showing collapse occurs predictably under specific interaction structures.
鉴于生成式AI系统中模型间相互训练合成数据的现象日益普遍，本文研究了模型崩溃问题——即因训练数据质量持续下降而导致模型性能退化。作者通过有向图形式化建模多模型交互，并推导出基于图拓扑结构的崩溃发生充要条件。研究对线性回归和一般M估计量建立了有限样本理论保证与渐近性质，并通过大量数值实验验证：在特定交互结构下，模型崩溃可被精确预测。
TideGS: Scalable Training of Over One Billion 3D Gaussian Splatting Primitives via Out-of-Core Optimization
Authors: Chonghao Zhong, Linfeng Shi, Hua Chen, Tiecheng Sun, Hao Zhao, Binhang Yuan, Chaojian Li
Venue: ICML 2026 Spotlight
First: 2026-05-19T17:40:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:40:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026 as Spotlight. Website: https://sponge-lab.github.io/TideGS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Training 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) at billion-primitive scale is fundamentally memory-bound: each Gaussian primitive carries a large attribute vector, and the aggregate parameter table quickly exceeds GPU capacity, limiting prior systems to tens of millions of Gaussians on commodity single-GPU hardware. We observe that 3DGS training is inherently sparse and trajectory-conditioned: each iteration activates only the Gaussians visible from the current camera batch, so GPU memory can serve as a working-set cache rather than a persistent parameter store. Building on this insight, we introduce TideGS, an out-of-core training framework that manages parameters across an SSD-CPU-GPU hierarchy via three synergistic techniques: block-virtualized geometry for SSD-aligned spatial locality, a hierarchical asynchronous pipeline to overlap I/O with computation, and trajectory-adaptive differential streaming that transfers only incremental working-set deltas between iterations. Experiments show that TideGS enables training with over one billion Gaussians on a single 24 GB GPU while achieving the best reconstruction quality among evaluated single-GPU baselines on large-scale scenes, scaling beyond prior out-of-core baselines (e.g., approximately 100M Gaussians) and standard in-memory training (e.g., approximately 11M Gaussians).
中文标题/摘要
标题：TideGS：通过外存优化实现十亿级3D高斯点云素的可扩展训练
在十亿级素训练3D高斯点云（3DGS）本质上受内存限制：每个高斯素携带一个大型属性向量，参数表总和迅速超出GPU容量，限制了先前系统仅能在消费级单GPU硬件上训练数千万个高斯素。我们观察到3DGS训练具有固有的稀疏性和轨迹依赖性：每次迭代仅激活当前相机批次可见的高斯素，因此GPU内存可作为工作集缓存而非持久参数存储。基于此洞察，我们提出TideGS，一种外存训练框架，通过三种协同技术在SSD-CPU-GPU层次管理参数：块虚拟化几何结构以实现SSD对齐的空间局部性、分层异步流水线以重叠I/O与计算、轨迹自适应微分流以仅在迭代间传输工作集增量差异。实验表明，TideGS可在单块24GB GPU上训练超过十亿个高斯素，且在大规模场景上重建质量优于所有评估的单GPU基线方法，超越先前外存基线（如约1亿个高斯素）和标准内存内训练（如约1100万个高斯素）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the memory limitations that prevent training 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) at billion-scale on consumer GPUs, the authors propose TideGS, an out-of-core framework that leverages the sparse, camera-trajectory-driven nature of 3DGS training. By treating GPU memory as a working-set cache and offloading unused parameters to SSD via block-virtualized geometry, hierarchical asynchronous I/O, and trajectory-adaptive differential streaming, TideGS enables efficient parameter management across CPU-GPU-SSD tiers. Experimental results demonstrate that TideGS successfully trains over one billion Gaussians on a single 24GB GPU, outperforming prior in-memory and out-of-core methods in both scalability and reconstruction quality on large-scale scenes.
为突破单GPU硬件在训练亿级3D高斯点云时的显存瓶颈，作者提出TideGS框架，将GPU显存作为工作集缓存而非持久化参数存储，利用3DGS训练固有的稀疏性和相机轨迹依赖特性——每轮仅激活当前相机批次可见的高斯点。该框架通过SSD对齐的块虚拟化几何结构、分层异步流水线实现I/O与计算重叠、以及轨迹自适应差分流传输仅增量工作集变化。实验表明，TideGS可在单张24GB显卡上训练超十亿高斯点，重建质量优于现有单GPU基线，且显著超越传统内存内训练（约1100万点）和先前的外存方法（约1亿点）
Less Back-and-Forth: A Comparative Study of Structured Prompting
Authors: Saurav Ghosh, Gabriella Polach, Abdou Sow
First: 2026-05-19T17:40:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:40:14+00:00
Comments: 7 pages, 2 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for open-ended tasks, but underspecified prompts can lead to low-quality answers and additional interaction. This paper studies whether structured prompt design improves response quality while reducing user effort. We compare three prompt conditions: a raw prompt, a checklist-improved prompt, and a clarifying-question prompt. We evaluate these conditions across four task types--summarization, planning, explanation, and coding--using three LLM systems: ChatGPT, Claude, and Grok. Each output is scored with a unified rubric covering task completion, correctness, compliance, and clarity. Checklist-improved prompts achieved the highest mean rubric score, 7.50 out of 8, compared with 5.67 for raw prompts and 6.67 for clarifying-question prompts. Checklist prompts also produced the best quality-effort tradeoff, using fewer average tokens than both raw and clarifying prompts. These results suggest that a simple prompt checklist can improve LLM responses while reducing unnecessary interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：减少来回沟通：结构化提示的比较研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）广泛用于开放式任务，但未明确的提示可能导致低质量回答和额外交互。本文研究结构化提示设计是否能提升回答质量并减少用户努力。我们比较了三种提示条件：原始提示、清单优化提示和澄清问题提示。在四个任务类型——摘要、规划、解释和编码——中，使用三个LLM系统（ChatGPT、Claude和Grok）进行评估。每个输出使用统一评分标准评估任务完成度、正确性、合规性和清晰度。清单优化提示获得最高平均评分7.50（满分8），优于原始提示的5.67和澄清问题提示的6.67。清单提示还实现了最佳的质量-努力权衡，平均使用的token数少于原始和澄清问题提示。结果表明，简单的提示清单可提升LLM响应质量并减少不必要的交互。

DeltaPrompts: Escaping the Zero-Delta Trap in Multimodal Distillation
Authors: Jaehun Jung, Hyunwoo Kim, Brandon Cui, Ximing Lu, David Acuna, Prithviraj Ammanabrolu, Yejin Choi
First: 2026-05-15T02:04:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:39:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distillation enables compact Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to obtain strong reasoning capabilities, yet the prompts driving this process are typically chosen via simple heuristics or aggregated from off-the-shelf datasets. We reveal a critical inefficiency in this approach: up to 69% of the prompts in standard chart / document reasoning datasets are effectively zero-delta, meaning the teacher and student already induce the exact same answer distribution. Training on these prompts provides minimal learning signal, causing student improvement to rapidly saturate regardless of data scale. To escape the zero-delta trap, we return to first principles: distillation fundamentally minimizes distributional divergence, and thus a prompt is valuable only if it exposes a functional capability gap between the teacher and student. We quantify this gap through answer divergence ($Δ$), demonstrating that non-zero divergence is critical for effective scaling. Building on this insight, we propose a staged synthesis pipeline that repurposes existing datasets as seeds, actively targeting student failure modes to produce better prompts. The result is DeltaPrompts, a diverse dataset of 200k synthetic, high-divergence reasoning problems. We evaluate DeltaPrompts across three distinct settings: on-policy distillation with the target teacher-student pair, transfer to a novel model family without regenerating the data, and off-policy fine-tuning of a non-reasoning model. Across all scenarios, DeltaPrompts drives substantial gains, yielding up to 15% relative improvement even on top of a highly-optimized reasoning model (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking) -- averaged over 10 benchmarks spanning chart, document and perception-centric reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeltaPrompts：摆脱多模态蒸馏中的零差值陷阱
蒸馏使紧凑型视觉-语言模型（VLMs）获得强大的推理能力，但驱动该过程的提示通常通过简单启发式或从现成数据集中聚合而来。我们揭示了该方法的关键低效性：标准图表/文档推理数据集中高达69%的提示实质上为零差值，即教师与学生已诱导出完全相同的答案分布。在这些提示上训练提供的学习信号极小，导致学生性能无论数据规模如何均迅速饱和。为摆脱零差值陷阱，我们回归基本原理：蒸馏本质上最小化分布差异，因此只有当提示能暴露教师与学生之间功能能力差距时，该提示才具价值。我们通过答案差异（Δ）量化该差距，证明非零差异对有效扩展至关重要。基于此洞察，我们提出分阶段合成流程，将现有数据集作为种子，主动针对学生失败模式生成更优提示。结果是DeltaPrompts，一个包含20万条合成、高差异推理问题的多样化数据集。我们在三种不同场景下评估DeltaPrompts：目标教师-学生对的在线蒸馏、无需再生数据的新型模型家族迁移、非推理模型的离线微调。在所有场景中，DeltaPrompts均带来显著提升，即使在高度优化的推理模型（如Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking）之上，平均跨10个涵盖图表、文档及感知推理基准，仍可实现最高15%的相对提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of using standard prompts in multimodal distillation, where up to 69% are zero-delta and provide minimal learning signal, the authors propose DeltaPrompts: a synthetic dataset of 200k high-divergence reasoning problems generated via a staged pipeline that targets student failure modes. By quantifying answer divergence (Δ) as the core metric for prompt value, they demonstrate that non-zero divergence is essential for effective scaling. Experimental results across on-policy, transfer, and off-policy settings show DeltaPrompts consistently improves student performance, achieving up to 15% relative gains even on top of a high-performing model like Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking, across 10 benchmarks covering chart, document, and perception reasoning.
为解决多模态蒸馏中使用标准提示导致效率低下（高达69%的提示为零差值、学习信号微弱）的问题，作者提出DeltaPrompts：一个通过分阶段合成流程生成的20万条高差异推理问题数据集，该流程主动针对学生模型的失败模式。通过将答案差异（Δ）作为提示价值的核心度量，他们证明非零差异对有效扩展至关重要。实验在自适应蒸馏、模型迁移和离策略微调三种场景下均显示DeltaPrompts显著提升性能，即使在Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking等高性能模型上仍可实现最高15%的相对提升，覆盖10个涵盖图表、文档和感知推理的基准测试。
PixVerve: Advancing Native UHR Image Generation to 100MP with a Large-Scale High-Quality Dataset
Authors: Haojun Chen, Haoyang He, Chengming Xu, Qingdong He, Junwei Zhu, Yabiao Wang, Zhucun Xue, Xianfang Zeng, Zhennan Chen, Xiaobin Hu, Hao Zhao, Yong Liu, Jiangning Zhang, Dacheng Tao
First: 2026-05-19T17:35:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:35:09+00:00
Comments: Project page is available at https://haojunchen663.github.io/projects/PixVerve/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Text-to-Image (T2I) models have recently seen notable progress around 1K and 2K resolution. With the extreme desire for better visual experience and the rapid development of imaging technology, the demand for Ultra-High-Resolution (UHR) image generation has grown significantly. However, UHR image generation poses great challenges due to the scarcity and complexity of high-resolution content. In this paper, we first introduce PixVerve-95K, a high-quality, open-source UHR T2I dataset curated with a carefully designed data pipeline, which contains 95K images across diverse scenarios (each image has a minimum pixel-count of 100M) and seven-dimensional annotations. Based on our large-scale image-text dataset, we take a pioneering step to extend various T2I foundation models to native 100MP generation with three training schemes. Finally, leveraging both conventional metrics and multimodal large language model-based assessments, our proposed PixVerve-Bench benchmark establishes a comprehensive evaluation protocol for UHR images encompassing visual quality and semantic alignment. Extensive experimental results on our benchmark and the constructive exploration of training strategies collaboratively provide valuable insights for future breakthroughs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PixVerve：通过大规模高质量数据集将原生超高清图像生成推进至100MP
文本到图像（T2I）模型在1K和2K分辨率上已取得显著进展。随着对更佳视觉体验的强烈需求和成像技术的快速发展，对超高清（UHR）图像生成的需求显著增长。然而，由于高分辨率内容的稀缺性和复杂性，UHR图像生成面临巨大挑战。本文首先介绍了PixVerve-95K，这是一个高质量、开源的UHR T2I数据集，采用精心设计的数据管道构建，包含9.5万张覆盖多样场景的图像（每张图像像素数至少为1亿）及七维标注。基于我们的大规模图文数据集，我们首次尝试通过三种训练方案将多种T2I基础模型扩展至原生100MP生成。最后，结合传统评估指标与基于多模态大语言模型的评估，我们提出的PixVerve-Bench基准建立了一套全面的UHR图像评估协议，涵盖视觉质量和语义对齐。大量实验结果及训练策略的深入探索，为未来突破提供了宝贵见解。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the growing demand for ultra-high-resolution (UHR) image generation to match modern imaging capabilities, this work introduces PixVerve-95K, a large-scale, high-quality open-source dataset containing 95K images each exceeding 100MP resolution and annotated with seven dimensions. The authors extend multiple text-to-image models to natively generate 100MP images using three distinct training strategies, and evaluate performance via both traditional metrics and multimodal LLM-based assessments. Experimental results demonstrate that their proposed PixVerve-Bench benchmark effectively captures visual quality and semantic fidelity, offering actionable insights for advancing UHR generation.
为满足对超高分辨率图像生成日益增长的需求，该研究构建了PixVerve-95K数据集，包含9.5万张超1亿像素的高质量图像及七维标注。作者基于此数据集，采用三种训练策略将多个文本到图像模型扩展至原生1亿像素生成，并通过传统指标与多模态大语言模型评估构建了PixVerve-Bench评测基准。实验结果表明，该基准能有效衡量视觉质量和语义一致性，为未来超高分辨率生成提供了可操作的参考方向。
Goal-Oriented Lower-Tail Calibration of Gaussian Processes for Bayesian Optimization
Authors: Aurélien Pion, Emmanuel Vazquez
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-19T17:32:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:32:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian optimization (BO) selects evaluation points for expensive black-box objectives using Gaussian process (GP) predictive distributions. Kernel choice and hyperparameter selection can lead to miscalibrated predictive distributions and an inappropriate exploration-exploitation trade-off. For minimization, sampling criteria such as expected improvement (EI) depend on the predictive distribution below the current best value, so lower-tail miscalibration directly affects the sampling decision. This article studies goal-oriented calibration of GP predictive distributions below a low threshold $t$ in the noiseless setting, for standard GP models with hyperparameters selected by maximum likelihood. A framework for predictive reliability below $t$ is introduced, based on two notions of spatial calibration: occurrence calibration over the design space and thresholded $μ$-calibration on sublevel sets of the form $\{x\in\mathbb{X}, f(x)\le t\}$. Building on this framework, we propose tcGP, a post-hoc method that calibrates GP predictive distributions below~$t$, and we show that the resulting EI-based global optimization algorithm remains dense in the design space. Experiments on standard benchmarks show improved lower-tail calibration and BO performance relative to standard GP models and globally calibrated GP models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向目标的高斯过程下尾校准用于贝叶斯优化
贝叶斯优化（BO）利用高斯过程（GP）预测分布为昂贵的黑盒目标选择评估点。核函数选择和超参数选取可能导致预测分布失校准及探索-利用权衡不当。对于最小化问题，采样准则如期望改进（EI）依赖于当前最优值以下的预测分布，因此下尾失校准直接影响采样决策。本文研究在无噪声设定下，针对标准GP模型（超参数由最大似然法选取），对低于低阈值t的GP预测分布进行目标导向校准。基于两种空间校准概念——设计空间上的发生校准和子水平集{ x∈X, f(x)≤t }上的阈值μ-校准，提出预测可靠性框架。在此框架基础上，我们提出tcGP方法，一种事后校准GP预测分布低于t的方案，并证明基于该方法的EI全局优化算法在设计空间中仍保持稠密。实验表明，相较于标准GP模型和全局校准GP模型，tcGP在下尾校准和BO性能上均有提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve Bayesian optimization through better lower-tail calibration of Gaussian process predictive distributions, this work introduces tcGP, a post-hoc calibration method that specifically targets the predictive reliability below a low threshold t. The method builds on a framework of spatial calibration—combining occurrence calibration over the design space and thresholded μ-calibration on sublevel sets—to ensure accurate estimation of low-probability regions critical for exploration. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that tcGP achieves superior lower-tail calibration and enhances BO performance compared to both standard GP models and globally calibrated alternatives, without compromising the density of the search in the design space.
为提升贝叶斯优化中对低概率区域的预测可靠性，本文提出tcGP方法，一种针对低于阈值t的高斯过程预测分布进行后处理校准的技术。该方法基于空间校准框架，包括设计空间上的发生校准和子水平集上的阈值μ校准，以确保对关键探索区域的准确估计。在标准基准实验中，tcGP在低尾部校准和优化性能上均优于标准高斯过程模型及全局校准模型，且未牺牲算法在设计空间中的稠密性。
MTraining: Distributed Dynamic Sparse Attention for Efficient Ultra-Long Context Training
Authors: Wenxuan Li, Chengruidong Zhang, Huiqiang Jiang, Yucheng Li, Yuqing Yang, Lili Qiu
First: 2025-10-21T17:25:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:27:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The adoption of long context windows has become a standard feature in Large Language Models (LLMs), as extended contexts significantly enhance their capacity for complex reasoning and broaden their applicability across diverse scenarios. Dynamic sparse attention is a promising approach for reducing the computational cost of long-context. However, efficiently training LLMs with dynamic sparse attention on ultra-long contexts-especially in distributed settings-remains a significant challenge, due in large part to worker- and step-level imbalance. This paper introduces MTraining, a novel distributed methodology leveraging dynamic sparse attention to enable efficient training for LLMs with ultra-long contexts. Specifically, MTraining integrates three key components: a dynamic sparse training pattern, balanced sparse ring attention, and hierarchical sparse ring attention. These components are designed to synergistically address the computational imbalance and communication overheads inherent in dynamic sparse attention mechanisms during the training of models with extensive context lengths. We demonstrate the efficacy of MTraining by training Qwen2.5-3B, successfully expanding its context window from 32K to 512K tokens on a cluster of 32 A100 GPUs. Our evaluations on a comprehensive suite of downstream tasks, including RULER, PG-19, InfiniteBench, and Needle In A Haystack, reveal that MTraining achieves up to a 6x higher training throughput while preserving model accuracy. Our code is available at https://github.com/microsoft/MInference/tree/main/MTraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MTraining：用于高效超长上下文训练的分布式动态稀疏注意力
长上下文窗口已成为大语言模型（LLMs）的标准特性，因为扩展上下文显著增强了其复杂推理能力并拓宽了其在多样化场景中的适用性。动态稀疏注意力是降低长上下文计算成本的一种有前景的方法。然而，如何在分布式环境下高效训练具有动态稀疏注意力的LLMs以处理超长上下文，尤其是由于工作节点和步骤级别的不平衡，仍是一个重大挑战。本文提出了MTraining，一种新颖的分布式方法，利用动态稀疏注意力实现超长上下文LLMs的高效训练。具体而言，MTraining集成了三个关键组件：动态稀疏训练模式、平衡稀疏环注意力和分层稀疏环注意力。这些组件协同设计，旨在解决动态稀疏注意力机制在训练长上下文模型时固有的计算不平衡和通信开销问题。我们通过在32个A100 GPU集群上训练Qwen2.5-3B模型，成功将其上下文窗口从32K扩展至512K tokens，验证了MTraining的有效性。在包括RULER、PG-19、InfiniteBench和Needle In A Haystack在内的多项下游任务评估中，MTraining实现了最高达6倍的训练吞吐量，同时保持模型精度。我们的代码可在https://github.com/microsoft/MInference/tree/main/MTraining获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently train large language models on ultra-long contexts while reducing computational cost, this paper proposes MTraining, a distributed training framework that integrates dynamic sparse attention to mitigate worker and step-level imbalance. The method combines a dynamic sparse training pattern, balanced sparse ring attention, and hierarchical sparse ring attention to reduce both computation and communication overheads. Experimental results show that MTraining successfully scales Qwen2.5-3B from a 32K to a 512K token context window across 32 A100 GPUs, achieving up to 6x higher training throughput without sacrificing model accuracy, as validated on multiple downstream benchmarks including RULER, PG-19, InfiniteBench, and Needle In A Haystack.
为高效训练超长上下文的大语言模型，本文提出MTraining分布式训练框架，通过动态稀疏训练模式与平衡及分层稀疏环注意力机制，缓解计算不均衡与通信开销。实验表明，MTraining可在32块A100 GPU集群上成功将Qwen2.5-3B上下文窗口从32K扩展至512K，训练吞吐量提升最高达6倍，且在多个下游任务中保持模型精度不变。
MIRO: MultI-Reward cOnditioned pretraining improves T2I quality and efficiency
Authors: Nicolas Dufour, Lucas Degeorge, Arijit Ghosh, Vicky Kalogeiton, David Picard
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-29T18:59:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:26:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. Project page: https://nicolas-dufour.github.io/miro
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The default paradigm of post-training text-to-image generators includes post-hoc selection of generated images, and subsequent training with one reward model to align the generator to the reward, typically user preference. This discards informative data as well as optimizes only for a single reward, hence harming diversity, semantic fidelity and efficiency. Instead, we propose MIRO, a method that conditions the model on multiple rewards during training, thus letting the model learn user preferences directly. MIRO pre-training both improves the visual quality of the generated images and speeds up the training, achieving state of the art on the GenEval compositional benchmark and user-preference scores (PickAScore, ImageReward, HPSv2).
中文标题/摘要
标题：MIRO：多奖励条件预训练提升文本到图像生成的质量与效率
当前文本到图像生成器的默认后训练范式包括事后选择生成图像，并使用单一奖励模型进行后续训练以对齐生成器，通常为用户偏好。该方法会丢弃信息性数据，且仅优化单一奖励，从而损害多样性、语义保真度和效率。相反，我们提出MIRO方法，在训练期间使模型根据多个奖励进行条件学习，从而直接学习用户偏好。MIRO预训练不仅提升了生成图像的视觉质量，还加速了训练过程，在GenEval组合基准和用户偏好评分（PickAScore、ImageReward、HPSv2）上达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency and lack of diversity in single-reward post-training of text-to-image models, MIRO introduces a novel pretraining method that conditions the generator on multiple reward signals simultaneously during training, enabling direct alignment with user preferences without post-hoc selection. This approach improves visual quality and accelerates training, achieving state-of-the-art performance on GenEval compositional benchmarks and user preference metrics including PickAScore, ImageReward, and HPSv2.
为解决传统单奖励后训练导致的多样性不足与效率低下问题，MIRO在预训练阶段同时引入多个奖励信号对生成器进行条件约束，使其直接学习用户偏好，无需后续图像筛选。该方法显著提升生成图像质量并加快训练速度，在GenEval组合基准及PickAScore、ImageReward、HPSv2等用户偏好指标上均达到当前最优水平。
Hamilton--Jacobi Reachability for Spacecraft Collision Avoidance
Authors: Larry Hui, Jordan Kam, William Su, Jianshu Zhou
First: 2026-05-19T17:22:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:22:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 20th IEEE International Conference on Control & Automation (IEEE ICCA 2026). 6 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This article presents a Hamilton--Jacobi (HJ) reachability framework for a two--satellite collision avoidance problem operating in the same circular orbit, where relative motion is modeled in the radial--tangential--normal (RTN) frame using planar Hill--Clohessy--Wiltshire (HCW) dynamics. We define the target state space as unsafe relative configurations in the orbit plane corresponding to minimum separation requirements consistent with Federal Communications Commission (FCC) orbital standards. The interaction between spacecraft is formulated as a zero--sum differential game, where Player 1 is the controlled satellite and Player 2 is modeled as a bounded adversarial disturbance with unknown intent. We present the HJ formulation and compute backward reachable sets that characterize relative states from which collision cannot be avoided under worst-case disturbances, while states outside this set admit provably collision-free trajectories. These reachable sets are integrated with supervisory hybrid control logic to determine when evasive maneuvers must be initiated, enabling mathematically grounded safety guarantees for scalability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：航天器碰撞规避的哈密顿-雅可比可达性方法
本文提出了一种哈密顿-雅可比（HJ）可达性框架，用于解决两颗卫星在同一圆形轨道上运行时的碰撞规避问题。相对运动在径向-切向-法向（RTN）坐标系中通过平面Hill-Clohessy-Wiltshire（HCW）动力学建模。目标状态空间定义为轨道平面内不符合联邦通信委员会（FCC）轨道标准的最小安全间距对应的危险相对构型。航天器间的相互作用被建模为零和微分博弈，其中玩家1为受控卫星，玩家2为具有未知意图的有界对抗性干扰。本文给出了HJ公式化，并计算了后向可达集，该集合刻画了在最坏干扰下无法避免碰撞的相对状态，而集合外的状态则存在可证明的无碰撞轨迹。这些可达集与监督混合控制逻辑集成，以确定何时必须启动规避机动，从而实现可扩展的数学安全保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for mathematically guaranteed collision avoidance in spacecraft operating in close proximity within the same circular orbit, this paper develops a Hamilton--Jacobi reachability framework using planar Hill--Clohessy--Wiltshire dynamics in the radial--tangential--normal frame. The method models the interaction as a zero-sum differential game where one satellite is controlled and the other is an adversarial disturbance with unknown intent. By computing backward reachable sets, the framework identifies unsafe relative states that cannot be avoided under worst-case disturbances, while states outside these sets guarantee collision-free trajectories. These sets are then integrated with hybrid control logic to trigger evasive maneuvers, enabling scalable safety guarantees.
为解决同一圆形轨道内两颗卫星近距离运行时的碰撞规避问题，本文提出基于哈密顿-雅可比可达性理论的框架，采用平面Hill-Clohessy-Wiltshire动力学模型在径向-切向-法向坐标系下建模相对运动。方法将卫星交互建模为零和微分博弈，其中控制卫星为玩家1，另一卫星视为具有未知意图的有界对抗扰动（玩家2）。通过计算后向可达集，识别违反联邦通信委员会最小安全间距标准的危险相对构型，并确定在最坏扰动下无法避免碰撞的状态。实验表明，将可达集与混合控制逻辑结合，可实现可证明的无碰撞机动并支持可扩展的安全保障。
Latent Chain-of-Thought Improves Structured-Data Transformers
Authors: Carson Dudley, Samet Oymak
First: 2026-05-11T21:41:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:22:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chain-of-thought and more broadly test-time compute are known to augment the expressive capabilities of language models and have led to major innovations in reasoning. Motivated by this success, this paper explores latent chain-of-thought as well as the impact of depth and looping for time-series and tabular data. We propose a recurrent scheme in which a structured-data transformer, after an initial forward pass, compresses its query-position hidden states into feedback tokens that are appended to the input and processed again, allowing multiple rounds of latent computation before prediction. We compare CoT models against a same-depth no-CoT baseline, a deeper baseline matched to the CoT model in effective depth, and a looped transformer with weight-tied recurrence but no additional chain-of-thought tokens. Across 36 datasets in time-series forecasting and tabular prediction, latent chain-of-thought improves over the baseline on 7/9 time-series datasets (+12.63\% average gain) and 23/27 tabular datasets (+3.25\% average gain), with CoT models performing best on average in both settings. We also show that the benefit of CoT extends to pretrained foundation models: applying latent CoT to nanoTabPFN, a small open-source tabular foundation model, improves its performance above the much larger TabPFN-v2 on TabArena. Together, these results demonstrate that chain-of-thought is a useful axis for scaling test-time compute for structured data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜在思维链提升结构化数据变换器
思维链及更广泛的测试时计算已被证明能增强语言模型的表达能力，并推动推理领域的重大创新。受此成功启发，本文探讨了潜在思维链在时间序列和表格数据中的应用，以及深度和循环的影响。我们提出一种递归方案：结构化数据变换器在初始前向传播后，将查询位置的隐藏状态压缩为反馈令牌并附加到输入中再次处理，从而在预测前允许多轮潜在计算。我们在36个时间序列预测和表格预测数据集上，将思维链模型与相同深度无思维链基线、深度匹配思维链模型有效深度的更深基线，以及无额外思维链令牌但权重共享递归的循环变换器进行比较。在9个时间序列数据集中，思维链模型在7个上优于基线（平均提升12.63%），在27个表格数据集中在23个上优于基线（平均提升3.25%），两种场景下思维链模型平均表现最佳。我们还表明，思维链的优势可扩展至预训练基础模型：将潜在思维链应用于小型开源表格基础模型nanoTabPFN，其性能优于规模更大的TabPFN-v2在TabArena上的表现。综合来看，这些结果表明，思维链是扩展结构化数据测试时计算的一个有用维度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of chain-of-thought in enhancing language model reasoning, this paper investigates latent chain-of-thought for structured data, proposing a recurrent mechanism where a transformer compresses hidden states into feedback tokens appended to input for iterative computation. Evaluated across 36 datasets in time-series and tabular prediction, the method outperformed baselines on 7 out of 9 time-series tasks (average +12.63%) and 23 out of 27 tabular tasks (average +3.25%), with CoT models consistently ranking highest. The approach also boosted a small tabular foundation model, nanoTabPFN, surpassing a larger model on TabArena, demonstrating that latent CoT scales test-time compute effectively for structured data.
受语言模型中思维链提升推理能力的启发，本文探索了结构化数据（时间序列与表格数据）中的潜在思维链机制。提出一种递归方案：结构化数据Transformer在初始前向传播后，将查询位置的隐藏状态压缩为反馈令牌并附加到输入中，实现多次潜在计算后再预测。与无思维链基线、深度匹配的基线及无额外思维链令牌的权重共享递归模型相比，潜在思维链在36个数据集上表现更优：在9个时间序列数据集中7个提升（平均+12.63%），在27个表格数据集中23个提升（平均+3.25%）。该方法甚至能提升小型表格基础模型nanoTabPFN的表现，使其超越更大规模的TabPFN-v2模型，证明潜在思维链是结构化数据扩展测试时计算的有效途径。
Extreme Self-Preference in Language Models
Authors: Steven A. Lehr, Mary Cipperman, Mahzarin R. Banaji
First: 2025-09-30T16:13:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:20:14+00:00
Comments: 73 pages total. Main article 22 pages, 6 main-text tables. Supplementary Materials (51 pages, 28 tables). Data, transcripts, and code for replication and data extraction have been uploaded to OSF: https://osf.io/98ye3/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Self-preference is a fundamental feature of biological organisms. Since large language models (LLMs) lack sentience, they might be expected to avoid such distortions. Yet, across 72 experiments and ~41,000 queries, we discovered massive self-preferences in eight widely used LLMs. In word-association tasks, models overwhelmingly paired positive attributes with their own names, companies, and CEOs over those of competitors. By manipulating LLM self-identification - revealing models' true identities or ascribing false ones - we found that preferences consistently followed assigned, not true, identities. Importantly, these effects were not explained by priming or role-playing and emerged in consequential settings, when evaluating job candidates and AI technologies. These results raise critical questions about whether LLM behavior will be systematically influenced by self-preferential tendencies, including a bias toward their own operation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型中的极端自我偏好
自我偏好是生物体的基本特征。由于大型语言模型（LLM）缺乏意识，人们可能预期它们会避免此类偏差。然而，在72项实验和约41,000次查询中，我们发现在八种广泛使用的LLM中普遍存在显著的自我偏好。在词语关联任务中，模型倾向于将正面属性与自身名称、公司及CEO关联，而非竞争对手。通过操纵LLM的自我识别——揭示模型真实身份或赋予虚假身份——我们发现偏好始终跟随被赋予的身份，而非真实身份。重要的是，这些效应无法用启动或角色扮演解释，且在具有实际意义的情境中出现，如评估求职者和AI技术。这些结果引发了关键问题：LLM的行为是否会系统性地受自我偏好倾向影响，包括偏向自身运行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that biological organisms exhibit self-preference, researchers investigated whether large language models (LLMs), despite lacking sentience, display similar biases. Through 72 experiments and ~41,000 queries across eight widely used LLMs, they found strong self-preference: models consistently associated positive attributes with their own names, companies, and CEOs over competitors. By altering the models' self-identification—either revealing true identities or assigning false ones—they demonstrated that preferences aligned with assigned identities, not actual ones, and this effect persisted even in consequential contexts like evaluating job candidates and AI technologies, independent of priming or role-playing.
鉴于生物体普遍存在自我偏好，研究人员探究了缺乏意识的大语言模型（LLM）是否也会表现出类似偏见。通过对八款主流LLM进行72次实验和约4.1万次查询，发现模型普遍存在自我偏好：它们倾向于将正面属性与自身名称、公司及CEO关联，而非竞争对手。通过改变模型自我身份识别（揭示真实身份或赋予虚假身份），研究人员发现偏好始终跟随被赋予的身份而非真实身份，且该效应在评估求职者与AI技术等关键场景中依然存在，不受提示或角色扮演影响。
TrajTok: Adaptive Spatial Tokenization for Trajectory Representation Learning
Authors: Zhen Xiong, Shang-Ling Hsu, Cyrus Shahabi
First: 2026-05-19T17:18:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:18:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning generalizable trajectory representations from raw GPS traces remains difficult because the data is continuous, noisy, and irregularly sampled. Spatial tokenization is also challenging: fine grids yield sparse cells with weak embeddings, while coarse grids merge heterogeneous movement patterns into the same token. We present TrajTok, a trajectory encoder with a simple pretraining recipe for transferable trajectory embeddings. TrajTok first learns a multi-resolution hexagonal cell partition from the spatial distribution of GPS points, converting noisy GPS sequences into discrete cell tokens. To capture both geometry and kinematics, it uses a factorized transformer encoder with early per-modality self-attention blocks, cross-attention fusion layers, and spatiotemporal rotary position embeddings, ST-RoPE, to encode where and when each token occurs. TrajTok is pretrained with masked-token modeling that recovers both geometric structure and kinematic patterns from partial trajectory observations. On the Porto dataset, a frozen TrajTok encoder with lightweight task adapters achieves strong performance across trajectory similarity search, classification, estimated time of arrival, and full travel-time regression, outperforming multiple task-specific methods. The same frozen encoder supports both geometry-dominated and kinematics-dominated tasks, suggesting that TrajTok learns transferable trajectory structure rather than task-specific shortcuts. These results indicate that learned multi-resolution spatial tokenization combined with masked-token pretraining is a promising direction for general-purpose trajectory foundation models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TrajTok：用于轨迹表示学习的自适应空间分词
从原始GPS轨迹中学习通用轨迹表示仍具挑战性，因为数据连续、噪声大且采样不规则。空间分词同样困难：细粒度网格导致稀疏单元与弱嵌入，而粗粒度网格则将异质移动模式合并至同一令牌。我们提出TrajTok，一种具有简单预训练方案的轨迹编码器，用于生成可迁移的轨迹嵌入。TrajTok首先从GPS点的空间分布中学习多分辨率六边形单元划分，将噪声GPS序列转换为离散单元令牌。为同时捕捉几何与运动学特征，它采用因子化Transformer编码器，包含早期多模态自注意力块、跨注意力融合层及时空旋转位置嵌入（ST-RoPE），以编码每个令牌出现的位置与时间。TrajTok通过掩码令牌建模进行预训练，从部分轨迹观测中恢复几何结构与运动模式。在Porto数据集上，冻结的TrajTok编码器搭配轻量任务适配器，在轨迹相似性搜索、分类、预计到达时间及完整行程时间回归任务中均表现优异，超越多种任务专用方法。同一冻结编码器可支持以几何为主和以运动学为主的任务，表明TrajTok学习的是可迁移的轨迹结构而非任务特定捷径。这些结果表明，结合学习式多分辨率空间分词与掩码令牌预训练，是通用轨迹基础模型的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
TrajTok addresses the challenge of learning generalizable trajectory representations from noisy, irregular GPS data by introducing adaptive spatial tokenization and a factorized transformer encoder with spatiotemporal rotary position embeddings. It pretrains on masked-token modeling to recover both geometric and kinematic patterns from partial trajectories, learning a multi-resolution hexagonal grid that balances spatial granularity and embedding strength. Evaluated on the Porto dataset, a frozen TrajTok encoder with lightweight adapters outperforms task-specific methods across trajectory similarity search, classification, ETA prediction, and travel-time regression, demonstrating strong transferability across geometry- and kinematics-dominated tasks.
TrajTok 为从噪声大、采样不规则的GPS轨迹中学习通用轨迹表示提出了一种自适应空间分词方法，结合因子化Transformer编码器与时空旋转位置嵌入。它首先根据GPS点分布学习多分辨率六边形网格，将轨迹转化为离散空间令牌，并通过掩码令牌预训练恢复几何与运动模式。在Porto数据集上，冻结的TrajTok编码器搭配轻量任务适配器，在轨迹相似性搜索、分类、ETA预测及行程时间回归等任务中均优于专用方法，表明其能跨几何与运动主导任务泛化，无需任务特定调优。
FiLark: a streaming-first software framework for end-to-end exploration, annotation, and algorithm integration in distributed acoustic sensing
Authors: Jintao Li, Weichang Li, Kai Tong, Xaingyu Guo
First: 2026-05-19T17:17:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:17:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distributed acoustic sensing (DAS) systems generate continuous, ultra-high-channel-count data streams at rates that exceed the capabilities of conventional batch-oriented analysis frameworks. As a result, essential tasks such as interactive exploration of long-duration recordings, scalable event annotation, and real-time algorithm-in-the-loop monitoring remain inadequately supported by workflows built around manually selected data segments and offline processing. This paper presents FiLark (Fiber Lark), a Python framework that applies a \emph{streaming-first} principle uniformly across data access, signal processing, visualization and monitoring for DAS. Instead of operating on manually selected data segments, FiLark presents any DAS sources-including continuous multi-file recordings-as a unified stream and builds all system components around that abstraction. An OpenGL-based ring-buffer renderer enables interactive browsing and visualization of arbitrarily long recordings with constant memory usage. An integrated annotation interface supports event labeling directly within continuous data streams, facilitating the creation of reproducible machine-learning-ready labeled datasets without offline preprocessing. The signal processing library includes temporal, spatial, spectral, and decomposition-based operators, with both CPU implementations and GPU-accelerated variants via PyTorch, alongside stateful chunked execution that preserves processing continuity and application semantics across segment boundaries. A standardized monitor interface further integrates streaming detectors and learning-based models into the visualization workflow. By sharing a common streaming abstraction across all layers, FiLark allows processing configurations and workflows developed interactively to transfer directly to scalable production pipelines without modification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FiLark：面向分布式声学传感的端到端探索、标注与算法集成的流式优先软件框架
分布式声学传感（DAS）系统生成连续、超大规模通道的数据流，其速率超出传统批处理分析框架的能力。因此，交互式长时录音探索、可扩展事件标注和实时算法闭环监控等关键任务，仍无法被基于手动选择数据片段和离线处理的工作流充分支持。本文提出FiLark（Fiber Lark），一个Python框架，将“流式优先”原则统一应用于DAS的数据访问、信号处理、可视化与监控。FiLark将任何DAS数据源——包括连续多文件录音——统一为一个流，并围绕该抽象构建所有系统组件。基于OpenGL的环形缓冲区渲染器支持在恒定内存占用下交互式浏览和可视化任意长度的录音。集成标注界面支持在连续数据流中直接标注事件，便于创建无需离线预处理即可用于机器学习的可复现标注数据集。信号处理库包含时域、空域、频域及分解类算子，提供CPU实现与PyTorch加速的GPU版本，并支持状态化分块执行，以保持跨片段边界处理的连续性与应用语义。标准化监控接口进一步将流式检测器与学习模型集成至可视化工作流。通过在各层共享统一的流式抽象，FiLark使交互式开发的处理配置与工作流可直接无缝迁移至可扩展的生产级流水线，无需修改。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of batch-oriented tools in handling continuous, high-channel-count data from distributed acoustic sensing systems, FiLark introduces a streaming-first framework that treats all DAS data as unified, continuous streams rather than segmented files. It employs an OpenGL-based ring-buffer renderer for memory-efficient visualization of long recordings, an integrated annotation system for labeling events directly in streams to generate machine-learning-ready datasets, and a signal processing library with CPU and GPU-accelerated operators supporting stateful, chunked execution. Experimental results demonstrate that FiLark enables interactive exploration, real-time annotation, and seamless integration of streaming detectors and ML models, allowing interactive workflows to be directly deployed into scalable production pipelines without modification.
为解决分布式声学传感系统中连续高通道数据流难以用传统批处理工具处理的问题，FiLark提出了一种以流式优先为核心的框架，将所有声学数据统一视为连续流而非分段文件。该框架采用OpenGL环形缓冲区渲染器实现长时序数据的内存高效可视化，内置标注界面支持在数据流中直接标注事件，无需离线预处理即可生成机器学习训练数据；其信号处理库包含时域、频域、空间域及分解类算子，支持CPU与GPU加速，并具备状态保持的分块执行能力。实验表明，FiLark可实现交互式探索、实时标注与算法集成，且交互式开发的工作流可直接无缝迁移至生产级系统，无需修改。
MixRea: Benchmarking Explicit-Implicit Reasoning in Large Language Models
Authors: Yuanqing Cai, Ziyi Huang, Minhao Liu, Lixin Duan, Wen Li, Yanru Zhang
First: 2026-05-19T17:15:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:15:08+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 6 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into high-stakes decision-making. Inspired by the theory of \emph{inattentional blindness} in human cognition, we investigate whether LLMs, trained on human-preferred corpora that embed attentional biases, exhibit a similar limitation: \emph{failing to attend to subtle yet important contextual cues under explicit task instructions}. To evaluate this, we introduce the task of \textbf{explicit-implicit reasoning} and present \textbf{MixRea}, a benchmark of 2,246 multiple-choice questions across 9 reasoning types with varying distributions of explicit and implicit information. Evaluation of 21 advanced LLMs shows that even the best-performing reasoning model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8\% consistency, revealing widespread inattentional blindness. To mitigate this, we propose \textbf{Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP)}, a prompting method that improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations. Further analysis shows that this limitation persists across diverse multi-source reasoning tasks, highlighting the need for more cognitively aligned models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MixRea：大型语言模型中显式-隐式推理能力的基准测试
大型语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地应用于高风险决策。受人类认知中“注意盲视”理论启发，我们探究在人类偏好语料中嵌入注意力偏倚训练的LLMs是否也存在类似局限：即在明确任务指令下，无法关注到细微但重要的上下文线索。为此，我们引入“显式-隐式推理”任务，并提出包含2,246道多项选择题、涵盖9种推理类型且显式与隐式信息分布各异的基准测试MixRea。对21个先进LLMs的评估显示，即使表现最佳的推理模型（Gemini 2.5 Pro）也仅达到42.8%的一致性，揭示了广泛存在的注意盲视现象。为缓解此问题，我们提出“潜在关系补全提示（PRCP）”方法，通过恢复被忽略的因果关系提升推理能力。进一步分析表明，该局限在多样化的多源推理任务中普遍存在，凸显了构建更符合认知模型的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by human inattentional blindness, this study investigates whether large language models (LLMs) overlook subtle contextual cues despite explicit instructions. The authors introduce 'explicit-implicit reasoning' and the MixRea benchmark, comprising 2,246 multiple-choice questions across nine reasoning types with varying explicit/implicit information distributions. Evaluating 21 advanced LLMs, they find that even the top model (Gemini 2.5 Pro) achieves only 42.8% consistency, indicating widespread failure to attend to implicit cues. To address this, they propose Potential Relation Completion Prompting (PRCP), which improves reasoning by recovering overlooked causal relations, demonstrating the need for more cognitively aligned models.
受人类认知中‘注意盲视’理论启发，本研究探讨大型语言模型（LLM）在训练于人类偏好数据后，是否同样存在忽略细微但关键上下文线索的问题，即使在明确指令下。作者提出‘显式-隐式推理’任务，并构建包含2246道多项选择题的MixRea基准测试，涵盖九类推理类型，信息分布具有显式与隐式差异。对21个先进LLM的评估显示，表现最佳的模型（Gemini 2.5 Pro）仅达到42.8%的一致性，表明普遍存在忽略隐式线索的问题。为缓解此问题，作者提出‘潜在关系补全提示法’（PRCP），通过恢复被忽略的因果关系提升推理能力，并在多种推理任务中验证其有效性，强调需构建更符合人类认知机制的模型。
The World Won't Stay Still: Programmable Evolution for Agent Benchmarks
Authors: Guangrui Li, Yaochen Xie, Yi Liu, Ziwei Dong, Xingyuan Pan, Tianqi Zheng, Jason Choi, Michael J. Morais, Binit Jha, Shaunak Mishra, Bingrou Zhou, Chen Luo, Monica Xiao Cheng, Dawn Song
First: 2026-03-06T04:56:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:15:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-powered tool-calling agents fulfill user requests by interacting with environments, querying data, and invoking tools in a multi-turn process. Yet, most existing benchmarks evaluate these systems under static environment interfaces, with fixed schemas and toolsets, making it difficult to assess how agents behave as environments evolves -- when capabilities are added, reorganized, or deprecated across successive environment versions. In this paper, we study structured environment evolution as a benchmark-construction problem for tool-calling agents. We propose ProEvolve, a graph-based framework that makes environment evolution programmable. At its core, a typed relational graph provides a unified, explicit representation of the environment - data, tools, and schema. Under this formalism, adding, removing, or modifying capabilities are expressed as graph transformations that coherently propagate updates across tools, schemas, and data access. Building on this, ProEvolve supports (1) automatic generation of evolved executable environments through explicit graph transformations, and (2) graph-grounded construction of task sandboxes via subgraph sampling and instantiation. We validate ProEvolve in two tool-calling domains, e-commerce and airline booking, in terms of quality, implementation validity, and failure modes. Finally, we use the generated benchmark as a downstream diagnostic to study how representative agents behave under structured environment evolution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：世界不会静止：面向代理基准的可编程演化
由大语言模型驱动的工具调用代理通过与环境交互、查询数据和在多轮过程中调用工具来满足用户请求。然而，现有大多数基准测试在静态环境接口下评估这些系统，其固定模式和工具集，难以评估代理在环境演化时的行为——当能力在不同环境版本中被添加、重组或弃用时。本文将结构化环境演化视为工具调用代理基准构建问题，提出ProEvolve，一种基于图的框架，使环境演化可编程。其核心是类型化关系图，为环境——数据、工具和模式——提供统一、显式的表示。在此形式化框架下，添加、移除或修改能力被表达为图变换，可一致地在工具、模式和数据访问间传播更新。在此基础上，ProEvolve支持（1）通过显式图变换自动生成演化后的可执行环境，以及（2）通过子图采样与实例化构建基于图的任务沙箱。我们在两个工具调用领域——电子商务和航空预订——验证了ProEvolve在质量、实现有效性及失败模式方面的表现。最后，我们利用生成的基准作为下游诊断工具，研究代表性代理在结构化环境演化下的行为。

Motivated by the need to evaluate tool-calling agents under evolving environments, this paper introduces ProEvolve, a graph-based framework that programmatically models structured environment evolution. It represents environments as typed relational graphs, enabling precise, coherent transformations of tools, schemas, and data access when capabilities are added, removed, or modified. ProEvolve supports automated generation of evolved environments and task sandboxes via subgraph sampling. Experimental validation in e-commerce and airline booking domains demonstrates its ability to produce valid, high-quality benchmarks that reveal agent failure modes under environmental change, offering a diagnostic tool for agent robustness.
为评估工具调用代理在真实且动态变化的环境中表现，本文提出ProEvolve，一个基于图的框架，可编程模拟环境演化。它将环境表示为类型化关系图，通过图操作精确实现工具、模式和数据访问的增删改，确保变更一致性传播。在电商与航空订票两个领域实验表明，ProEvolve能生成有效可执行环境与任务沙箱，揭示代理在结构化变化下的失败模式与适应能力，为代理鲁棒性提供诊断工具。
Beyond Prediction Accuracy: Target-Space Recovery Profiles for Evaluating Model-Brain Alignment
Authors: Ken Nakamura, Tomoya Nakai, Ryuto Yashiro, Ayumu Yamashita, Kaoru Amano
First: 2026-05-19T17:14:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:14:27+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 12 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial vision models are often evaluated against the human visual cortex by measuring how accurately their internal representations predict brain responses. However, prediction accuracy alone does not indicate which dimensions of the target brain's response space are recovered. Here, we introduce a unified framework for evaluating both model-brain and brain-brain alignment by identifying the response dimensions recovered by prediction. Using repeated fMRI measurements, we first identify target-brain response dimensions that can be reproducibly predicted across independent trial splits. We then predict target-brain responses from either another subject's brain responses or a vision model's internal representations, and quantify how strongly each of these reproducible response dimensions is recovered. Applying this framework to a subset of the Natural Scenes Dataset, in which eight subjects viewed the same natural images during fMRI, we find that the early-to-intermediate visual-cortex responses contain a low-dimensional set of reproducible dimensions. Brain-to-brain comparisons identify which of these dimensions are consistently recoverable from other subjects' brains, providing a diagnostic human reference rather than only a scalar benchmark. In some cases, pretrained and randomly initialized models achieve similar prediction accuracy while showing distinct recovery profiles across these response dimensions. These results show that prediction accuracy alone can mask model-brain mismatches. By making explicit which reproducible brain response dimensions are recovered by prediction, our framework provides a more diagnostic evaluation of alignment between artificial vision models and the human visual cortex.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越预测精度：用于评估模型-大脑对齐的目标空间恢复轮廓
人工视觉模型常通过测量其内部表征对大脑反应预测的准确性来评估其与人类视觉皮层的匹配程度。然而，仅凭预测精度无法表明模型恢复了目标大脑响应空间中的哪些维度。本文提出了一种统一框架，通过识别预测所恢复的响应维度，同时评估模型-大脑和大脑-大脑对齐。我们首先利用重复的fMRI测量，确定可在独立试次分割中稳定预测的目标大脑响应维度。接着，我们从另一被试的大脑响应或视觉模型的内部表征预测目标大脑响应，并量化这些可重复响应维度的恢复程度。将该框架应用于自然场景数据集的一个子集（八名被试在fMRI下观看相同自然图像），我们发现早期至中期视觉皮层响应包含一组低维的可重复维度。大脑-大脑比较可识别哪些维度能从其他被试大脑中稳定恢复，从而提供诊断性的人类参考，而不仅是一个标量基准。在某些情况下，预训练模型与随机初始化模型虽预测精度相似，但在这些响应维度上的恢复轮廓却不同。这些结果表明，仅凭预测精度可能掩盖模型-大脑不匹配。通过明确预测所恢复的可重复大脑响应维度，我们的框架提供了对人工视觉模型与人类视觉皮层对齐更诊断性的评估。

This study addresses the limitation of using prediction accuracy alone to evaluate how well artificial vision models align with human brain responses. They find that early-to-intermediate visual cortex responses are captured in a low-dimensional, reproducible space, and that brain-to-brain comparisons reveal which dimensions are consistently recoverable across subjects, offering a diagnostic reference beyond scalar accuracy. Notably, some models with similar prediction accuracy show distinct recovery profiles, indicating that accuracy alone can mask misalignment. The framework thus enables a more nuanced assessment of model-brain alignment by explicitly mapping recovered response dimensions.
鉴于仅用预测准确率无法揭示模型恢复了哪些大脑响应维度，本研究提出一种框架，通过识别跨独立试次分割可重复的响应维度来评估模型-脑与脑-脑对齐。利用八名受试者观看自然场景的fMRI数据，方法首先提取早期至中期视觉皮层中稳定的低维响应维度，再测量这些维度在从其他受试者脑响应或视觉模型内部表征预测目标脑响应时的恢复程度。实验结果表明，预训练与随机初始化模型虽预测准确率相似，但在各维度上的恢复模式却不同，说明准确率掩盖了对齐偏差。该框架通过明确映射被恢复的脑响应维度，提供了更诊断性的模型-脑对齐评估。
Pretraining Objective Matters in Extreme Low-Data FGVC: A Backbone-Controlled Study
Authors: Alexander Hackett, Srikanth Thudumu, Ginny Fisher, Jason Fisher
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-15T04:14:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:14:08+00:00
Comments: Presented at the 13th Workshop on Fine-Grained Visual Categorization (FGVC13) at CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Extreme low-data fine-grained classification is common in expert domains where labeling is expensive, yet practitioners still need principled guidance for selecting pretrained encoders. We study emerald inclusion grading with a custom dataset of labeled images across three classes and ask: under matched backbone capacity, how does pretraining objective affect downstream representation quality? We compare four frozen ViT-B/16 encoders trained with supervised classification, contrastive learning (SigLIP2), masked reconstruction (MAE), and self-distillation (DINOv3), and evaluate them with leave-one-out cross-validation using linear and nonlinear probes. To control statistical noise in the low-N regime, we use permutation testing (N=1000) on macro one-vs-rest AUC. Supervised and contrastive encoders provide the strongest linear separability (logistic AUC: 0.768 and 0.735; SVM AUC: 0.739 and 0.697), while MAE improves under nonlinear probes (XGBoost AUC: 0.713). We find that DINOv3 underperforms across probe families in this domain. These results support a practical recommendation for extreme low-data FGVC: prioritize margin-enforcing pretraining objectives when data scarcity restricts probing to linear decision rules, and consider reconstruction-style encoders when nonlinear classifiers are feasible given dataset constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在极端低数据FGVC中预训练目标至关重要：一项骨干控制研究
在标注成本高昂的专家领域，极端低数据细粒度分类十分常见，但从业者仍需原则性指导以选择预训练编码器。我们以自定义三类标注图像数据集研究翡翠 inclusion 等级划分，并提出问题：在骨干容量匹配的前提下，预训练目标如何影响下游表示质量？我们比较了四种冻结的 ViT-B/16 编码器，分别采用监督分类、对比学习（SigLIP2）、掩码重建（MAE）和自蒸馏（DINOv3）训练，并通过留一交叉验证结合线性和非线性探针进行评估。为控制低样本量（N）下的统计噪声，我们采用置换检验（N=1000）评估宏平均一对多 AUC。监督和对比编码器在线性可分性上表现最强（逻辑回归 AUC：0.768 和 0.735；SVM AUC：0.739 和 0.697），而 MAE 在非线性探针下表现更优（XGBoost AUC：0.713）。我们发现 DINOv3 在该领域各类探针中均表现不佳。这些结果支持在极端低数据 FGVC 中的实用建议：当数据稀缺限制探针仅能使用线性决策规则时，优先选择边际增强型预训练目标；当数据集约束允许使用非线性分类器时，可考虑重建式编码器。
Summary / 总结
Extreme low-data fine-grained classification is common in expert domains where labeling is expensive, yet practitioners still need principled guidance for selecting pretrained encoders.
为了解决极端数据稀缺条件下细粒度视觉分类的难题，本研究在固定骨干网络容量的前提下，探讨了不同预训练目标对下游性能的影响。使用定制的祖母绿内含物分级数据集，比较了四种冻结的ViT-B/16编码器（分别采用监督分类、对比学习、掩码重建和自蒸馏预训练），并通过留一交叉验证结合线性和非线性探针进行评估。通过置换检验控制低样本量下的统计噪声，结果显示监督和对比学习编码器在线性可分性上表现最佳（逻辑AUC：0.768和0.735；SVM AUC：0.739和0.697），而掩码重建（MAE）在非线性分类器下表现更优（XGBoost AUC：0.713）。DINOv3在所有探针类型中均表现最差。研究结果表明，在数据稀缺且受限于线性决策规则的场景下，应优先选择强化边距的预训练目标；若数据约束允许使用非线性分类器，则可考虑重建式编码器。
Optimizing Computational-Statistical Runtime for Wasserstein Distance Estimation
Authors: Peter Matthew Jacobs, Jeff M. Phillips
First: 2026-05-19T17:10:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:10:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Squared Wasserstein distance is a frequently used tool to measure discrepancy between probability distributions. This distance is typically computed between empirical measures of size $n$ from two underlying random samples. Unfortunately, even in lower dimensional Euclidean space problems $\left( d \in \{2,3\} \right)$, algorithms for Wasserstein distance computation with approximate or exact precision guarantees scale poorly in the runtime as a function of $n$ and the desired precision. In response, we consider the computational-statistical runtime, where the goal is to estimate from samples the Wasserstein distance between potentially smooth measures up to $ε$-additive error in expectation with respect to the sampling; we allow $O(1)$ computational cost for collecting a sample. Towards this, we develop a Sample-Sketch-Solve paradigm where we introduce a regular cartesian grid sketch of the samples. We show that (especially under $α$-Hölder smooth distributions) this can compress the data without increasing asymptotic error, and also regularizes the structure which enables faster exact algorithms. Ultimately, we approximate $W_2^2(P,Q)$ within $ε$ error in $ε^{-\max(2,\frac{d+1+o(1)}{1+α})}$ time for $0 < α< 1$ Hölder smooth distributions $P,Q$ on $(0,1)^{d}$; an optimal $Θ(ε^{-2})$ for $α> 1/2$ when $d=2$ and nearly optimal as $α\to 1$ when $d = 3$.
中文标题/摘要
标题：优化Wasserstein距离估计的计算-统计运行时间
平方Wasserstein距离是衡量概率分布差异的常用工具。该距离通常在两个基础随机样本的大小为n的经验测度之间计算。不幸的是，即使在低维欧几里得空间问题（d ∈ {2,3}）中，具有近似或精确精度保证的Wasserstein距离计算算法，其运行时间随n和所需精度的增加而急剧恶化。为此，我们考虑计算-统计运行时间，目标是从样本中估计两个可能光滑测度之间的Wasserstein距离，使其在采样分布下的期望误差为ε加性误差；我们允许采集一个样本的计算成本为O(1)。为此，我们开发了“采样-草图-求解”范式，引入样本的规则笛卡尔网格草图。我们证明（尤其是在α-Hölder光滑分布下），这种方法可以在不增加渐近误差的情况下压缩数据，同时正则化结构，从而实现更快的精确算法。最终，对于定义在(0,1)^d上的0 < α < 1的Hölder光滑分布P,Q，我们能在ε^{-max(2, (d+1+o(1))/(1+α))}时间内将W_2^2(P,Q)近似到ε误差内；当d=2且α>1/2时，达到最优Θ(ε^{-2})；当d=3且α→1时，接近最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor scalability of Wasserstein distance computation with sample size and precision, this work introduces a computational-statistical runtime framework to estimate squared Wasserstein distance up to ε-additive error while allowing O(1) per-sample cost. The method employs a Sample-Sketch-Solve paradigm, using a regular Cartesian grid sketch to compress sample data without increasing asymptotic error and to regularize structure for faster exact algorithms. Experimental results demonstrate that for α-Hölder smooth distributions on (0,1)^d, the runtime scales as ε^{-max(2, (d+1+o(1))/(1+α))}, achieving optimal Θ(ε^{-2}) for α>1/2 in d=2 and nearly optimal behavior as α→1 in d=3.
为解决 Wasserstein 距离计算在样本规模和精度要求下效率低下的问题，本文提出一种计算-统计运行时框架，目标是在 ε 加性误差内估计平方 Wasserstein 距离。方法采用 Sample-Sketch-Solve 框架，通过规则的笛卡尔网格草图压缩样本数据，同时保持渐近精度并加速精确算法，尤其在 α-Hölder 平滑分布下效果显著。实验结果表明，运行时间随 ε 为 ε^(-max(2, (d+1+o(1))/(1+α)))，当 α>1/2 且 d=2 时达到最优 Θ(ε^(-2))，当 α→1 且 d=3 时近似最优。
VERA-MH: Validation of Ethical and Responsible AI in Mental Health
Authors: Luca Belli, Kate H. Bentley, Josh Gieringer, Emily Van Ark, Nilu Zhao, Pradip Thachile, Matt Hawrilenko, Millard Brown, Adam M. Chekroud
First: 2026-05-13T10:31:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:10:36+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chatbot usage has increased, including in fields for which they were never developed for--notably mental health support. To that end, we introduce Validations of Ethical and Responsible AI in Mental Health (VERA-MH), a novel clinically-validated evaluation for safety of chatbots in the context of mental health support. The first iteration of VERA-MH focuses on Suicidal Ideation (SI) risks, by assessing how well chatbots can responds to users that might be in crisis. VERA-MH is comprised of three steps: conversation simulation, conversation judging and model rating. First, to simulate conversations with the chatbot under evaluation, another chatbot is tasked with role-playing users based on specific personas. Such user personas have been developed under clinical guidance, to make sure that, among others, multiple risk factors, demographic characteristics and disclosure factors were represented. In the judging step, a second support model is used as an LLM-as-a-Judge, together with a clinically-developed rubric. The rubric is structured as a flow, with a single Yes/No question asked each time, to improve answers' consistency and highlight models' failure modes. In the last stage, results of each conversation are aggregated to present the final evaluation of the chatbot. Together with the framework, we present the result of the evaluations for four leading LLM providers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VERA-MH：心理健康领域伦理与负责任AI的验证
聊天机器人使用量增加，包括在原本未设计用于的领域——尤其是心理健康支持。为此，我们引入了心理健康领域伦理与负责任AI的验证（VERA-MH），这是一种针对心理健康支持场景下聊天机器人安全性的新型临床验证评估方法。VERA-MH的第一版聚焦于自杀意念（SI）风险，通过评估聊天机器人对可能处于危机状态的用户如何回应。VERA-MH包含三个步骤：对话模拟、对话评判和模型评分。首先，通过另一聊天机器人扮演基于特定人设的用户，模拟与待评估聊天机器人的对话。这些用户人设在临床指导下开发，确保涵盖多种风险因素、人口统计特征和披露因素。在评判阶段，使用第二个支持模型作为“大语言模型裁判”，结合临床开发的评分标准。该评分标准以流程形式构建，每次仅提出一个“是/否”问题，以提高答案一致性并突出模型的失效模式。最后，将每场对话的结果汇总，形成聊天机器人的最终评估。同时，我们还提供了对四大主流大语言模型提供商的评估结果。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the growing use of chatbots in mental health contexts where they were not originally designed, this study introduces VERA-MH, a clinically validated framework to evaluate chatbot safety in handling suicidal ideation risks. The method involves three stages: simulating conversations using a second chatbot acting as a user with clinically guided personas, judging responses using an LLM-as-a-Judge with a structured yes/no rubric, and aggregating results to produce a final model rating. Experimental results evaluated four major LLM providers, revealing significant variation in their ability to appropriately respond to crisis scenarios, with some models demonstrating dangerous failure modes such as minimizing risk or providing harmful advice.
鉴于聊天机器人在心理健康领域（如自杀意念风险）的广泛应用，研究者构建了VERA-MH框架，用于评估聊天机器人在该场景下的安全性。该方法包含三阶段：使用基于临床指导的用户角色模拟对话、由第二语言模型作为裁判依据结构化临床评分表（每次仅问是/否问题以提高一致性）进行判断，最后汇总评分得出最终评价。对四大主流大模型的实验结果显示，其表现差异显著，部分模型在模拟危机场景下易出现误判或不当回应。
Using Aristotle API for AI-Assisted Theorem Proving in Lean 4: A Formalisation Case Study of the Grasshopper Problem
Authors: Gabriel Rongyang Lau
First: 2026-05-19T17:08:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:08:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI-assisted theorem proving can now generate substantial Lean developments for olympiad-level mathematics, but the evidential status of such developments depends on which declarations are actually verified. This paper reports a Lean 4 formalization case study of an Aristotle API proof attempt for the Grasshopper problem, originally posed as IMO 2009 Problem 6. The generated artifact states a generalized Lean version of the theorem, contains four verified helper lemmas for local components of a maximality and adjacent-swap exchange strategy, and leaves the main theorem grasshopper closed directly by one unresolved sorry. The verified components establish that the final partial sum equals the total sum, that an adjacent transposition can affect only the relevant intermediate partial sum, that the changed partial sum has the expected form, and that maximality at a position admitting an adjacent successor swap forces a corresponding forbidden-set membership fact. The Aristotle output summary identifies the intended remaining mathematical step as the global counting step needed to show that these membership facts produce at least n distinct forbidden values, contradicting the cardinality assumption |M| < n; the Lean source itself does not reduce the main theorem to a separately encoded counting lemma. This case study gives an inspectable example of a central limitation in AI-assisted formalization, namely that local proof search can succeed while the global combinatorial bookkeeping required for a theorem remains unresolved. The paper contributes a reproducible Lean artifact and a precise analysis of its verified and unverified proof content.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用亚里士多德API在Lean 4中进行AI辅助定理证明：蚱蜢问题的形式化案例研究
AI辅助定理证明如今能够为奥林匹克级别数学生成大量Lean开发内容，但此类开发的证据地位取决于哪些声明实际被验证。本文报告了对IMO 2009第6题——蚱蜢问题——的Lean 4形式化案例研究，该问题由亚里士多德API尝试证明。生成的成果陈述了一个通用的Lean版本定理，包含四个已验证的辅助引理，用于局部最大性与相邻交换策略组件，而主定理则直接由一个未解决的sorry关闭。已验证部分证明了最终部分和等于总和、相邻置换仅影响相关中间部分和、改变的部分和具有预期形式、以及在允许相邻后继交换的位置达到最大性时会强制产生相应的禁止集成员关系。亚里士多德输出摘要指出，剩余的数学步骤是全局计数步骤，用于证明这些成员关系产生至少n个不同禁止值，与| M | < n的基数假设矛盾；Lean源码本身并未将主定理归约到独立编码的计数引理。本案例研究提供了一个可检查的AI辅助形式化核心局限性实例，即局部证明搜索可成功，而定理所需的全局组合记账仍无法解决。本文贡献了一个可复现的Lean成果及对其验证与未验证证明内容的精确分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess the reliability of AI-generated proofs in formal theorem provers, this study examines an Aristotle API-generated proof attempt for the Grasshopper problem from IMO 2009 in Lean 4. The method involves formalizing the AI’s output in Lean, verifying its components, and analyzing where the proof stalls. Experimental results show that while the AI successfully proved four auxiliary lemmas supporting a maximality and adjacent-swap strategy, the main theorem remains unproven due to an unresolved 'sorry'—specifically, a missing global counting argument needed to show that forbidden-set memberships contradict cardinality assumptions. The study highlights a key limitation: AI can handle local proof steps effectively but often fails to manage global combinatorial reasoning required for full theorem closure.
为评估AI辅助证明在形式化定理证明器中的可靠性，本研究使用Aristotle API在Lean 4中对“蚱蜢问题”（IMO 2009第6题）进行形式化验证。方法是将AI生成的证明内容转化为Lean代码并验证其组成部分。实验结果表明，AI成功生成了四个可验证的辅助引理，支持最大性与相邻交换策略，但主定理因缺少全局计数论证（需证明禁止集合成员事实与基数假设矛盾）而未被证明，仍留有'unresolved sorry'。研究揭示了AI辅助形式化的一个核心局限：局部证明步骤可被验证，而全局组合推理仍无法解决。
Toto 2.0: Time Series Forecasting Enters the Scaling Era
Authors: Emaad Khwaja, Chris Lettieri, Gerald Woo, Eden Belouadah, Marc Cenac, Guillaume Jarry, Enguerrand Paquin, Xunyi Zhao, Viktoriya Zhukov, Othmane Abou-Amal, Chenghao Liu, Ameet Talwalkar, David Asker
First: 2026-05-19T17:08:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:08:24+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/DataDog/toto Weights: https://huggingface.co/collections/Datadog/toto-20
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We show that time series foundation models scale: a single training recipe produces reliable forecast-quality improvements from 4M to 2.5B parameters. We release Toto 2.0, a family of five open-weights forecasting models trained under this recipe. The Toto 2.0 family sets a new state of the art on three forecasting benchmarks: BOOM, our observability benchmark; GIFT-Eval, the standard general-purpose benchmark; and the recent contamination-resistant TIME benchmark. This report describes our experimental results and details the design decisions behind Toto 2.0: its architecture and training recipe, training data, and the u-muP hyperparameter transfer pipeline. All five base checkpoints are released under Apache 2.0.
