Motivated by the need to demonstrate scalable performance in time series forecasting, the authors introduce Toto 2.0, a family of five open-source forecasting models trained under a unified recipe that consistently improves forecast quality as model size scales from 4 million to 2.5 billion parameters. The models are trained using a specific architecture and hyperparameter transfer pipeline (u-muP) and evaluated across three benchmarks—BOOM, GIFT-Eval, and TIME—where Toto 2.0 achieves state-of-the-art results on all. The study validates that scaling model size with consistent training methodology yields reliable performance gains across diverse forecasting tasks.
为有效提升时间序列预测模型的规模能力，作者提出了Toto 2.0，这是一个包含五个开源权重模型的系列，采用统一训练配方，在参数规模从4M扩展至2.5B时持续提升预测质量。该方法结合了精心设计的架构、训练数据和u-muP超参数迁移管道，以实现高效扩展。实验结果表明，Toto 2.0在BOOM、GIFT-Eval和TIME三个基准测试中均达到当前最优性能，全面超越先前模型在各类预测任务中的表现。
Stochastic Penalty-Barrier Methods for Constrained Machine Learning
Authors: Adam Bosák, Andrii Kliachkin, Jana Lepšová, Gilles Bareilles, Jakub Mareček
First: 2026-05-18T16:26:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T17:03:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Constrained machine learning enables fairness-aware training, physics-informed neural networks, and integration of symbolic domain knowledge into statistical models. Despite its practical importance, no general method exists for the non-convex, non-smooth, stochastic setting that arises naturally in deep learning. We propose the Stochastic Penalty-Barrier Method (SPBM), which extends classical penalty and barrier methods to this setting via exponential dual averaging, a stabilized penalty schedule, and the Moreau envelope to handle non-smoothness. Experiments across multiple settings show that SPBM matches or outperforms existing constrained optimization baselines while incurring only linear runtime overhead compared to unconstrained Adam for up to 10,000 constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于约束机器学习的随机惩罚-障碍方法
约束机器学习可实现公平性感知训练、物理信息神经网络，并将符号领域知识整合进统计模型。尽管其具有重要实践价值，但目前尚无适用于深度学习中自然出现的非凸、非光滑、随机场景的通用方法。我们提出随机惩罚-障碍方法（SPBM），通过指数对偶平均、稳定惩罚调度和莫雷包络，将经典惩罚与障碍方法扩展至该场景。多场景实验表明，SPBM在性能上可媲美或超越现有约束优化基线方法，且相比无约束Adam算法，其运行时间开销仅线性增长，适用于最多10,000个约束条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to incorporate constraints into machine learning for fairness, physics, and symbolic knowledge, the paper introduces the Stochastic Penalty-Barrier Method (SPBM) to handle non-convex, non-smooth, and stochastic optimization problems common in deep learning. SPBM adapts classical penalty and barrier techniques using exponential dual averaging, a stabilized penalty schedule, and the Moreau envelope to manage non-smoothness. Experimental results across multiple scenarios demonstrate that SPBM achieves performance comparable or superior to existing constrained optimization methods while adding only linear runtime overhead relative to unconstrained Adam, even when handling up to 10,000 constraints.
为实现公平性、物理约束和符号知识融入机器学习，本文提出随机惩罚-障碍法（SPBM），用于解决深度学习中常见的非凸、非光滑、随机优化问题。SPBM通过指数对偶平均、稳定惩罚调度和莫雷包络（Moreau envelope）扩展经典惩罚-障碍方法以处理非光滑性。实验表明，SPBM在多个场景下性能与现有约束优化方法相当或更优，且相比无约束Adam仅增加线性运行开销，即使在处理多达10,000个约束时仍保持高效。
BuildArena: A Physics-Aligned Interactive Benchmark of LLMs for Engineering Construction
Authors: Tian Xia, Tianrun Gao, Wenhao Deng, Long Wei, Xiaowei Qian, Chenglei Yu, Tailin Wu
First: 2025-10-18T16:13:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:59:34+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Engineering construction automation aims to transform natural language specifications into physically viable structures, requiring complex integrated reasoning under strict physical constraints. While modern LLMs possess broad knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities that make them promising candidates for this domain, their construction competencies remain largely unevaluated. To address this gap, we introduce BuildArena, the first physics-aligned interactive benchmark designed for language-driven engineering construction. It takes a first step towards engineering automation using LLMs. Technically, it contributes to the community in two aspects:(1) an extendable task design strategy spanning static and dynamic mechanics across multiple difficulty tiers; (2) a 3D Spatial Geometric Computation Library for supporting construction based on language instructions. On nine frontier LLMs, BuildArena comprehensively evaluates their capabilities for language-driven and physics-grounded construction automation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BuildArena：面向工程建造的物理对齐LLM交互基准
工程建造自动化旨在将自然语言规范转化为物理可行的结构，需在严格物理约束下进行复杂综合推理。尽管现代大语言模型具备广泛知识和强大推理能力，使其成为该领域的有力候选者，但其建造能力尚未得到充分评估。为填补这一空白，我们提出BuildArena——首个面向语言驱动工程建造的物理对齐交互基准，迈出利用LLM实现工程自动化的第一步。技术上，它在两个方面贡献社区：(1) 覆盖静态与动态力学、多难度层级的可扩展任务设计策略；(2) 支持基于语言指令的建造的3D空间几何计算库。在九个前沿LLM上，BuildArena全面评估其语言驱动与物理基础的建造自动化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate large language models' ability to translate natural language into physically viable engineering structures under strict constraints, the authors introduce BuildArena, the first interactive benchmark aligned with physics principles for construction tasks. The method combines an extendable task design covering static and dynamic mechanics across difficulty levels with a 3D spatial geometric computation library to enable language-driven construction. Experimental results on nine frontier LLMs reveal significant performance disparities, with only a few models demonstrating basic construction competence, highlighting the current gap in physics-grounded reasoning capabilities among LLMs.
为评估大语言模型将自然语言转化为符合物理约束的工程结构的能力，作者提出了BuildArena，这是首个面向工程建造任务的物理对齐交互式基准。该方法结合了覆盖静态与动态力学、多难度层级的可扩展任务设计，以及支持语言指令驱动建造的3D空间几何计算库。在九个前沿大语言模型上的实验表明，模型在处理复杂空间推理和物理约束时表现不佳，揭示了当前大语言模型在工程自动化中的能力局限。
SetCon: Towards Open-Ended Referring Segmentation via Set-Level Concept Prediction
Authors: Zhixiong Zhang, Yizhuo Li, Shuangrui Ding, Yuhang Zang, Shengyuan Ding, Long Xing, Yibin Wang, Qiaosheng Zhang, Jiaqi Wang
First: 2026-05-19T16:59:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:59:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Referring segmentation grounds natural-language queries to pixel-level masks, but extending it to complex scenarios with multiple instances, cross-category groups, or open-ended target sets remains challenging. Previous Large Vision Language Model (LVLM)-based methods represent referred targets with one or more special tokens sequentially, treating multiple targets as separate outputs rather than a coherent set and offering little incentive to capture set-level properties such as completeness and mutual exclusivity. We reformulate open-ended referring segmentation as explicit set-level concept prediction and propose Set-Concept Segmentation (SetCon), which uses LVLM-generated natural-language concepts, instead of segmentation-specific tokens, as semantic conditions for joint mask-set decoding. A hierarchical semantic decomposition first predicts a shared set-level concept defining the target scope and then refines it into fine-grained concept groups aligned with target subsets. To support this, a two-stage annotation pipeline augments existing reasoning segmentation datasets with hierarchical semantic supervision (236k samples, 784k concept phrases). SetCon achieves state-of-the-art results on image benchmarks (+3.3 gIoU on gRefCOCO, +12.1 gIoU on MUSE), with margins that grow as the number of referred targets increases. The concept interface also transfers to video under a detect-and-track setting, yielding new state-of-the-art results on seven referring video benchmarks, including +10.9 J&F on MeViS and +12.4 J&F on Ref-SeCVOS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SetCon：通过集合级概念预测实现开放端指代分割
指代分割将自然语言查询映射到像素级掩码，但在处理多实例、跨类别组或开放目标集合等复杂场景时仍具挑战性。此前基于大型视觉语言模型（LVLM）的方法使用一个或多个特殊标记顺序表示指代目标，将多个目标视为独立输出而非统一集合，且缺乏捕捉集合级属性（如完整性与互斥性）的激励。我们重新定义开放端指代分割为显式集合级概念预测，并提出集合概念分割（SetCon），其利用LVLM生成的自然语言概念（而非分割专用标记）作为联合掩码集解码的语义条件。分层语义分解首先预测定义目标范围的共享集合级概念，再将其细化为与目标子集对齐的细粒度概念组。为此，我们设计两阶段标注流程，为现有推理分割数据集补充分层语义监督（23.6万样本，78.4万概念短语）。SetCon在图像基准上取得最先进结果（gRefCOCO提升3.3 gIoU，MUSE提升12.1 gIoU），且随指代目标数量增加，性能优势扩大。该概念接口在检测与跟踪设置下可迁移至视频，于七个指代视频基准上取得新SOTA结果，包括MeViS提升10.9 J&F、Ref-SeCVOS提升12.4 J&F。
Summary / 总结
Referring segmentation grounds natural-language queries to pixel-level masks, but extending it to complex scenarios with multiple instances, cross-category groups, or open-ended target sets remains challenging.
为了解决现有基于大视觉语言模型的指代分割方法在处理多目标、跨类别组或开放目标集合时缺乏集合级语义建模的问题，本文提出SetCon框架，将开放指代分割重新定义为集合级概念预测。该方法利用LVLM生成的自然语言概念作为联合分割掩码解码的语义条件，采用分层语义分解：首先预测一个定义目标范围的共享集合级概念，再细化为与目标子集对齐的细粒度概念组。通过两阶段标注流程生成23.6万样本和78.4万概念短语作为监督信号，SetCon在图像基准上取得最先进性能（如gRefCOCO上+3.3 gIoU，MUSE上+12.1 gIoU），且性能随目标数量增加而提升，并成功迁移到视频场景，在七个指代视频基准上刷新SOTA（如MeViS上+10.9 J&F，Ref-SeCVOS上+12.4 J&F）。
k-Inductive Neural Barrier Certificates for Unknown Nonlinear Dynamics
Authors: Ben Wooding, Hongchao Zhang, Taylor T. Johnson, Abolfazl Lavaei
First: 2026-05-19T16:58:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:58:37+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures, 3rd International Conference on Neuro-Symbolic Systems (NeuS)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While conventional (k=1) discrete-time barrier certificate conditions impose strict safety constraints by requiring the function to be non-increasing at every step, k-inductive barrier certificates relax this by allowing a temporary increase -- up to k-1 times, each within a threshold $ε$ -- while maintaining overall safety, and improving flexibility. This paper leverages neural networks and constructs k-inductive neural barrier certificates (k-NBCs) for (partially) unknown nonlinear systems. While neural networks offer scalability in the design process, they lack formal guarantees, requiring additional approaches such as counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS) with satisfiability modulo theories (SMT) for verification. However, the CEGIS-SMT framework requires knowledge of system dynamics, which is unavailable in practical settings. To address this, we leverage the generalization of the Willems et al.'s fundamental lemma, using a single state trajectory, to construct a data-driven representation of (partially) unknown models for SMT verification without sacrificing accuracy. Additionally, CEGIS-SMT further removes the constraint of restricting barrier certificates to specific function classes, such as sum-of-squares, enabling greater flexibility in their design. We validate our approach on three nonlinear case studies with (partially) unknown dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于未知非线性动态系统的k-归纳神经屏障证书
虽然传统（k=1）离散时间屏障证书条件通过要求函数在每一步均非递增来施加严格的安全部署，k-归纳屏障证书则通过允许函数在阈值ε内最多临时增加k-1次，同时保持整体安全性，从而提升灵活性。本文利用神经网络构建k-归纳神经屏障证书（k-NBCs），适用于（部分）未知非线性系统。尽管神经网络在设计过程中具有可扩展性，但缺乏形式化保证，需结合如基于反例引导的归纳合成（CEGIS）与可满足性模理论（SMT）的验证方法。然而，CEGIS-SMT框架需要已知系统动态，这在实际场景中不可用。为此，我们利用Willems等人基本引理的泛化，仅用单条状态轨迹构建（部分）未知模型的数据驱动表示，以支持SMT验证而不牺牲精度。此外，CEGIS-SMT进一步消除了对屏障证书函数类（如平方和）的限制，使其设计更具灵活性。我们在三个具有（部分）未知动态的非线性案例研究中验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rigidity of traditional k=1 barrier certificates that demand strict non-increasing behavior at every step, this work introduces k-inductive neural barrier certificates (k-NBCs) to allow temporary, bounded increases in barrier values up to k-1 times per trajectory, enhancing flexibility while preserving safety. The method combines neural networks for scalable barrier design with a data-driven model constructed via the generalization of Willems’ lemma using a single state trajectory, enabling SMT-based verification without requiring explicit system dynamics. This approach also integrates counterexample-guided inductive synthesis (CEGIS) to avoid restricting barrier functions to specific classes like sum-of-squares. Experimental results on three nonlinear systems with partially unknown dynamics demonstrate that k-NBCs successfully verify safety under relaxed conditions while maintaining accuracy and scalability.
为克服传统k=1屏障证书在每一步都要求函数严格非增所带来的刚性约束，本文提出k-归纳神经屏障证书（k-NBC），允许屏障值在每次轨迹中最多临时增加k-1次，每次增加幅度不超过阈值ε，从而在保持安全的前提下提升灵活性。该方法结合神经网络实现可扩展的屏障设计，并利用Willems引理的推广，仅通过单条状态轨迹构建数据驱动的系统模型，支持无需显式动力学的SMT验证。同时，采用反例引导归纳合成（CEGIS）框架，避免对屏障函数形式（如平方和）的限制。在三个具有部分未知动力学的非线性系统实验中，k-NBC成功验证了安全约束，且在灵活性与准确性之间取得良好平衡。
Beyond Isotropy in JEPAs: Hamiltonian Geometry and Symplectic Prediction
Authors: Robert Jenkinson Alvarez
First: 2026-05-19T16:57:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:57:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
JEPAs often regularize one-view embeddings toward an isotropic Gaussian, implicitly baking Euclidean symmetry into the representation. We show that this is not merely a benign default. For a known structured downstream geometry $H\succ0$, the minimax and maximum-entropy covariance under a Hamiltonian energy budget is $(c/d)H^{-1}$, and Euclidean isotropy incurs a closed-form price of isotropy. More importantly, when the downstream geometry is unknown, no geometry-independent fixed marginal target is canonical: every fixed covariance shape can be maximally misaligned for some structured geometry. We further show that even oracle one-view marginals do not identify the JEPA view-to-view predictive coupling. These results suggest that the structural bias in JEPAs should enter the cross-view coupling rather than a fixed encoder marginal. We instantiate this principle with \textbf{HamJEPA}, which encodes each view as a phase-space state $(q,p)$ and predicts view-to-view transitions with a learned Hamiltonian leapfrog map, while non-isotropic scale and spectral floors prevent collapse. In a deliberately headless token protocol, HamJEPA improves over SIGReg on CIFAR-100 by $+4.89$ kNN@20 and $+3.52$ linear-probe points at 30 epochs, and by $+6.45$ kNN@20 and $+10.64$ linear-probe points at 80 epochs, while a matched MLP predictor ablation shows that the symplectic coupling is the ingredient driving the neighborhood-geometry gain. On ImageNet-100, HamJEPA-$q$ improves by $+4.82$ kNN@20 and $+7.52$ linear-probe points at 45 epochs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越各向同性：JEPAs中的哈密顿几何与辛预测
JEPAs通常将单视图嵌入正则化为各向同性高斯分布，隐式地将欧几里得对称性嵌入表示中。我们证明，这并非仅仅是无害的默认设置。对于已知的结构化下游几何 $H\succ0$，在哈密顿能量预算下，最小最大和最大熵协方差为 $(c/d)H^{-1}$，而欧几里得各向同性会带来一个闭式的价格。更重要的是，当下游几何未知时，不存在与几何无关的固定边际目标：每种固定协方差形状都可能对某些结构化几何产生最大偏差。我们进一步表明，即使拥有oracle单视图边际，也无法识别JEPA视图间的预测耦合。这些结果表明，JEPAs中的结构偏差应体现在跨视图耦合中，而非固定的编码器边际。我们通过\textbf{HamJEPA} 实现这一原则：将每个视图编码为相空间状态 $(q,p)$，并用学习到的哈密顿leapfrog映射预测视图间转换，同时非各向同性的尺度和频谱下限防止坍塌。在刻意无头的token协议中，HamJEPA在CIFAR-100上优于SIGReg：在30个epoch时，kNN@20提升+4.89，线性探测提升+3.52；在80个epoch时，kNN@20提升+6.45，线性探测提升+10.64。匹配MLP预测器的消融实验表明，辛耦合是驱动邻域几何增益的关键因素。在ImageNet-100上，HamJEPA-$q$在45个epoch时kNN@20提升+4.82，线性探测提升+7.52。
Summary / 总结
JEPAs often regularize one-view embeddings toward an isotropic Gaussian, implicitly baking Euclidean symmetry into the representation.
该研究指出，JEPAs中隐含的欧氏对称性迫使单视图嵌入趋向各向同性高斯分布，这种结构偏差在下游任务具有非欧几里得几何时是次优的。论文推导出在已知哈密顿几何H≻0下，最优协方差为(c/d)H⁻¹，而各向同性会带来明确代价；当几何未知时，不存在普适的固定边际目标，即使理想边际也无法捕捉视图间耦合。作者提出HamJEPA，将视图编码为相空间状态(q,p)，通过学习的哈密顿勒让德步长预测视图间转换，并引入非各向同性缩放与频谱下限防止坍缩。实验表明HamJEPA在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著优于SIGReg，在kNN@20和线性探测准确率上均有提升，且消融实验验证了辛耦合是几何感知性能的关键。
Optimal Representation Size: High-Dimensional Analysis of Pretraining and Linear Probing
Authors: Valentina Njaradi, Clémentine Dominé, Rachel Swanson, Marco Mondelli, Andrew Saxe
First: 2026-05-19T16:56:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:56:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning to generalise from limited data is a fundamental challenge for both artificial and biological systems. A common strategy is to extract reusable structure from abundant unlabelled data, enabling efficient adaptation to new tasks from limited labelled data. This two-stage paradigm is now standard in modern training pipelines, where pretraining is followed by fine-tuning or linear probing. We provide an analytical model of this process: structure extraction is formalized as principal component analysis on unlabelled data, and downstream learning as linear regression on a separate labelled dataset. In the high-dimensional regime, we derive exact expressions for training and generalisation error showcasing their dependence on representation dimensionality, unlabelled and labelled sample sizes, and task alignment. Our results show that pretrained representations strongly influence downstream generalisation, and we characterize the optimal representation size as a function of task parameters: with abundant pretraining data but scarce downstream data, maximally compressed representations are optimal, whereas with limited pretraining data, higher-dimensional representations generalise better. Furthermore, we establish an exact trade-off between pretraining and supervision, quantifying how much unlabelled data is required to replace a single labelled sample. Beyond our idealised model, we observe similar phenomenology in autoencoders and pretrained LLMs. Altogether, we highlight that optimising representation size is critical, giving conditions for when compression during pretraining improves generalisation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：最优表示尺寸：预训练与线性探针的高维分析
从有限数据中学习泛化是人工和生物系统的基本挑战。一种常见策略是从大量无标签数据中提取可复用结构，从而高效适应新任务所需的少量标签数据。这种两阶段范式现已成为现代训练流程的标准，即先进行预训练，再进行微调或线性探针。我们为此过程建立了分析模型：结构提取形式化为无标签数据的主成分分析，下游学习则为独立标签数据集上的线性回归。在高维情形下，我们推导出训练误差与泛化误差的精确表达式，揭示其对表示维度、无标签与标签样本量及任务对齐度的依赖关系。结果表明，预训练表示显著影响下游泛化能力，我们将其最优表示尺寸表征为任务参数的函数：当预训练数据充足而下游数据稀缺时，最大压缩表示最优；而当预训练数据有限时，更高维表示泛化能力更强。此外，我们建立了预训练与监督之间的精确权衡关系，量化了无标签数据替代单个标签样本所需量。在超越理想化模型的场景中，我们观察到类似现象存在于自编码器和预训练大语言模型中。总体而言，我们强调优化表示尺寸至关重要，并给出了压缩预训练如何提升泛化的条件。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how representation size affects generalization in two-stage learning pipelines, this study models pretraining as principal component analysis on unlabeled data and downstream learning as linear regression on labeled data. In high dimensions, it derives exact error expressions showing that optimal representation size depends on data availability and task alignment: compressed representations are best when pretraining data is abundant but downstream data is scarce, while higher dimensions improve generalization when pretraining is limited. The model also quantifies the trade-off between unlabeled and labeled data, revealing that a large amount of unlabeled data can substitute a single labeled sample. Experimental validation in autoencoders and pretrained LLMs confirms similar behavior, underscoring that representation size optimization is critical for generalization.
为理解表示尺寸如何影响两阶段学习中的泛化能力，该研究将预训练建模为无标签数据的主成分分析，下游学习建模为标签数据上的线性回归。在高维情况下，推导出精确的误差表达式，表明最优表示尺寸取决于无标签和有标签样本量及任务对齐程度：当预训练数据充足而下游数据稀缺时，压缩表示最优；当预训练数据有限时，更高维度表示泛化更好。模型还量化了无标签与有标签数据之间的权衡，揭示单个标签样本可被特定量无标签数据替代。在自编码器和预训练大语言模型中观察到类似现象，强调优化表示尺寸对泛化能力至关重要。
Draft Less, Retrieve More: Hybrid Tree Construction for Speculative Decoding
Authors: Yuhao Shen, Tianyu Liu, Xinyi Hu, Quan Kong, Baolin Zhang, Jun Dai, Jun Zhang, Shuang Ge, Lei Chen, Yue Li, Mingcheng Wan, Cong Wang
First: 2026-05-19T16:55:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:55:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates large language model inference by leveraging a draft-then-verify paradigm. To maximize the acceptance rate, recent methods construct expansive draft trees, which unfortunately incur severe VRAM bandwidth and computational overheads that bottleneck end-to-end speedups. While dynamic-depth pruning can reduce this latency by removing marginal branches, it also discards potentially valid candidates, preventing the acceptance rate from reaching the upper bound of dense trees. In this paper, we identify a critical opportunity in resource allocation: the transition from dense to pruned drafting frees up significant computational budget. To break this Pareto tradeoff, we introduce Graft, a compensation framework that couples pruning and retrieval as mutually reinforcing operations. Pruning supplies sufficient budget for retrieval, while retrieval compensates for pruning-induced coverage loss and recovers accepted length. By employing a sequential `prune-then-graft' mechanism, Graft attaches highly predictive retrieved tokens into positions opened by pruning, filling the topological gaps with near-zero overhead. Graft is entirely training-free and lossless. Comprehensive evaluations show that Graft establishes a new Pareto frontier across practical deployment settings, including short-context generation, long-context generation, and large-scale models. On short-context benchmarks, it achieves up to 5.41$\times$ speedup and improves average speedup over EAGLE-3 by up to 21.8% on the large-scale Qwen3-235B. We also provide a preliminary exploration of applying Graft to the DFlash-style block drafting paradigm, offering initial evidence and insights for extending grafting beyond autoregressive draft trees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：少写多取：用于推测解码的混合树构建
推测解码（SD）通过先草稿后验证的范式加速大语言模型推理。为最大化接受率，近期方法构建扩展的草稿树，但不幸的是，这会带来严重的VRAM带宽和计算开销，成为端到端加速的瓶颈。虽然动态深度剪枝可通过移除边缘分支减少延迟，但也丢弃了潜在有效候选，使接受率无法达到密集树的上限。本文识别出资源分配中的关键机会：从密集草稿过渡到剪枝草稿可释放大量计算预算。为打破这一帕累托权衡，我们提出Graft，一个将剪枝与检索耦合为相互强化操作的补偿框架。剪枝为检索提供充足预算，而检索补偿剪枝导致的覆盖损失并恢复被接受的长度。通过采用顺序的“剪枝-再嫁接”机制，Graft将高度预测性的检索词附加到剪枝打开的位置，以近零开销填补拓扑空缺。Graft完全无需训练且无损。全面评估表明，Graft在实际部署场景（包括短上下文生成、长上下文生成和大模型）中建立了新的帕累托前沿。在短上下文基准上，其速度提升最高达5.41倍，在大规模Qwen3-235B上相比EAGLE-3平均速度提升最高达21.8%。我们还初步探索了将Graft应用于DFlash风格块草稿范式，为将嫁接扩展至自回归草稿树之外提供了初步证据和见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tradeoff between draft tree density and computational efficiency in speculative decoding, this paper introduces Graft, a training-free, lossless framework that couples pruning and retrieval to break the Pareto barrier. Graft first prunes marginal branches to free up computational budget, then strategically retrieves and inserts highly predictive tokens into pruned positions with near-zero overhead, compensating for coverage loss and restoring accepted sequence length. Evaluated across short- and long-context generation and large-scale models, Graft achieves up to 5.41x speedup on Qwen3-235B and improves average speedup over EAGLE-3 by up to 21.8%, establishing a new performance frontier without sacrificing acceptance rate.
为解决投机解码中密集树结构与计算效率之间的权衡，论文提出Graft框架，通过先剪枝后检索的方式，在降低计算开销的同时恢复性能损失。该框架按顺序剪除低价值分支，并将高预测性的检索词插入剪枝后留下的空位，从而在不牺牲质量且无需训练的前提下保持高接受率。在短上下文、长上下文及大模型场景下，Graft在Qwen3-235B上实现最高5.41倍加速，相较EAGLE-3平均加速提升21.8%，确立了新的帕累托前沿。
Robots that learn to evaluate models of collective behavior
Authors: Mathis Hocke, Andreas Gerken, David Bierbach, Jens Krause, Tim Landgraf
First: 2026-04-08T17:11:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:53:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Understanding and modeling animal behavior is essential for studying collective motion, decision-making, and bio-inspired robotics. Yet, evaluating the accuracy of behavioral models still often relies on offline comparisons to static trajectory statistics. Here we introduce a reinforcement-learning-based framework that uses a biomimetic robotic fish (RoboFish) to evaluate computational models of live fish behavior through closed-loop interaction. We trained policies in simulation using four distinct fish models-a simple constant-follow baseline, two rule-based models, and a biologically grounded convolutional neural network model-and transferred these policies to the real RoboFish setup, where they interacted with live fish. Policies were trained to guide a simulated fish to goal locations, enabling us to quantify how the response of real fish differs from the simulated fish's response. We evaluate the fish models by quantifying the sim-to-real gaps, defined as the Wasserstein distance between simulated and real distributions of behavioral metrics such as goal-reaching performance, inter-individual distances, wall interactions, and alignment. The neural network-based fish model exhibited the smallest gap across goal-reaching performance and most other metrics, indicating higher behavioral fidelity than conventional rule-based models under this benchmark. More importantly, this separation shows that the proposed evaluation can quantitatively distinguish candidate models under matched closed-loop conditions. Our work demonstrates how learning-based robotic experiments can uncover deficiencies in behavioral models and provides a general framework for evaluating animal behavior models through embodied interaction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：能够学习评估群体行为模型的机器人
理解与建模动物行为对于研究集体运动、决策制定和仿生机器人至关重要。然而，评估行为模型的准确性仍常依赖于离线对比静态轨迹统计量。本文提出一种基于强化学习的框架，利用仿生机器鱼（RoboFish）通过闭环交互评估活鱼行为的计算模型。我们在仿真环境中训练了四种不同鱼模型的策略——简单常量跟随基线模型、两个规则模型和一个基于生物机制的卷积神经网络模型，并将这些策略迁移到真实RoboFish装置中，使其与活鱼互动。策略被训练以引导仿真鱼到达目标位置，从而量化真实鱼与仿真鱼响应之间的差异。我们通过量化“仿真到现实差距”（即仿真与真实行为指标分布——如目标到达性能、个体间距离、墙壁交互、对齐度——的Wasserstein距离）来评估鱼模型。神经网络模型在目标到达性能及其他多数指标上表现出最小差距，表明其在该基准下比传统规则模型具有更高的行为保真度。更重要的是，这种差异表明所提出的评估方法能够在匹配的闭环条件下定量区分候选模型。本研究展示了基于学习的机器人实验如何揭示行为模型的缺陷，并提供了一种通过具身交互评估动物行为模型的通用框架。

Motivated by the need to better evaluate behavioral models of animal collectives, this study introduces a reinforcement-learning framework using a biomimetic robotic fish (RoboFish) to interact with live fish in real time. The method trains simulated policies on four fish models—ranging from simple rules to a biologically grounded neural network—and transfers them to the physical robot to measure how real fish respond compared to simulated ones. Experimental results show that the neural network model achieved the smallest sim-to-real gap across multiple behavioral metrics, including goal-reaching and social spacing, indicating superior behavioral fidelity over rule-based models. This approach enables quantitative, closed-loop evaluation of models and highlights the potential of embodied robotic experiments to reveal model shortcomings.
为更准确评估动物群体行为模型，本研究提出一种基于强化学习的框架，利用仿生机器鱼（RoboFish）与活鱼实时互动进行评估。方法在仿真环境中训练四种不同模型（从简单基线到生物基础神经网络）的策略，并将其部署到真实机器鱼上，通过比较真实鱼与仿真鱼的行为响应来量化模型差异。实验结果表明，神经网络模型在多个行为指标（如目标到达率、个体间距等）上表现出最小的仿真-现实差距，优于传统规则模型，证明该方法能定量区分模型优劣，揭示静态评估难以发现的模型缺陷。
Topology-Optimized Pneumatic Soft Actuator: Design and Experimental Validation
Authors: Sumit Mehta, Konstantinos Poulios
First: 2026-05-19T16:52:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:52:49+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper demonstrates the computational design of soft elastomeric pneumatic actuators using nonlinear topology optimization. An existing density- and porohyperelasticity-based topology optimization framework was extended from 2D to 3D and used to generate two manufacturable actuator designs, which were then studied numerically and experimentally. For both designs, the objective was to maximize the bending response for a prescribed actuation pressure under two different allowable strain limits. A key advantage of the employed topology optimization framework is that it can consistently, during the optimization, account for the very large deformations induced upon pressurization. The two optimized 3D designs were fabricated using stereolithography and experimentally tested to validate their performance.

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-performance, manufacturable soft actuators capable of large deformations, this study extends a 2D topology optimization framework based on density and porohyperelasticity to 3D to design pneumatic soft actuators. The method maximizes bending response under fixed pressure while respecting two strain limits, consistently modeling large deformations during optimization. Experimental validation via stereolithography-fabricated prototypes confirmed that both designs achieved significant bending performance, demonstrating the framework’s effectiveness in generating functional, manufacturable soft actuators.
为满足高性能、可制造且能承受大变形的软执行器需求，本研究将基于密度与多孔超弹性理论的二维拓扑优化框架扩展至三维，用于设计气动软执行器。该方法在固定压力下最大化弯曲响应，同时限制两种应变水平，并在优化过程中持续考虑大变形效应。通过立体光刻制造的原型实验验证表明，两个设计均实现了显著的弯曲性能，证明该框架在生成实用、可制造的软执行器方面有效。
Neurosymbolic Learning for Inference-Time Argumentation
Authors: Gabriel Freedman, Adam Dejl, Adam Gould, Mansi, Lihu Chen, Jianqi Jiang, Francesca Toni
First: 2026-05-19T16:49:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:49:29+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Claim verification is an important problem in high-stakes settings, including health and finance. When information underpinning claims is incomplete or conflicting, uncertain answers may be more appropriate than binary true or false classifications. In all cases, faithful explanations of the considerations determining the final verdict are crucial. We introduce inference-time argumentation (ITA), a trainable neurosymbolic framework for ternary claim verification in which a formal argumentation semantics giving the strength of claims is used both (i) to guide LLM training as models learn to generate arguments and assign them base scores (representing intrinsic strengths) and (ii) to compute ternary (true/false/uncertain) predictions from generated, scored arguments. As a result, at training time, argument generation and scoring can be optimised according to the quality of the induced argumentative predictions. Moreover, at inference time, the final prediction is faithful, by construction, to the arguments and scores determining the verdict, rather than being justified by a potentially unfaithful post-hoc reasoning trace as in conventional reasoning models. We finally show that, on two datasets for ternary claim verification, ITA improves upon argumentative baselines and can perform competitively against non-argumentative direct-prediction baselines, while providing verdicts that are computed deterministically from explicit, inspectable argumentative structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理时论证的神经符号学习
主张验证在高风险场景（如健康和金融）中至关重要。当支撑主张的信息不完整或冲突时，不确定的答案可能比简单的真/假二元分类更合适。在所有情况下，对最终判决决定因素的忠实解释都至关重要。我们引入了推理时论证（ITA），这是一种可训练的神经符号框架，用于三元主张验证。该框架采用形式论证语义，用于（i）指导大语言模型训练，使模型学习生成论证并为其分配基础分数（代表内在强度）；（ii）从生成并评分的论证中计算三元（真/假/不确定）预测。因此，在训练时，论证生成和评分可根据诱导的论证性预测质量进行优化。此外，在推理时，最终预测因构造而忠实于决定判决的论证和分数，而非像传统推理模型那样依赖可能不忠实的后置推理轨迹。最后，我们在两个三元主张验证数据集上表明，ITA优于论证性基线，并能与非论证性直接预测基线竞争，同时提供由明确、可检查的论证结构确定性计算出的判决。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for explainable and reliable claim verification in high-stakes domains like health and finance, this work introduces inference-time argumentation (ITA), a neurosymbolic framework that generates and scores arguments during training to guide LLMs toward ternary (true/false/uncertain) predictions. ITA uses formal argumentation semantics to assign intrinsic strengths to generated arguments and then combines them to produce final verdicts, ensuring predictions are inherently faithful to the reasoning process. Experiments on two ternary claim verification datasets show ITA outperforms argumentative baselines and competes with non-argumentative models while offering deterministic, inspectable argument structures for each decision.
为在医疗和金融等高风险领域实现可解释且可靠的主张验证，本文提出推理时论证（ITA）框架，该框架在训练阶段引导大语言模型生成并评分论证，再通过形式论证语义计算三元（真/假/不确定）预测结果。该方法确保最终预测与论证结构一致，而非事后生成的不可靠推理链。实验表明，ITA在两个三元验证数据集上优于现有论证基线，且可与非论证模型竞争，同时提供可确定性检查的论证结构。
MetaEarth-MM: Unified Multimodal Remote Sensing Image Generation with Scene-centered Joint Modeling
Authors: Zhiping Yu, Chenyang Liu, Jinqi Cao, Qinzhe Yang, Siwei Yu, Zhengxia Zou, Zhenwei Shi
First: 2026-05-19T16:47:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:47:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-modal remote sensing images are vital for Earth observation, yet complete paired observations are often scarce in practice. Existing generative methods commonly address this problem through isolated pairwise modality translation, but their versatility and scalability remain limited as the number of modalities and generation tasks increases. Here, we develop a generative foundation model MetaEarth-MM for multi-modal remote sensing imagery, enabling paired joint generation and any-to-any translation across five modalities within a unified model. Recognizing the intrinsic scene consistency underlying multi-modal observations, we introduce a scene-centered joint modeling paradigm in MetaEarth-MM. Unlike previous methods that rely on direct appearance-level cross-modal mapping, our model organizes the generation around the underlying scene content. Specifically, MetaEarth-MM adopts a decoupled architecture that first infers a latent scene representation from available observations, and then generates target modalities conditioned on this intermediate state. To support training, we further construct EarthMM, a large-scale dataset comprising 2.8 million multi-resolution global images with 2.2 million aligned pairs. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MetaEarth-MM not only exhibits strong generative capability and robust generalization across diverse generation tasks, but also supports downstream tasks at both data and representation levels, highlighting its potential as a general foundation model for cross-modal Earth observation. The code and dataset will be available at https://github.com/YZPioneer/MetaEarth-MM.
Summary / 总结
Driven by the scarcity of complete multi-modal remote sensing image pairs in real-world Earth observation, this work introduces MetaEarth-MM, a unified generative foundation model capable of joint generation and any-to-any translation across five modalities. The model employs a scene-centered joint modeling approach, first inferring a latent scene representation from available modalities and then generating target modalities conditioned on this shared scene state, thereby avoiding direct cross-modal appearance mapping. Trained on EarthMM, a large-scale dataset of 2.8 million global images with 2.2 million aligned pairs, MetaEarth-MM demonstrates strong generative performance and broad generalization across diverse generation tasks, while also supporting downstream applications at both data and representation levels.
鉴于实际地球观测中多模态遥感图像配对数据稀缺，本文提出MetaEarth-MM，一个统一的生成式基础模型，支持五种模态间的联合生成与任意模态间翻译。该模型采用以场景为中心的联合建模方法，先从输入模态推断出共享的场景潜在表示，再基于此生成目标模态，避免直接跨模态外观映射。在包含280万张全球多分辨率图像及220万对齐样本的EarthMM数据集上训练后，MetaEarth-MM在多种生成任务中展现出强大的生成能力和广泛泛化性，并支持数据与表征层面的下游任务应用。
Critique-Guided Distillation for Robust Reasoning via Refinement
Authors: Berkcan Kapusuzoglu, Supriyo Chakraborty, Zain Sarwar, Chia-Hsuan Lee, Sambit Sahu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-05-16T18:45:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:46:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Supervised fine-tuning with expert demonstrations often produces models that imitate outputs without internalizing the reasoning processes needed for robust generalization. While critique-based approaches show promise, training models to generate critiques directly, such as Critique Fine-Tuning (CFT), can lead to output-format drift and degradation of general capabilities. We propose Critique-Guided Distillation (CGD), a training framework that decouples critique consumption from critique generation. During fine-tuning, the student is trained to refine flawed responses conditioned on teacher critiques. CGD treats critiques as a \textit{training-time-only} supervision signal, encouraging internalization of error-aware reasoning: critiques guide learning but are absent at inference. Controlled ablations confirm that these reasoning gains are directly driven by the specificity and relevance of the teacher's feedback. Across five model families, CGD consistently outperforms CFT and standard distillation on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, yielding 7\% average improvements and gains of up to +15.0\% on AMC23 and +12.2\% on MATH-500. On challenging competition problems such as AIME24 and AIME25, CGD achieves substantially higher Pass@1 and stronger performance at low Pass@k, indicating improved reasoning quality per sample. Importantly, CGD preserves general instruction-following capabilities where CFT degrades significantly ($-$21.3\% on IFEval). These results position CGD as a practical and compute-efficient intermediate training paradigm for reasoning-centric tasks without introducing architectural inference-time overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过精炼实现批判引导蒸馏以增强鲁棒推理
监督微调结合专家演示常导致模型仅模仿输出而未内化实现鲁棒泛化的推理过程。尽管基于批判的方法前景良好，但直接训练模型生成批判（如批判微调CFT）可能导致输出格式漂移及通用能力退化。我们提出批判引导蒸馏（CGD），一种将批判消费与批判生成解耦的训练框架。微调过程中，学生模型在教师批判指导下学习修正错误响应。CGD将批判视为仅在训练时存在的监督信号，鼓励模型内化错误感知推理：批判引导学习，但推理时不存在。受控消融实验证实，这些推理增益直接源于教师反馈的特异性与相关性。在五个模型家族中，CGD在数学推理基准上持续优于CFT和标准蒸馏，平均提升7%，在AMC23上最高提升+15.0%，在MATH-500上最高提升+12.2%。在AIME24和AIME25等高难度竞赛题中，CGD实现显著更高的Pass@1及低Pass@k下的更强表现，表明每样本推理质量提升。重要的是，CGD保持了通用指令遵循能力，而CFT显著退化（IFEval上下降-21.3%）。这些结果使CGD成为无需引入推理时架构开销的实用且计算高效的推理中心任务中间训练范式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that supervised fine-tuning often produces models that mimic outputs without internalizing reasoning, the paper introduces Critique-Guided Distillation (CGD), a framework that trains a student model to refine flawed responses based on teacher critiques during fine-tuning, while excluding critiques at inference. CGD treats critiques as a training-time-only signal to encourage error-aware reasoning without output-format drift or degradation of general capabilities. Experiments across five model families show CGD outperforms Critique Fine-Tuning and standard distillation on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, achieving a 7% average improvement and up to +15.0% on AMC23 and +12.2% on MATH-500. On AIME24 and AIME25, CGD achieves higher Pass@1 and stronger low-k performance, indicating better reasoning quality per sample, while preserving instruction-following abilities where CFT degrades by -21.3% on IFEval.
为了解决监督微调模型仅模仿输出而不内化推理过程的问题，本文提出批判引导蒸馏（CGD）框架，该框架在微调阶段训练学生模型根据教师批判性反馈修正错误回答，但推理时不再使用批判信号，从而促进错误感知推理而不引发输出格式漂移或通用能力退化。在五个模型家族的实验中，CGD在数学推理基准上优于批判微调和标准蒸馏，平均提升7%，在AMC23上最高提升15.0%，在MATH-500上最高提升12.2%。在AIME24和AIME25等高难度竞赛题上，CGD实现更高的Pass@1和更强的低k值表现，表明单样本推理质量更优，同时保持指令遵循能力，而批判微调在IFEval上退化达-21.3%。
INSHAPE: Instance-Level Shapelets for Interpretable Time-Series Classification
Authors: Seongjun Lee, Seokhyun Lee, Changhee Lee
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2026-05-19T16:43:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:43:41+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IJCAI 2026. 25 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discovering shapelets -- i.e., discriminative temporal patterns within time series -- has been widely studied to address the inherent complexity of time-series classification (TSC) and to make model decision-making processes more transparent. However, existing methods primarily focus on population-level shapelets optimized across the entire dataset, which leads to two fundamental limitations: (i) population-level patterns often misalign with instance-specific features, resulting in suboptimal performance and potentially misleading interpretations, and (ii) most methods treat shapelets as independent entities, overlooking important temporal dependencies and interactions among multiple patterns. To address these limitations, we propose INSHAPE, an interpretable TSC framework that discovers variable-length, discriminative temporal patterns specific to each time series. INSHAPE identifies these patterns as non-overlapping segments and models their temporal dependencies, thereby providing clear instance-level interpretations while achieving strong predictive performance. Furthermore, INSHAPE bridges local and global interpretability through a bottom-up approach, aggregating instance-level shapelets into prototypical (population-level) shapelets. Extensive experiments on 128 UCR and 30 UEA benchmark datasets show that INSHAPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art shapelet-based methods while providing more intuitive and interpretable insights.
中文标题/摘要
标题：INSHAPE：面向可解释时间序列分类的实例级形状子序列
发现形状子序列——即时间序列中的判别性时序模式——已被广泛研究，旨在应对时间序列分类（TSC）固有的复杂性并提升模型决策过程的透明度。然而，现有方法主要聚焦于在整个数据集上优化的群体级形状子序列，导致两个根本性局限：(i) 群体级模式常与实例特异性特征不匹配，导致性能欠佳且可能产生误导性解释；(ii) 大多数方法将形状子序列视为独立实体，忽视了多个模式间重要的时序依赖与交互关系。为解决上述问题，我们提出INSHAPE，一种可解释的TSC框架，用于发现每个时间序列特有的可变长度判别性时序模式。INSHAPE将这些模式识别为不重叠片段，并建模其时序依赖关系，从而在保持强预测性能的同时提供清晰的实例级解释。此外，INSHAPE通过自下而上的方法，将实例级形状子序列聚合为原型（群体级）形状子序列，弥合局部与全局可解释性之间的鸿沟。在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上的大量实验表明，INSHAPE在性能上持续优于现有最先进的形状子序列方法，同时提供更直观、可解释的洞察。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of population-level shapelets that misalign with instance-specific features and ignore temporal interactions, INSHAPE introduces an interpretable time-series classification framework that discovers variable-length, non-overlapping shapelets unique to each time series. These instance-level patterns are modeled with their temporal dependencies to enhance both interpretability and predictive performance. Experimental results across 128 UCR and 30 UEA datasets demonstrate that INSHAPE consistently outperforms existing shapelet-based methods while offering clearer, more intuitive explanations by aggregating instance-level shapelets into prototypical patterns.
为了解决现有群体级形状特征与实例特征不匹配、忽略时间依赖的问题，INSHAPE提出了一种可解释的时间序列分类框架，为每个时间序列发现具有可变长度、非重叠的判别性形状特征，并建模其时间依赖关系，从而提升可解释性与预测性能。在128个UCR和30个UEA基准数据集上的实验表明，INSHAPE持续优于现有形状特征方法，同时通过聚合实例级形状特征生成原型特征，提供更直观的解释。
ThoughtTrace: Understanding User Thoughts in Real-World LLM Interactions
Authors: Chuanyang Jin, Binze Li, Haopeng Xie, Cathy Mengying Fang, Tianjian Li, Shayne Longpre, Hongxiang Gu, Maximillian Chen, Tianmin Shu
First: 2026-05-19T16:42:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:42:06+00:00
Comments: 53 pages, 23 figures, 4 tables. Project website: https://thoughttrace-project.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Conversational AI has now reached billions of users, yet existing datasets capture only what people say, not what they think. We introduce ThoughtTrace, the first large-scale dataset that pairs real-world multi-turn human--AI conversations with users' self-reported thoughts: their reasons for sending prompts and reactions to assistant responses. ThoughtTrace comprises 1,058 users, 2,155 conversations, 17,058 turns, and 10,174 thought annotations collected across 20 language models. Our analysis shows that ThoughtTrace captures long-horizon, topically diverse interactions, and that thoughts are semantically distinct from messages, difficult for frontier LLMs to infer from context, diverse in content, and tied to conversation stages. We further demonstrate the utility of thoughts for downstream modeling. First, thoughts improve user-behavior prediction as inference-time context. Second, thought-guided rewrites provide fine-grained alignment signals for training personalized assistants. Together, ThoughtTrace establishes user thoughts as a new data modality for studying the cognitive dynamics behind human--AI interaction and provides a foundation for building assistants that better understand and adapt to users' latent goals, preferences, and needs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ThoughtTrace：理解真实世界大语言模型交互中的用户思维
对话式AI已覆盖数十亿用户，但现有数据集仅记录用户所说内容，未捕捉其思维过程。我们引入ThoughtTrace，这是首个将真实多轮人机对话与用户自报思维（发送提示的原因及对助手回复的反应）配对的大规模数据集。ThoughtTrace包含1058名用户、2155次对话、17058轮交互及10174条思维标注，覆盖20种语言模型。分析表明，ThoughtTrace捕捉了长跨度、主题多样化的交互，且思维在语义上与消息不同，前沿大语言模型难以从上下文推断，内容多样且与对话阶段相关。我们进一步证明思维对下游建模的效用：首先，思维可作为推理时上下文提升用户行为预测；其次，思维引导的重写可为训练个性化助手提供细粒度对齐信号。总体而言，ThoughtTrace确立了用户思维作为研究人机交互认知动态的新数据模态，并为构建更理解并适应用户潜在目标、偏好与需求的助手奠定基础。

What Do Evolutionary Coding Agents Evolve?
Authors: Nico Pelleriti, Sree Harsha Nelaturu, Zhanke Zhou, Zongze Li, Max Zimmer, Bo Han, Sebastian Pokutta
First: 2026-05-19T16:41:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:41:45+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 12 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work pairs LLMs with evolutionary search to iteratively generate, modify, and select code using task-specific feedback. These systems have produced strong results in mathematical discovery and algorithm design, yet a fundamental question remains: what do they actually evolve? Progress is typically summarized by the best score a run reaches under a task-specific evaluator, but that score can reflect several different mechanisms: new algorithmic structure, re-tuning an existing strategy, recombining ideas already in the model's internal knowledge, or overfitting to the evaluator. Distinguishing these mechanisms requires inspecting the search process itself, not only its final outcome. We introduce EvoTrace, a dataset of evolutionary coding traces spanning four evolutionary frameworks, reasoning and non-reasoning models, and 16 tasks across mathematics and algorithm design. To analyze these traces, we develop EvoReplay, a replay-based methodology that reconstructs the local search states behind high-scoring solutions and tests controlled interventions, including adjusting constants, removing program components and substituting models or prompting contexts. We annotate every code edit in EvoTrace with one of nine recurring edit types using an LLM-as-judge pipeline validated against blind human re-annotation. Across EvoTrace, most score gains come from a small subset of these edit types. We further find a deterministic cycling pattern: about 30% of code lines added during search are byte-identical re-introductions of previously-deleted lines, present throughout nearly every run. These results show that benchmark gains in evolutionary coding agents can arise from qualitatively different mechanisms, only some of which correspond to new algorithmic structure. EvoTrace enables more diagnostic evaluation of evolutionary coding agents beyond final benchmark scores.
Summary / 总结
Recent work pairs LLMs with evolutionary search to iteratively generate, modify, and select code using task-specific feedback.
Spatially Prompted Visual Trajectory Prediction for Egocentric Manipulation
Authors: Yifan Li, Xinyu Zhou, Yunhao Ge, Yu Kong
First: 2026-05-19T16:39:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:39:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic manipulation is often specified through language instructions or task identifiers, yet cluttered environments with similar objects are better handled by spatially indicating what to move and where to place it. Addressing the vision-centric challenge of object and goal specification, we present, to the best of our knowledge, the first formalization of Spatially Prompted Visual Trajectory Prediction (SP-VTP). This novel setting utilizes initial spatial prompts (like bounding boxes or points) to define task objectives, tasking the model with forecasting future end-effector trajectories from egocentric streams. To study this problem, we collect and annotate EgoSPT, a dataset of egocentric spatially prompted manipulation trajectories with first-frame object and target grounding annotations and recovered 3D end-effector motion. SP-VTP is challenging because the task specification is static, while the scene configuration evolves over time. To solve this problem, we propose SPOT(Spatially Prompted Object-Target Policy), which combines a task encoder for first-frame visual and coordinate spatial prompts, an observation encoder for current visual and history context, and a trajectory generator for future end-effector motion. Experiments under strict scene-level splits show that SPOT improves cross-scene trajectory prediction over non-prompted or single-source prompted baselines. Together, EgoSPT and SPOT establish a new spatial prompting problem SP-VTP, as a simple and scalable task condition for egocentric manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于空间提示的视觉轨迹预测用于第一人称操作
机器人操作通常通过语言指令或任务标识符指定，但在包含相似物体的杂乱环境中，通过空间指示要移动的对象及其放置位置更为有效。为应对以视觉为中心的物体与目标指定挑战，我们提出了空间提示视觉轨迹预测（SP-VTP）的首次形式化定义。该新设定利用初始空间提示（如边界框或点）定义任务目标，要求模型从第一人称视觉流中预测未来末端执行器轨迹。为研究该问题，我们收集并标注了EgoSPT数据集，包含第一帧物体与目标定位标注及恢复的三维末端执行器运动轨迹。SP-VTP具有挑战性，因为任务指定是静态的，而场景配置随时间演变。为解决此问题，我们提出SPOT（空间提示对象-目标策略），结合任务编码器（处理首帧视觉与坐标空间提示）、观测编码器（处理当前视觉与历史上下文）及轨迹生成器（预测未来末端执行器运动）。在严格场景级划分的实验中，SPOT在跨场景轨迹预测上优于无提示或单一源提示基线。EgoSPT与SPOT共同确立了新的空间提示问题SP-VTP，作为第一人称操作的简单且可扩展的任务条件。
