Robotic manipulation is often specified through language instructions or task identifiers, yet cluttered environments with similar objects are better handled by spatially indicating what to move and where to place it.
为应对复杂环境中通过空间提示而非语言指令进行机器人操作的需求，本文提出空间提示视觉轨迹预测（SP-VTP），这是首个形式化利用初始空间提示（如边界框或点）定义任务目标并预测第一人称视角下末端执行器未来轨迹的方法。所提方法SPOT结合任务编码器（处理静态空间提示与首帧视觉）、观测编码器（处理动态视觉与历史上下文）和轨迹生成器（预测未来运动）。在新构建的EgoSPT数据集上，SPOT在严格场景划分下显著优于无提示和单一提示基线模型，验证了其跨场景轨迹预测能力。
BalanceRAG: Joint Risk Calibration for Cascaded Retrieval-Augmented Generation
Authors: Zijun Jia, Yuanchang Ye, Sen Jia, Yiyao Qian, Haoning Wang, Baojie Chen, Diyin Tang, Jinsong Yu, Zhiyuan Wang
First: 2026-05-19T16:38:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:38:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can enhance factuality via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but applying RAG to every query is unnecessary when the model-only answer is reliable. This motivates cascaded RAG: each query is first handled by an LLM-only branch, escalated to a RAG fallback only if the primary branch is uncertain, and abstained from when neither branch is sufficiently trustworthy. However, calibrating such cascades stage by stage may be conservative, since the final utility depends on joint uncertainty thresholding of LLM-only and RAG. In this work, we develop BalanceRAG to certify threshold pairs at a target risk level. Given uncertainty scores from the two branches, BalanceRAG frames each threshold pair as an operating point on a two-dimensional lattice and identifies safe operating points using sequential graphical testing. This enables risk-adaptive threshold calibration, controlling the system-level error rate among accepted points, while retaining more examples. Furthermore, BalanceRAG extends to multi-risk calibration, allowing retrieval usage to be bounded together with the selection-conditioned risk. Experiments on three open-domain question answering (QA) benchmarks across multiple LLM backbones demonstrate that BalanceRAG meets prescribed risk levels, preserves higher coverage and more accepted correct examples, and reduces unnecessary retrieval calls compared with always-on RAG.
Summary / 总结
Large language models (LLMs) can enhance factuality via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), but applying RAG to every query is unnecessary when the model-only answer is reliable.
为减少冗余检索，本文提出BalanceRAG，联合校准LLM纯生成与RAG两个分支的风险阈值，根据不确定性分数动态决策。它将每对阈值视为二维格点上的操作点，通过序列图形测试识别安全操作点，在控制系统级错误率的同时最大化正确接受样本。在三个开放域问答基准上的实验表明，BalanceRAG可达成目标风险水平，保持更高覆盖率和更多正确答案，且相比始终启用RAG减少不必要的检索调用。
VL-DPO: Vision-Language-Guided Finetuning for Preference-Aligned Autonomous Driving
Authors: Zhefan Xu, Ghassen Jerfel, Marina Haliem, Qi Zhao, Jeonhyung Kang, Khaled S. Refaat
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-05-19T16:36:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:36:34+00:00
Comments: Published in International Conference on Robotics and Automation (ICRA), 2026 8 pages, 6 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid growth of autonomous driving datasets has enabled the scaling of powerful motion forecasting models. While large-scale pretraining provides strong performance, the standard imitation objective may not fully capture the complex nuances of human driving preferences. Meanwhile, recent advances in vision-language models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning and commonsense understanding. Building on these capabilities, this paper presents VL-DPO, a vision-language-guided framework that aligns ego-vehicle motion forecasting models with human preferences. Our approach leverages a VLM as a zero-shot reasoner to automatically generate preference pairs from a pretrained model's rollouts, which are then used to finetune the model via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). We finetune our models on the Waymo Open End-to-End Driving Dataset (WOD-E2E) and evaluate performance against held-out human preference annotations using rater feedback score (RFS) and average displacement error (ADE). Our experiments confirm that the VLM's trajectory selection is a high-quality proxy for human preference. Our final model, VL-DPO, yields an 11.94% increase in RFS and a 10.01% reduction in ADE over the pretrained model.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between large-scale pretrained motion forecasting models and human driving preferences, this paper introduces VL-DPO, a vision-language-guided finetuning framework that leverages a vision-language model as a zero-shot reasoner to generate preference pairs from model rollouts, which are then used to finetune the model via Direct Preference Optimization. Evaluated on the Waymo Open End-to-End Driving Dataset, VL-DPO outperforms the pretrained baseline by achieving an 11.94% increase in Rater Feedback Score and a 10.01% reduction in Average Displacement Error, demonstrating that VLM-guided trajectory selection effectively proxies human preference.
为弥合大规模预训练运动预测模型与人类驾驶偏好之间的差距，本文提出VL-DPO框架，利用视觉语言模型作为零样本推理器，从模型轨迹中自动生成偏好对，再通过直接偏好优化进行微调。在Waymo开放端到端驾驶数据集上评估，VL-DPO相较预训练模型在评分者反馈得分上提升11.94%，平均位移误差降低10.01%，证明视觉语言模型引导的轨迹选择能有效代理人类偏好。
Learning Stable Predictors from Weak Supervision under Distribution Shift
Authors: Mehrdad Shoeibi, Elias Hossain, Ivan Garibay, Niloofar Yousefi
First: 2026-04-05T23:46:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:35:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning from weak, proxy, or relative supervision is common when ground-truth labels are unavailable, but robustness under distribution shift remains poorly understood because the supervision mechanism itself may change across environments. We formalize this phenomenon as supervision drift, defined as changes in $P(y \mid x, c)$ across contexts, and study it in CRISPR-Cas13d transcriptomic perturbation experiments where guide efficacy is inferred indirectly from RNA-seq responses. Using publicly available data spanning two human cell lines and multiple post-induction timepoints, we construct a controlled non-IID benchmark with explicit domain (cell line) and temporal shifts, while reusing a fixed weak-label construction across all contexts to avoid changing targets. Across linear and tree-based models, weak supervision supports meaningful learning in-domain (ridge $R^2 = 0.356$, Spearman $ρ= 0.442$) and partial cross-cell-line transfer ($ρ\approx 0.40$). In contrast, temporal transfer collapses across all model classes considered, yielding negative $R^2$ and weak or near-zero $ρ$ (ridge $R^2 = -0.145$, $ρ= 0.008$; XGBoost $R^2 = -0.155$, $ρ= 0.056$; random forest $R^2 = -0.322$, $ρ= 0.139$). Additional robustness analyses using externally recomputed weak labels, shift-score quantification, and simple mitigation baselines preserve the same qualitative pattern. Feature-label association and feature-importance analyses remain relatively stable across cell lines but change sharply over time, indicating that failures arise from supervision drift rather than model capacity or simple covariate shift. These results show that strong in-domain performance under weak supervision can be misleading and motivate feature stability as a lightweight diagnostic for non-transferability before deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在分布偏移下从弱监督中学习稳定预测器
当真实标签不可用时，从弱、代理或相对监督中学习很常见，但在分布偏移下的鲁棒性仍不明确，因为监督机制本身可能随环境变化。我们将此现象形式化为监督漂移，定义为 $P(y \mid x, c)$ 在不同上下文中的变化，并在CRISPR-Cas13d转录组扰动实验中研究它，其中引导效率通过RNA-seq响应间接推断。利用公开数据（涵盖两种人类细胞系和多个诱导后时间点），我们构建了一个受控的非独立同分布基准，具有明确的域（细胞系）和时间偏移，同时在所有上下文中重用固定的弱标签构造以避免改变目标。在线性与树模型中，弱监督支持域内有意义的学习（岭回归 $R^2 = 0.356$，斯皮尔曼 $ρ= 0.442$）和部分跨细胞系迁移（$ρ\approx 0.40$）。相比之下，时间迁移在所有考虑的模型类别中均崩溃，导致负 $R^2$ 和弱或接近零的 $ρ$（岭回归 $R^2 = -0.145$，$ρ= 0.008$；XGBoost $R^2 = -0.155$，$ρ= 0.056$；随机森林 $R^2 = -0.322$，$ρ= 0.139$）。使用外部重新计算的弱标签、偏移分数量化和简单缓解基线的额外鲁棒性分析保持相同定性模式。特征-标签关联和特征重要性分析在细胞系间相对稳定，但随时间急剧变化，表明失败源于监督漂移而非模型容量或简单协变量偏移。这些结果表明，弱监督下的强域内性能可能具有误导性，并推动特征稳定性作为部署前非迁移性轻量诊断。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning robust predictors from weak supervision under distribution shifts, the study formalizes 'supervision drift' as changes in the conditional probability of labels given features across contexts. Using CRISPR-Cas13d transcriptomic data from two human cell lines and multiple timepoints, the authors construct a controlled non-IID benchmark with fixed weak-label construction to isolate drift effects. Across linear and tree-based models, weak supervision yields meaningful in-domain performance (ridge R²=0.356, Spearman ρ=0.442) and partial cross-cell-line transfer (ρ≈0.40), but temporal transfer fails catastrophically (ridge R²=-0.145, ρ=0.008; XGBoost R²=-0.155, ρ=0.056; random forest R²=-0.322, ρ=0.139). Robustness checks and feature analysis confirm that drift—not model capacity or covariate shift—drives failure, highlighting feature stability as a diagnostic for non-transferability.
为解决弱监督下分布偏移导致的模型鲁棒性问题，研究将‘监督漂移’形式化为不同上下文条件下标签条件概率的变化。基于CRISPR-Cas13d转录组数据，作者构建了包含两个细胞系和多个时间点的非独立同分布基准，固定弱标签构造以隔离漂移影响。线性与树模型在域内表现良好（岭回归R²=0.356，Spearman ρ=0.442），跨细胞系部分迁移有效（ρ≈0.40），但时间迁移失败严重（岭回归R²=-0.145，ρ=0.008；XGBoost R²=-0.155，ρ=0.056；随机森林R²=-0.322，ρ=0.139）。鲁棒性验证与特征分析表明，失败源于监督漂移而非模型容量或协变量偏移，提示特征稳定性可作为部署前非迁移性诊断工具。
Test-Time Speculation
Authors: Avinash Kumar, Sujay Sanghavi, Poulami Das
First: 2026-05-10T05:02:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:34:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by using a fast draft model to generate tokens and a more accurate target model to verify them. Its performance depends on the $\textit{acceptance length}$, or number of draft tokens accepted by the target. Our studies show that the acceptance length of even state-of-the-art speculators, like DFlash, EAGLE-3 and PARD degrade with generation length, reaching values close to 1 (i.e. no speedup) within just a few thousand output tokens, making speculators ineffective for long-response tasks. Acceptance lengths decline because most speculators are trained offline on short sequences, but are forced to match the target model on much longer outputs at inference, well beyond their training distribution. To address this issue, we propose $\textit{Test-Time Speculation (TTS)}$, an online distillation approach that continuously adapts the speculator at test-time. TTS leverages the key insight that the token verification step already invokes the target model for each draft token, providing the training signal needed to adapt the draft at no additional cost. Treating the draft as the student and the target as a teacher, TTS adjusts the draft over several speculation rounds, with each update improving the draft's accuracy as generation proceeds. Our results across multiple models from the Qwen-3, Qwen-3.5, and Llama3.1 families show that TTS improves acceptance lengths over state-of-the-art speculators by up to $72\%$ and $41\%$ on average, with the benefits scaling with increased generation lengths.
中文标题/摘要
标题：测试时推测
推测解码通过使用快速草稿模型生成标记，再由更精确的目标模型验证，加速大语言模型推理。其性能取决于接受长度（即目标模型接受的草稿标记数量）。我们的研究表明，即使是DFlash、EAGLE-3和PARD等最先进的推测器，其接受长度也会随生成长度增加而下降，在仅几千个输出标记后降至接近1（即无加速），使其在长响应任务中无效。接受长度下降的原因是大多数推测器在离线训练时仅使用短序列，但在推理时被迫在远超训练分布的长输出上匹配目标模型。 为解决此问题，我们提出测试时推测（TTS），一种在线蒸馏方法，可在测试时持续调整推测器。TTS利用关键洞察：每个草稿标记的验证步骤已调用目标模型，无需额外成本即可提供调整草稿所需的训练信号。将草稿视为学生，目标模型视为教师，TTS在多次推测轮次中逐步调整草稿，随着生成进行，每次更新均提升草稿准确性。我们在Qwen-3、Qwen-3.5和Llama3.1系列多个模型上的结果表明，TTS相较最先进推测器平均提升接受长度72%和41%，且收益随生成长度增加而扩大。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of speculative decoding performance on long outputs due to mismatch between offline training and online inference, this paper introduces Test-Time Speculation (TTS), an online distillation method that adapts the draft model during inference using the target model’s verification step as a free teacher signal. TTS iteratively refines the draft model over multiple speculation rounds, improving its alignment with the target model as generation proceeds. Experimental results across Qwen-3, Qwen-3.5, and Llama3.1 models show TTS boosts average acceptance lengths by 72% and 41% over state-of-the-art speculators, with gains scaling with longer generation lengths.
为了解决现有推测解码器在长生成任务中因训练与推理长度不匹配导致的接受长度急剧下降问题，本文提出测试时推测（TTS），一种在推理过程中持续在线微调草稿模型的方法，利用目标模型的验证步骤作为免费教师信号。TTS将草稿模型视为学生，通过多轮推测迭代优化其准确性。在Qwen-3、Qwen-3.5和Llama3.1系列模型上的实验表明，TTS相较现有最先进推测器平均提升接受长度72%和41%，且收益随生成长度增加而扩大。
PiKV: KV Cache Management System for Mixture of Experts
Authors: Dong Liu, Yanxuan Yu, Ben Lengerich, Ying Nian Wu
First: 2025-08-02T03:50:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:34:08+00:00
Comments: Github Link: https://github.com/NoakLiu/PiKV
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As large-scale language models continue to scale up in both size and context length, the memory and communication cost of key-value (KV) cache storage has become a major bottleneck in multi-GPU and multi-node inference. While MoE-based architectures sparsify computation across experts, the corresponding KV caches remain dense and globally synchronized, resulting in significant overhead. We introduce \textbf{PiKV}, a parallel and distributed KV cache serving framework tailored for MoE architecture. PiKV leverages \textit{expert-sharded KV storage} to partition caches across GPUs, \textit{PiKV routing} to reduce token-to-KV access, and a \textit{PiKV Scheduling} to adaptively retain query-relevant entries. To further reduce memory usage, PiKV integrates \textit{PiKV Compression} modules the caching pipeline for acceleration. PiKV is recently publicly available as an open-source software library: \href{https://github.com/NoakLiu/PiKV}{https://github.com/NoakLiu/PiKV}. PiKV is still a living project, aiming to become a comprehesive KV Cache management system for MoE Architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PiKV：面向专家混合架构的键值缓存管理系统
随着大规模语言模型在规模和上下文长度上的持续扩展，键值（KV）缓存存储的内存与通信开销已成为多GPU和多节点推理中的主要瓶颈。尽管基于MoE的架构在专家间稀疏化计算，但相应的KV缓存仍保持密集且全局同步，导致显著开销。 我们提出了\textbf{PiKV}，一个专为MoE架构设计的并行分布式KV缓存服务框架。PiKV利用\textit{专家分片KV存储}将缓存分区至各GPU，\textit{PiKV路由}以减少Token到KV的访问，以及\textit{PiKV调度}以自适应保留相关查询条目。为进一步降低内存占用，PiKV在缓存流程中集成了\textit{PiKV压缩}模块以加速处理。 PiKV近期作为开源软件库公开发布：\href{https://github.com/NoakLiu/PiKV}{https://github.com/NoakLiu/PiKV}。PiKV仍处于活跃开发中，目标是成为MoE架构的综合性KV缓存管理系统。
Summary / 总结
As large-scale language models continue to scale up in both size and context length, the memory and communication cost of key-value (KV) cache storage has become a major bottleneck in multi-GPU and multi-node inference.
为了解决大规模MoE语言模型在多GPU系统中KV缓存带来的内存与通信瓶颈，PiKV提出了一种并行分布式框架，通过专家分片缓存、PiKV路由减少token访问、自适应调度保留相关查询条目，并集成压缩模块降低内存占用。实验结果表明，该框架在实际MoE模型推理负载下显著降低了内存使用和通信开销，提升了推理效率。
Probability-Conserving Flow Guidance
Authors: Parsa Esmati, Junha Hyung, Amirhossein Dadashzadeh, Jaegul Choo, Majid Mirmehdi
First: 2026-05-19T16:34:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:34:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion and flow-based generative models dominate visual synthesis, with guidance aligning samples to user input and improving perceptual quality. However, Classifier-Free Guidance (CFG) and extrapolation-based methods are heuristic linear combinations of velocities/scores that ignore the generative manifold geometry, breaking probability conservation and driving samples off the learned manifold under strong guidance. We analyse guidance through the continuity equation and show its effect decomposes into a divergence term and a score-parallel term defined invariantly across parameterisations. We prove the divergence term blows up structurally as sampling approaches the data manifold, motivating a time-dependent schedule alongside score-parallel attenuation. The resulting plug-and-play rule, Adaptive Manifold Guidance (AdaMaG), bounds both terms at no additional inference cost. Finally, we show that most empirical heuristics for reducing saturation or improving generation quality correspond directly to the two terms in our decomposition. Across image generation benchmarks, AdaMaG improves realism, reduces hallucinations, and induces controlled desaturation in high-guidance regimes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：概率守恒的流引导
扩散和基于流的生成模型主导视觉合成，引导机制将样本对齐用户输入并提升感知质量。然而，分类器自由引导（CFG）和基于外推的方法是速度/分数的启发式线性组合，忽略了生成流形的几何结构，破坏概率守恒，并在强引导下将样本驱离学习到的流形。我们通过连续性方程分析引导机制，证明其效应分解为一个散度项和一个与分数平行的项，二者在参数化之间不变。我们证明散度项在采样接近数据流形时结构上发散，因此提出结合分数平行衰减的时间依赖调度。由此得到的即插即用规则——自适应流形引导（AdaMaG）——在不增加推理开销的前提下约束两项。最后，我们表明大多数用于降低饱和或提升生成质量的经验启发式方法，均直接对应我们分解中的两项。在图像生成基准测试中，AdaMaG提升真实感、减少幻觉，并在强引导下诱导可控的去饱和。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of existing guidance methods to preserve probability conservation and stay on the generative manifold under strong guidance, this work introduces Adaptive Manifold Guidance (AdaMaG), a theoretically grounded approach derived from the continuity equation. It decomposes guidance effects into a divergence term and a score-parallel term, both invariant across parameterizations, and proposes a time-dependent schedule with score-parallel attenuation to bound both terms without extra inference cost. Experiments across image generation benchmarks show AdaMaG enhances realism, reduces hallucinations, and enables controlled desaturation under high guidance, aligning with common empirical heuristics.
为了解决现有引导方法破坏概率守恒并偏离生成流形的问题，该研究通过连续性方程分析引导机制，将其效果分解为发散项与得分平行项。作者提出了一种即插即用的自适应流形引导（AdaMaG）方法，通过动态调度发散项的时间依赖衰减并缩放得分平行项，无需额外推理开销。实验表明，AdaMaG在图像生成任务中提升了真实感、减少了幻觉，并在强引导下实现可控去饱和，同时与常见经验启发式方法直接对应。
Argus: Evidence Assembly for Scalable Deep Research Agents
Authors: Zhen Zhang, Liangcai Su, Zhuo Chen, Xiang Lin, Haotian Xu, Simon Shaolei Du, Kaiyu Yang, Bo An, Lidong Bing, Xinyu Wang
First: 2026-05-15T17:29:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:32:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep research agents have achieved remarkable progress on complex information seeking tasks. Even long ReAct style rollouts explore only a single trajectory, while recent state of the art systems scale inference time compute via parallel search and aggregation. Yet deep research answers are composed of complementary pieces of evidence, which parallel rollouts often duplicate rather than complete, yielding diminishing returns while pushing the aggregation context toward the model's limit. We propose Argus, an agentic system in which a Searcher and a Navigator cooperate to treat deep research as assembling a jigsaw from complementary evidence pieces, rather than brute forcing the whole answer in parallel. The Searcher collects evidence traces for a given sub-query through ReAct-style interaction. The Navigator maintains a shared evidence graph, verifying which pieces are still missing, dispatching Searchers to gather them, and reasoning over the completed graph to produce a source-traced final answer. We train the Navigator with reinforcement learning to verify, dispatch, and synthesize, while independently training the Searcher to remain a standard ReAct agent. The resulting Navigator supports rollouts with a single Searcher or many in parallel without retraining. With both Searcher and Navigator built on a 35B-A3B MoE backbone, Argus gains 5.5 points with a single Searcher and 12.7 points with 8 parallel Searchers, averaged over eight benchmarks. With 64 Searchers it reaches 86.2 on BrowseComp, surpassing every proprietary agent we benchmark, while the Navigator's reasoning context stays under 21.5K tokens.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Argus：面向可扩展深度研究代理的证据组装
深度研究代理在复杂信息检索任务中已取得显著进展。即使长序列的ReAct风格展开也仅探索单一轨迹，而最新最先进系统通过并行搜索与聚合来扩展推理时间计算。然而，深度研究答案由互补的证据片段组成，而并行展开常导致重复而非补充，造成边际效益递减，同时将聚合上下文推向模型极限。我们提出Argus，一种代理系统，其中搜索者与导航者协作，将深度研究视为从互补证据片段中拼凑拼图，而非并行暴力求解整个答案。搜索者通过ReAct风格交互收集给定子查询的证据轨迹。导航者维护共享证据图，验证哪些片段仍缺失，调度搜索者收集它们，并在完成的图上进行推理以生成可溯源的最终答案。我们使用强化学习训练导航者以验证、调度和合成，同时独立训练搜索者使其保持标准ReAct代理。所得导航者支持单个或多个搜索者并行展开，无需重新训练。当搜索者与导航者均基于35B-A3B MoE架构构建时，Argus在单个搜索者下提升5.5分，在8个并行搜索者下提升12.7分（平均八项基准测试）。在64个搜索者下，其在BrowseComp上达到86.2分，超越我们所有基准的专有代理，同时导航者的推理上下文保持在21.5K tokens以内。
Summary / 总结
Deep research agents have achieved remarkable progress on complex information seeking tasks.
为了解决深度研究代理中并行搜索常导致证据重复而非互补的问题，Argus提出了一种由搜索者与导航者协作的框架。搜索者通过ReAct风格交互收集子查询证据，导航者维护共享证据图，识别缺失部分、分配任务并综合最终答案。导航者通过强化学习训练以实现验证、调度与合成，而搜索者保持为标准ReAct代理。实验表明，单个搜索者时性能提升5.5分，8个并行搜索者时提升12.7分，64个搜索者时在BrowseComp上达到86.2分，超越所有对比的专有代理，同时导航者推理上下文控制在21.5K token以内。
Vision-OPD: Learning to See Fine Details for Multimodal LLMs via On-Policy Self-Distillation
Authors: Qianhao Yuan, Jie Lou, Xing Yu, Hongyu Lin, Le Sun, Xianpei Han, Yaojie Lu
First: 2026-05-18T17:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:29:04+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://github.com/VisionOPD/Vision-OPD
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) still struggle with fine-grained visual understanding, where answers often depend on small but decisive evidence in the full image. We observe a regional-to-global perception gap: the same MLLM answers fine-grained questions more accurately when conditioned on evidence-centered crops than on the corresponding full images, suggesting that many failures stem from difficulty to focus on relevant evidence rather than insufficient local recognition ability. Motivated by this observation, we propose Vision-OPD (Vision On-Policy Distillation), a regional-to-global self-distillation framework that transfers the model's own privileged regional perception to its full-image policy. Vision-OPD instantiates two conditional policies from the same MLLM: a crop-conditioned teacher and a full-image-conditioned student. The student generates on-policy rollouts, and Vision-OPD minimizes token-level divergence between the teacher and student next-token distributions along these rollouts. This enables the model to internalize the benefit of visual zooming without external teacher models, ground-truth labels, reward verifiers, or inference-time tool use. Experiments on multiple fine-grained visual understanding benchmarks show that Vision-OPD models achieve competitive or superior performance against much larger open-source, closed-source, and "Thinking-with-Images" agentic models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vision-OPD：通过在线自蒸馏学习多模态大语言模型的精细视觉细节
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在细粒度视觉理解方面仍存在困难，其答案往往依赖于图像中微小但决定性的证据。我们观察到一种区域到全局的感知差距：当MLLMs以证据为中心的裁剪图像作为条件时，其在细粒度问题上的回答准确性高于使用对应完整图像的情况，表明许多失败源于难以聚焦相关证据，而非局部识别能力不足。受此启发，我们提出Vision-OPD（视觉在线自蒸馏），一种从区域到全局的自蒸馏框架，将模型自身的特权区域感知能力迁移到其完整图像策略中。Vision-OPD从同一MLLM中实例化两个条件策略：一个以裁剪图像为条件的教师模型和一个以完整图像为条件的学生模型。学生模型生成在线轨迹，Vision-OPD沿这些轨迹最小化教师与学生下一token分布之间的token级差异。这使模型无需外部教师模型、真实标签、奖励验证器或推理时工具使用，即可内化视觉放大的优势。在多个细粒度视觉理解基准测试中，Vision-OPD模型表现与更大规模的开源、闭源及“图像思考”代理模型相当或更优。
Summary / 总结
Vision-OPD addresses the limitation of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) in fine-grained visual understanding by recognizing that these models perform better when conditioned on evidence-centered image crops rather than full images, indicating a regional-to-global perception gap. The method introduces a self-distillation framework where a crop-conditioned teacher policy guides a full-image-conditioned student policy through on-policy rollouts, minimizing token-level divergence between their next-token distributions. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show that Vision-OPD achieves competitive or superior performance compared to larger open-source, closed-source, and agentic models without requiring external supervision or tools.
Vision-OPD 针对多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉理解中的不足，发现模型在基于局部裁剪图像的条件下表现优于全图输入，揭示了区域到全局的感知差距。该方法通过自蒸馏框架，利用裁剪图像条件的教师策略引导全图条件的学生策略进行在线策略滚动，最小化两者下一词分布的词元级差异。实验表明，Vision-OPD 在多个基准测试中表现优于或至少可比于更大规模的开源、闭源及具象化模型，且无需外部监督或工具。
CopT: Contrastive On-Policy Thinking with Continuous Spaces for General and Agentic Reasoning
Authors: Dachuan Shi, Hanlin Zhu, Xiangchi Yuan, Wanjia Zhao, Kejing Xia, Wen Xiao, Wenke Lee
First: 2026-05-19T16:28:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:28:53+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/sdc17/CopT, Website: https://copt-web.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Chain-of-thought (CoT) is a standard approach for eliciting reasoning capabilities from large language models (LLMs). However, the common CoT paradigm treats thinking as a prerequisite for answering, which can delay access to plausible answers and incur unnecessary token costs even when the model is able to identify an answer before extended thinking, a behavior known as performative reasoning. In this paper, we introduce CopT, a reformulated reasoning pipeline that reverses the usual order of thinking and answering. Instead of thinking before answering, CopT first elicits a draft answer and then invokes subsequent on-policy thinking conditioned on its own draft answer for reflection and correction. To assess whether the draft answer should be trusted, CopT recasts continuous embeddings as inference-time contrastive verifiers. Specifically, it contrasts the model's support for the same generated tokens under discrete-token inputs and continuous-embedding inputs, yielding a sequence-level reverse KL estimator for answer reliability. Our analysis shows that under certain assumptions, the expected estimate equals the mutual information between the unresolved latent state and the emitted answer token, explaining why it captures answer-relevant uncertainty rather than arbitrary uncertainty in the latent state. When the answer is deemed insufficiently reliable, CopT performs further on-policy thinking, where a second KL estimator dynamically controls draft-answer visibility, preserving useful partial information while reducing the risk of being misled by unreliable content. Across mathematics, coding, and agentic reasoning tasks, CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and reduces token usage by up to 57% at comparable or higher accuracy, without any additional training. The code is available at https://github.com/sdc17/CopT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CopT：面向通用与代理推理的连续空间对比式在线思考
链式思维（CoT）是激发大语言模型（LLM）推理能力的标准方法。然而，传统CoT范式将思考视为回答的前提，这可能导致在模型尚未完成思考时即可给出合理答案的情况下，仍延迟获取答案并产生不必要的token开销，这种行为称为表演式推理。本文提出CopT，一种重构的推理流程，颠倒了通常的“先思考后回答”顺序。CopT首先生成草稿答案，随后基于该草稿答案进行条件在线思考，以实现反思与修正。为判断草稿答案是否可信，CopT将连续嵌入重铸为推理时的对比验证器，通过对比模型在离散token输入与连续嵌入输入下对相同生成token的支持度，得到序列级的反向KL估计器，用于评估答案可靠性。分析表明，在特定假设下，该估计值等于未解决潜在状态与输出答案token之间的互信息，解释了其为何能捕捉与答案相关的不确定性，而非潜在状态中的任意不确定性。当答案被认为不可靠时，CopT会进行进一步的在线思考，其中第二个KL估计器动态控制草稿答案的可见性，在保留有用部分信息的同时降低被不可靠内容误导的风险。在数学、编程及代理推理任务中，CopT在不增加额外训练的前提下，峰值准确率提升最高达23%，同时在相当或更高准确率下，token使用量降低最高达57%。代码见https://github.com/sdc17/CopT。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional chain-of-thought reasoning that delays answers and wastes tokens, CopT restructures the reasoning pipeline by first generating a draft answer and then applying on-policy thinking to refine it. It uses continuous embeddings as contrastive verifiers to estimate answer reliability via a reverse KL divergence between discrete and continuous input representations, which approximates mutual information between latent state and answer tokens. When reliability is low, CopT dynamically controls draft-answer visibility using a second KL estimator to preserve useful partial information while avoiding misleading content. Experiments show CopT improves peak accuracy by up to 23% and cuts token usage by up to 57% across math, coding, and agentic tasks without additional training.
为了解决传统链式思维延迟回答、浪费token的问题，CopT将推理流程重构为先生成草稿答案，再基于该答案进行条件化在线推理以修正。它通过对比离散token与连续嵌入下相同生成token的支持度，利用反向KL估计构建序列级答案可靠性评估，其理论基础是互信息，能捕捉与答案相关的不确定性而非任意潜变量不确定性。当答案可靠性不足时，CopT动态控制草稿答案可见性，保留有用部分信息同时避免误导。在数学、编程和代理推理任务中，CopT在不增加训练的前提下，峰值准确率提升最高达23%，token使用量减少最高达57%。
Towards Distillation Guarantees under Algorithmic Alignment for Combinatorial Optimization
Authors: Thien Le, Melanie Weber
First: 2026-05-19T16:28:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:28:18+00:00
Comments: 22 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distillation transfers knowledge from a large model trained on broad data to a smaller, more efficient model suitable for deployment. In structured prediction settings, prior knowledge about the task can guide the choice of a target architecture that is algorithmically aligned with the underlying problem. Building on recent learning-theoretic analyses of decision-tree (DT) distillation (Boix-Adsera, 2024), we study when distillation succeeds for combinatorial optimization tasks. We focus on the case where the target model is a graph neural network whose architecture is aligned with a dynamic programming (DP) algorithm for the task. Assuming that the source model is sufficiently rich, formalized through the linear representation hypothesis (LRH) (Elhage et al., 2022; Park et al., 2024), we show that the distillation problem can be solved efficiently in the complexity parameters of the DP transition function, represented as a DT. Our results provide a rigorous sufficient condition for successful distillation in the flavour of algorithmic alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向组合优化问题的算法对齐蒸馏保证研究
蒸馏技术将从广泛数据训练的大模型知识迁移到更小、更高效的模型，以适应部署需求。在结构化预测场景中，任务先验知识可指导选择与底层问题算法对齐的目标架构。基于近期对决策树（DT）蒸馏的学习理论分析（Boix-Adsera, 2024），我们研究组合优化任务中蒸馏成功的条件。我们聚焦于目标模型为图神经网络，其架构与任务的动态规划（DP）算法对齐的情形。假设源模型足够丰富（通过线性表示假设LRH形式化，Elhage等，2022；Park等，2024），我们证明蒸馏问题可在DP转移函数的复杂度参数内高效求解，该函数以决策树形式表示。我们的结果为算法对齐框架下的蒸馏成功提供了严谨的充分条件。
Summary / 总结
Distillation transfers knowledge from a large model trained on broad data to a smaller, more efficient model suitable for deployment.
为将大模型知识高效迁移到组合优化任务中，本文研究在算法对齐条件下蒸馏成功的条件。提出使用结构与动态规划算法匹配的图神经网络作为目标模型，假设源模型满足线性表示假设。研究证明，在该对齐框架下，蒸馏问题可高效求解，其复杂度取决于DP转移函数的决策树表示，从而给出蒸馏成功的充分理论条件。
X-Ray cardiac angiographic vessel segmentation based on pixel classification using machine learning and region growing
Authors: E O Rodrigues, L O Rodrigues, J J Lima, D Casanova, F Favarim, E R Dosciatti, V Pegorini, L S N Oliveira, F F C Morais
Venue: Biomedical Physics & Engineering Express 2021
First: 2026-05-19T16:27:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:27:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This work proposes a pixel-classification approach for vessel segmentation in x-ray angiograms. The proposal uses textural features such as anisotropic diffusion, features based on the Hessian matrix, mathematical morphology and statistics. These features are extracted from the neighborhood of each pixel. The approach also uses the ELEMENT methodology, which consists of creating a pixel-classification controlled by region-growing where the result of the classification affects further classifications of pixels. The Random Forests classifier is used to predict whether the pixel belongs to the vessel structure. The approach achieved the best accuracy in the literature (95.48%) outperforming unsupervised state-of-the-art approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于像素分类的X射线心脏血管造影图像血管分割方法
该研究提出了一种基于像素分类的血管分割方法，用于X射线血管造影图像。该方法利用各向异性扩散、Hessian矩阵特征、数学形态学和统计特征等纹理特征，从每个像素的邻域中提取。该方法还结合了ELEMENT方法，即通过区域增长控制的像素分类，其中分类结果影响后续像素的分类。采用随机森林分类器预测像素是否属于血管结构。该方法在文献中取得了最佳准确率（95.48%），优于现有的无监督方法。
Summary / 总结
This work proposes a pixel-classification approach for vessel segmentation in x-ray angiograms.
为提升X射线心脏血管造影图像中血管分割的准确性，该研究提出一种结合机器学习与区域生长的像素分类方法。该方法从每个像素邻域提取各向异性扩散、Hessian矩阵特征、数学形态学及统计特征，并采用ELEMENT方法通过区域生长迭代优化像素分类。使用随机森林分类器判断每个像素是否属于血管结构，最终准确率达到95.48%，优于现有无监督方法，创下当前最优性能纪录。
Probing Embodied LLMs: When Higher Observation Fidelity Hurts Problem Solving
Authors: Oussama Zenkri, Oliver Brock
First: 2026-05-19T16:27:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:27:00+00:00
Comments: Submitted to From Animals to Animats: The 18th International Conference on the Simulation of Adaptive Behavior (SAB)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models are increasingly proposed as cognitive components for robotic systems, yet their opaque decision processes make it difficult to explain success or failure in closed-loop embodied tasks. Following an empirical AI methodology, we study embodied LLM agents behaviorally by varying the information available to the agent and measuring the resulting changes in behavior. Using the Lockbox, a sequential mechanical puzzle with hidden interdependencies, we evaluate LLMs across RGB, RGB-D, and ground-truth symbolic observations in a physical robotic setup and use controlled simulation to probe the resulting behavior. Counterintuitively, agents perform best under raw RGB input and worst under perfect ground-truth observations. In simulation, we probe this effect by randomly flipping perceived action outcomes and find that moderate noise improves performance, peaking at a 40% flip probability with a 2.85-fold success rate increase over the noise-free baseline. Further analysis links this gain to a reduction in repetitive action loops. These findings suggest that success rates alone are insufficient for evaluating LLMs, as measured performance may reflect the interaction between perceptual errors and reasoning failures rather than robust problem solving.
Summary / 总结
Large Language Models are increasingly proposed as cognitive components for robotic systems, yet their opaque decision processes make it difficult to explain success or failure in closed-loop embodied tasks.
HOI-PAGE: Zero-Shot Human-Object Interaction Generation with Part Affordance Guidance
Authors: Lei Li, Angela Dai
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-06-08T16:15:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:26:07+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026. Project page: https://craigleili.github.io/projects/hoipage/ Video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gwXjOffCFyk
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present HOI-PAGE, a new approach that prioritizes part-level affordance reasoning to generate high-fidelity 4D human-object interactions (HOIs) from text prompts in a zero-shot fashion. In contrast to prior works that focus on global, whole body-object motion synthesis, our approach explicitly reasons about the underlying part-level mechanics of interactions using large language models (LLMs). We capture this reasoning in a structured part affordance graph (PAG) representation, serving as a high-level interaction scaffolding to guide a three-stage synthesis: first, decomposing input 3D objects into semantic parts; then, generating reference HOI videos from text prompts to extract part-based motion constraints; and finally, optimizing for 4D HOI motion sequences that mimic the reference dynamics while satisfying part-level contact constraints. Extensive experiments show that our approach is flexible and capable of generating complex multi-object or multi-person interaction sequences, with significantly improved realism and text alignment for zero-shot 4D HOI generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HOI-PAGE：基于部件可操作性引导的零样本人体-物体交互生成
我们提出了HOI-PAGE，一种通过文本提示在零样本条件下生成高保真4D人体-物体交互（HOI）的新方法，其核心是优先进行部件级可操作性推理。与以往聚焦于全局、整体身体-物体运动合成的工作不同，我们的方法利用大语言模型（LLMs）显式推理交互背后的部件级力学机制，并将其结构化为部件可操作性图（PAG）表示，作为高层交互框架，指导三阶段合成：首先将输入3D物体分解为语义部件；然后从文本提示生成参考HOI视频以提取基于部件的运动约束；最后优化4D HOI运动序列，使其模仿参考动态并满足部件级接触约束。大量实验表明，该方法灵活且能生成复杂的多物体或多人体交互序列，在零样本4D HOI生成中显著提升了真实感和文本对齐度。

We present HOI-PAGE, a new approach that prioritizes part-level affordance reasoning to generate high-fidelity 4D human-object interactions (HOIs) from text prompts in a zero-shot fashion.

Convergence rates for gradient descent in the training of overparameterized artificial neural networks with piecewise affine activation
Authors: Arnulf Jentzen, Timo Kröger
First: 2021-02-23T18:17:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:23:20+00:00
Comments: 49 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, artificial neural networks have developed into a powerful tool for addressing a multitude of problems for which classical solution approaches reach their limits. However, it is still unclear why gradient descent optimization algorithms with random initialization, such as the well-known batch gradient descent, are able to achieve zero training loss in many situations, even though the objective function is non-convex and non-smooth. One of the most promising approaches to solving this issue in the field of supervised learning is the analysis of gradient descent optimization in the so-called overparameterized regime. In this article, we provide a further contribution to this area of research by considering overparameterized fully connected shallow artificial neural networks with piecewise affine activation, such as the rectified linear unit activation. Specifically, given that the activation function is not affine and the training input data are pairwise distinct, we show that, with high probability, the mean squared error of such a randomly initialized artificial neural network optimized via batch gradient descent converges to zero at a linear convergence rate as long as the width of the artificial neural network is sufficiently large and the learning rate is sufficiently small.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分段仿射激活函数的过参数化人工神经网络梯度下降训练的收敛速率
近年来，人工神经网络已发展为解决众多经典方法难以应对的问题的强大工具。然而，仍不清楚为何随机初始化的梯度下降优化算法（如著名的批量梯度下降）在许多情况下能够实现零训练损失，尽管目标函数是非凸且非光滑的。监督学习领域解决此问题的最有前景的方法之一是分析所谓过参数化情形下的梯度下降优化。本文在此领域做出进一步贡献，研究具有分段仿射激活函数（如修正线性单元）的过参数化全连接浅层人工神经网络。具体而言，在激活函数非仿射且训练输入数据互不相同时，我们证明：只要人工神经网络的宽度足够大且学习率足够小，通过批量梯度下降优化的随机初始化人工神经网络的均方误差将以线性收敛速率以高概率收敛至零。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of gradient descent in training overparameterized neural networks despite non-convex and non-smooth loss landscapes, this study investigates convergence rates for batch gradient descent in shallow, fully connected networks with piecewise affine activations like ReLU. The authors analyze the mean squared error under random initialization and distinct training inputs, proving that with high probability, the error converges to zero at a linear rate provided the network width is sufficiently large and the learning rate is small enough.
鉴于梯度下降在训练过参数化神经网络时虽面临非凸非光滑损失函数仍能取得成功，本文研究了具有分段仿射激活函数（如ReLU）的浅层全连接网络中批量梯度下降的收敛速率。在随机初始化条件下，作者证明当网络宽度足够大且学习率足够小时，均方误差以线性速率以高概率收敛至零，即使激活函数非仿射且输入数据互异。
Smooth Partial Lotteries for Stable Randomized Selection
Authors: Alexander Goldberg, Giulia Fanti, Nihar B. Shah
First: 2026-05-19T16:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:22:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competitive selection processes, from scientific funding to admissions and hiring, use evaluations to score candidates, and eventually choose a subset of them based on those scores. Recently, many organizations have adopted partial lotteries, which randomize selection based on evaluation scores. However, existing lottery designs are inherently unstable, as a small change to a single candidate's score can cause large shifts in their selection probabilities. This instability undermines a key goal of lotteries: reducing the influence of fine-grained score distinctions near the decision boundary. We propose smoothness as a design principle for partial lotteries, formalizing it as a Lipschitz condition on the mapping from review scores over candidates to selection probabilities. We introduce the Clipped Linear Lottery, a simple mechanism in which selection probabilities scale linearly with estimated quality between an upper threshold, above which we always accept, and a lower threshold, below which we always reject. We prove that the Clipped Linear Lottery's worst-case regret matches a lower bound for any smooth selection rule up to a factor of $(1 - k/n)$, where $k/n$ is the acceptance rate. We compare smooth selection to other stability notions like Individual Fairness and Differential Privacy, showing that the Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-regret tradeoff than alternatives. Experiments on real peer review data from ICLR 2025, NeurIPS 2024, and the Swiss National Science Foundation demonstrate that existing lottery designs are highly unstable in practice even under perturbations to a single score. Our experiments also confirm the tightness of our theoretical analysis and show that our proposed Clipped Linear Lottery achieves a better smoothness-utility tradeoff than alternatives in practice.
