标题：平滑部分抽签以实现稳定的随机选择
从科研资助到招生和招聘，竞争性选拔过程通过评估对候选人打分，最终根据这些分数选择部分候选人。最近，许多机构采用了基于评估分数的抽签机制。然而，现有抽签设计本质上不稳定，因为单个候选人分数的微小变化可能导致其入选概率发生大幅波动。这种不稳定性削弱了抽签的核心目标：减少决策边界附近细微分数差异的影响。我们提出“平滑性”作为部分抽签的设计原则，并将其形式化为从候选人评审分数到入选概率的Lipschitz条件。我们引入了“截断线性抽签”，这是一种简单的机制，其中入选概率在上阈值（高于该阈值则始终接受）与下阈值（低于该阈值则始终拒绝）之间线性增长。我们证明，截断线性抽签在最坏情况下的遗憾值与任何平滑选择规则的下界匹配，仅相差因子(1 - k/n)，其中k/n为接受率。我们将平滑选择与其他稳定性概念（如个体公平性和差分隐私）进行比较，表明截断线性抽签在平滑性与遗憾值之间的权衡优于其他方案。在ICLR 2025、NeurIPS 2024和瑞士国家科学基金会的真实同行评审数据上的实验表明，即使对单个分数进行微小扰动，现有抽签设计在实践中仍高度不稳定。我们的实验也验证了理论分析的紧致性，并表明所提出的截断线性抽签在实践中实现了优于其他方案的平滑性-效用权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of existing partial lottery systems in competitive selection processes, which can cause disproportionate changes in selection probabilities with minor score adjustments, the paper proposes smoothness as a formal design principle, defined via a Lipschitz condition on score-to-probability mappings. It introduces the Clipped Linear Lottery, a mechanism that assigns selection probabilities linearly between upper and lower thresholds, ensuring smooth transitions. Theoretical analysis shows this mechanism achieves near-optimal regret, bounded by a factor of (1 - k/n) relative to any smooth rule, and experimental results on real peer review data confirm its superior smoothness-utility tradeoff compared to alternatives, while also validating the theoretical bounds.
为了解决现有部分抽签机制在竞争性选拔中因微小分数变动导致选择概率剧烈波动的问题，本文提出以“平滑性”作为设计原则，形式化为映射的利普希茨连续性。提出“截断线性抽签”机制，通过在设定的上下阈值间线性分配选择概率，确保稳定性。理论分析表明该机制的最坏遗憾接近任意平滑规则的下界，且与个体公平性或差分隐私等替代方案相比，其平滑性-效用权衡更优。在ICLR 2025、NeurIPS 2024及瑞士国家科学基金会的真实评审数据实验中，验证了现有机制在单分数扰动下高度不稳定，同时证实理论分析的紧致性及所提机制在实践中更优的平滑性-效用平衡。
Contrastive Reasoning Alignment: Reinforcement Learning from Hidden Representations
Authors: Haozheng Luo, Yimin Wang, Jiahao Yu, Binghui Wang, Yan Chen
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-03-18T03:00:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:22:05+00:00
Comments: International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose CRAFT, a red-teaming alignment framework that leverages model reasoning capabilities and hidden representations to improve robustness against jailbreak attacks. Unlike prior defenses that operate primarily at the output level, CRAFT aligns large reasoning models to generate safety-aware reasoning traces by explicitly optimizing objectives defined over the hidden state space. Methodologically, CRAFT integrates contrastive representation learning with reinforcement learning to separate safe and unsafe reasoning trajectories, yielding a latent-space geometry that supports robust, reasoning-level safety alignment. Theoretically, we show that incorporating latent-textual consistency into GRPO eliminates superficially aligned policies by ruling them out as local optima. Empirically, we evaluate CRAFT on multiple safety benchmarks using two strong reasoning models, Qwen3-4B-Thinking and R1-Distill-Llama-8B, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses such as IPO and SafeKey. Notably, CRAFT delivers an average 79.0% improvement in reasoning safety and 87.7% improvement in final-response safety over the base models, demonstrating the effectiveness of hidden-space reasoning alignment.
Summary / 总结
We propose CRAFT, a red-teaming alignment framework that leverages model reasoning capabilities and hidden representations to improve robustness against jailbreak attacks.
为提升模型对 jailbreak 攻击的鲁棒性，CRAFT 提出一种基于隐藏表征的强化学习对齐框架，通过在隐藏状态空间中优化目标函数，使大模型生成安全意识的推理轨迹。该方法结合对比表征学习与 GRPO，构建安全与非安全推理轨迹的潜在空间几何结构，理论上排除了表面对齐的局部最优策略。实验表明，CRAFT 在多个安全基准测试中显著优于 IPO 和 SafeKey 等现有防御方法，使用 Qwen3-4B-Thinking 和 R1-Distill-Llama-8B 模型时，推理安全性和最终响应安全性分别提升 79.0% 和 87.7%。
Tail Annealing for Heavy-Tailed Flow Matching
Authors: Jean Pachebat
First: 2026-05-19T16:22:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:22:03+00:00
Comments: 18 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Standard generative models struggle with heavy-tailed data: Lipschitz architectures cannot produce power-law tails from Gaussian noise, and interpolating between heavy-tailed data and Gaussians is ill-posed. We propose a simple fix: apply the soft-log transform $φ(x) = \mathrm{sign}(x) \cdot \log(1 + |x|)$ coordinate-wise to data before training, then exponentiate samples after generation. A Hill diagnostic decides per-coordinate whether to transform, leaving light-tailed margins untouched at no added complexity. This compresses heavy tails into a range where standard flow matching succeeds, without heavy-tailed base distributions or architectural modifications. We provide theoretical intuition for why this works: the log-transform maps Pareto tails to exponentials, and the induced dynamics implement a form of tail annealing via power transformations. On a 144-configuration multivariate benchmark (3 copulas, $d$ up to 100, 4 tail indices), Log-FM dominates specialized baselines on $W_1$, CVaR$_{99}$, and extreme-quantile metrics, and is the only method with zero severe divergences across 2{,}880 runs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于重尾流匹配的尾部退火
标准生成模型难以处理重尾数据：Lipschitz架构无法从高斯噪声中生成幂律尾部，且在重尾数据与高斯分布之间插值是病态的。我们提出一个简单解决方案：在训练前对数据逐坐标应用软对数变换 $φ(x) = \mathrm{sign}(x) \cdot \log(1 + |x|)$，生成后对样本进行指数化。Hill诊断决定每个坐标是否进行变换，无需额外复杂度即可保留轻尾边缘。该方法将重尾压缩至标准流匹配可成功处理的范围，无需重尾基础分布或架构修改。我们提供了理论解释：对数变换将帕累托尾部映射为指数分布，诱导的动力学通过幂变换实现了一种尾部退火形式。在包含144种配置的多元基准测试（3种Copula，维度d最高至100，4种尾指数）中，Log-FM在$W_1$、CVaR$_{99}$及极值分位数指标上优于专用基线方法，且是唯一在2880次运行中无严重发散的方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty standard generative models face in modeling heavy-tailed distributions, this work introduces tail annealing via a soft-log transform applied coordinate-wise to data before training, followed by exponentiation after generation. A Hill diagnostic determines per-coordinate whether transformation is needed, preserving light-tailed components without added complexity. The method compresses heavy tails into a range where standard flow matching succeeds, without requiring heavy-tailed priors or architectural changes. Experiments on a 144-configuration multivariate benchmark show Log-FM outperforms specialized baselines on Wasserstein-1, CVaR99, and extreme-quantile metrics, and achieves zero severe divergences across 2,880 runs.
为了解决标准生成模型在处理重尾数据时因Lipschitz约束和插值问题导致的困难，该论文提出通过坐标级软对数变换（前训练）和指数化（后生成）实现尾部退火。Hill诊断决定每个坐标是否应用变换，保留轻尾边际而不增加复杂度。该方法将重尾压缩到标准流匹配可处理的域，无需重尾先验或架构修改。在144配置的多元基准测试中，Log-FM在Wasserstein距离、CVaR99和极值分位数指标上优于专用基线方法，并在2880次运行中实现零严重发散。
Text-to-SPARQL Generation with Reinforcement Learning: A GRPO-based Approach on DBLP
Authors: Jann Pfeifer, Debayan Banerjee, Ricardo Usbeck
First: 2026-05-19T16:20:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:20:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted by NeSy 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Knowledge graph question answering seeks to translate natural language questions into executable queries over knowledge graphs, but existing approaches often rely on large models or full supervision in the form of gold query annotations. This study examines whether reinforcement learning with outcome-based rewards can train a small instruction-tuned language model to perform zero-shot Text-to-SPARQL generation in the scholarly domain. Group-Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is applied to the Qwen3-1.7B model on DBLP-QuAD, using prompts that combine natural language questions with symbolic hints about entities and relations. Training relies on execution feedback, structural constraints, and answer-level rewards, with an additional variant that incorporates gold-query-based shaping. The resulting models are compared to the unmodified zero-shot baseline and to a supervised DoRA-finetuned baseline across answer-level accuracy, execution accuracy, category-wise scores, and generalization to held-out templates. GRPO substantially improves over the zero-shot baseline and exhibits competitive generalization, while supervised DoRA finetuning achieves higher overall accuracy on the same model scale. Ablation analyses indicate that execution-based rewards account for most gains, with additional shaping yielding limited additional benefit, suggesting that outcome-based reinforcement learning is a viable training strategy when gold queries are unavailable for token-level supervision.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to avoid large models and full supervision in Text-to-SPARQL generation, this study explores reinforcement learning with GRPO to train a small instruction-tuned language model for zero-shot performance on DBLP-QuAD. The method uses prompts combining natural language questions with symbolic hints, and trains via execution feedback, structural constraints, and answer-level rewards, with an optional gold-query shaping variant. Experimental results show GRPO significantly outperforms the zero-shot baseline and achieves competitive generalization, while supervised DoRA finetuning yields higher accuracy on the same model scale; ablation studies confirm execution rewards drive most gains, with shaping offering minimal extra benefit.
为避免大模型和全监督标注，本研究探索基于GRPO的强化学习方法，训练小型指令调优语言模型在DBLP-QuAD上实现零样本Text-to-SPARQL生成。方法结合自然语言问题与符号提示，利用执行反馈、结构约束和答案级奖励，并可选加入黄金查询引导。实验表明，GRPO显著优于零样本基线，泛化能力竞争力强，而监督DoRA微调在相同模型规模下整体准确率更高；消融分析显示执行奖励贡献主要提升，引导奖励增益有限，表明无黄金查询时基于结果的强化学习是可行策略。
Cardiac fat segmentation using computed tomography and an image-to-image conditional generative adversarial neural network
Authors: Guilherme Santos da Silva, Dalcimar Casanova, Jefferson Tales Oliva, Erick Oliveira Rodrigues
Venue: Medical Engineering & Physics 2024
First: 2026-05-19T16:20:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:20:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In recent years, research has highlighted the association between increased adipose tissue surrounding the human heart and elevated susceptibility to cardiovascular diseases such as atrial fibrillation and coronary heart disease. However, the manual segmentation of these fat deposits has not been widely implemented in clinical practice due to the substantial workload it entails for medical professionals and the associated costs. Consequently, the demand for more precise and time-efficient quantitative analysis has driven the emergence of novel computational methods for fat segmentation. This study presents a novel deep learning-based methodology that offers autonomous segmentation and quantification of two distinct types of cardiac fat deposits. The proposed approach leverages the pix2pix network, a generative conditional adversarial network primarily designed for image-to-image translation tasks. By applying this network architecture, we aim to investigate its efficacy in tackling the specific challenge of cardiac fat segmentation, despite not being originally tailored for this purpose. The two types of fat deposits of interest in this study are referred to as epicardial and mediastinal fats, which are spatially separated by the pericardium. The experimental results demonstrated an average accuracy of 99.08% and f1-score 98.73 for the segmentation of the epicardial fat and 97.90% of accuracy and f1-score of 98.40 for the mediastinal fat. These findings represent the high precision and overlap agreement achieved by the proposed methodology. In comparison to existing studies, our approach exhibited superior performance in terms of f1-score and run time, enabling the images to be segmented in real time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用计算机断层扫描和图像到图像条件生成对抗神经网络进行心脏脂肪分割
近年来，研究强调了人类心脏周围脂肪组织增加与心房颤动和冠心病等心血管疾病易感性升高的关联。然而，由于手动分割这些脂肪沉积物对医务人员工作量大且成本高，尚未在临床实践中广泛实施。因此，对更精确、更高效定量分析的需求推动了脂肪分割新计算方法的出现。本研究提出了一种基于深度学习的新方法，可自主分割并量化两种不同的心脏脂肪沉积物。该方法采用pix2pix网络——一种主要用于图像到图像翻译任务的生成式条件对抗网络。尽管该网络并非专为心脏脂肪分割设计，我们仍旨在评估其在解决该特定挑战中的有效性。本研究关注的两种脂肪沉积物称为心外膜脂肪和纵隔脂肪，二者由心包分隔。实验结果显示，心外膜脂肪分割的平均准确率为99.08%，F1分数为98.73%；纵隔脂肪分割的准确率为97.90%，F1分数为98.40%。这些结果体现了该方法的高精度和高重合度。与现有研究相比，本方法在F1分数和运行时间上表现更优，可实现图像实时分割。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the clinical need for efficient and accurate quantification of cardiac fat deposits linked to cardiovascular diseases, this study employs a pix2pix-based conditional generative adversarial network to automate the segmentation of epicardial and mediastinal fats from CT scans. The method leverages the network’s image-to-image translation capability despite its original design for other tasks. Experimental results show high segmentation accuracy—99.08% and 97.90% for epicardial and mediastinal fat, respectively—with corresponding f1-scores of 98.73% and 98.40%, outperforming prior methods in both precision and real-time processing speed.
鉴于手动分割心脏周围脂肪组织耗时且成本高，本研究采用pix2pix条件生成对抗网络，利用其图像到图像转换能力实现心脏CT扫描中 epicardial 和 mediastinal 脂肪的自动化分割。实验结果显示，epicardial 脂肪分割准确率达99.08%，F1分数98.73；mediastinal 脂肪准确率97.90%，F1分数98.40，优于现有方法在F1分数和实时处理速度上的表现。
Rewarding Beliefs, Not Actions: Consistency-Guided Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Agents
Authors: Wenjie Tang, Minne Li, Sijie Huang, Liquan Xiao, Yuan Zhou
First: 2026-05-19T16:19:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:19:29+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 4 figures, 3 tables, plus appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) is a promising paradigm for improving large language model (LLM) agents on long-horizon interactive tasks. However, in partially observable environments, incomplete observations cause agent beliefs to drift over time, while delayed rewards obscure the causal impact of intermediate decisions, exacerbating temporal credit assignment challenges. To address this, we propose ReBel (Reward Belief), a process-level reinforcement learning algorithm that explicitly models structured belief states to summarize interaction history and guide subsequent policy learning. ReBel introduces belief-consistency supervision, converting discrepancies between predicted beliefs and observed feedback into dense self-supervised signals without requiring external step-wise annotations or verifiers. It also employs belief-aware grouping to compare trajectories under similar belief states, yielding more robust and lower-variance advantage estimates. We evaluate ReBel on challenging long-horizon benchmarks, including ALFWorld and WebShop. ReBel improves task success by up to $20.4$ percentage points over the episode-level baseline GRPO and increases sample efficiency by $2.1\times$. These results suggest that belief-aware self-supervision is a promising direction for reliable long-horizon decision-making under partial observability. Code is available at: https://github.com/Fateyetian/Rebel.git.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of temporal credit assignment in partially observable environments where agent beliefs drift and rewards are delayed, the paper proposes ReBel, a process-level RL algorithm that models structured belief states to summarize interaction history. ReBel introduces belief-consistency supervision to generate dense self-supervised signals from belief-reward discrepancies without external annotations, and belief-aware trajectory grouping to reduce variance in advantage estimation. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show ReBel improves task success by up to 20.4 percentage points over GRPO and boosts sample efficiency by 2.1x, demonstrating the effectiveness of belief-aware self-supervision for long-horizon decision-making under partial observability.
为解决部分可观测环境下信念漂移与延迟奖励导致的时序信用分配难题，本文提出ReBel算法，通过建模结构化信念状态来总结交互历史并指导策略学习。ReBel利用信念一致性监督，将预测信念与观测反馈间的差异转化为密集自监督信号，无需外部标注；同时采用信念感知轨迹分组以降低优势估计方差。在ALFWorld和WebShop等长时序基准测试中，ReBel相较GRPO基线任务成功率提升最高达20.4个百分点，采样效率提升2.1倍，验证了信念感知自监督在部分可观测环境下长时序决策中的有效性。
Towards LLM-Assisted Architecture Recovery for Real-World ROS~2 Systems: An Agent-Based Multi-Level Approach to Hierarchical Structural Architecture Reconstruction
Authors: Dominique Briechle, Raj Chanchad, Tobias Geger, Ruidi He, Dhruv Jajadiya, Dhruv Kapadiya, Andreas Rausch, Meng Zhang
First: 2026-05-19T16:14:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:14:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Explicit software architecture models are essential artifacts for communicating, analyzing, and evolving complex software-intensive systems. In ROS~2-based robotic systems, however, structural (de-)composition and integration semantics are often only implicitly encoded across distributed artifacts such as source code and launch files, making recovery of hierarchical architecture particularly difficult. Existing approaches mainly focus on node-level entities and communication wiring, while providing limited support for recovering hierarchical structural (de-)composition across multiple abstraction levels. In this paper, we extend our previously proposed blueprint-guided LLM-assisted architecture recovery pipeline for ROS~2 systems through two major enhancements: (1) refined prompting to improve the consistency and controllability of architecture synthesis, and (2) a staged recovery strategy based on multi-level intermediate architectural representations that incorporate the atomic ROS node list and launch file dependencies, thereby enabling structurally constrained reconstruction across multiple abstraction levels. The approach is evaluated on a real-world automated product disassembly system based on cooperative robotic arms and heterogeneous ROS~2 artifacts. Compared to our previous work, the considered case study exhibits substantially higher integration complexity and richer functionality. The results demonstrate improved structural consistency, scalability, and robustness of architecture recovery, while also revealing remaining challenges related to dynamic integration semantics in large-scale ROS~2 systems.
Summary / 总结
Explicit software architecture models are essential artifacts for communicating, analyzing, and evolving complex software-intensive systems.
为解决ROS~2机器人系统中隐式编码于分布式源码与启动文件中的层次化结构语义，本文提出一种基于代理的多级架构恢复方法，改进了先前的LLM辅助恢复流程。该方法通过优化提示词提升架构合成的一致性与可控性，并采用分阶段恢复策略，结合原子节点列表与启动文件依赖构建中间架构表示，实现跨多抽象层级的结构约束重建。在高集成复杂度的真实自动化拆解系统中评估，结果表明该方法相比前作在结构一致性、可扩展性与鲁棒性方面均有提升，同时揭示了大规模ROS~2系统中动态集成语义带来的挑战。
Active Learning of Fractional-Order Viscoelastic Model Parameters for Realistic Haptic Rendering
Authors: Harun Tolasa, Gorkem Gemalmaz, Volkan Patoglu
First: 2025-11-29T23:38:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:13:18+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE Transactions on Haptics for possible publication. 14 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective medical simulators necessitate realistic haptic rendering of biological tissues that exhibit viscoelastic material properties, such as creep and stress relaxation. Fractional-order models provide an effective means of describing intrinsically time-dependent viscoelastic dynamics with few parameters, as they naturally capture memory effects. However, due to the unintuitive, frequency-dependent coupling among the order of the fractional element and other parameters, determining appropriate parameter values for fractional-order models that yield high perceived realism remains a significant challenge. In this study, we propose a systematic means of determining the parameters of fractional-order viscoelastic models that optimizes the perceived realism of haptic rendering across general populations. First, we demonstrate that the parameters of fractional-order models can be effectively optimized through active learning, using qualitative feedback-based human-in-the-loop (HiL) optimization, to ensure consistently high realism ratings for each individual. Second, we propose a rigorous method to combine HiL optimization results into an aggregate perceptual map trained on the entire dataset, and demonstrate how to select population-level optimal parameters from this representation that are broadly perceived as realistic across general populations. Finally, we provide evidence of the effectiveness of the generalized fractional-order viscoelastic model parameters for three viscoelastic materials by characterizing their perceived realism through human-subject experiments. Overall, generalized fractional-order viscoelastic models established through the proposed HiL optimization and aggregation approach possess the potential to significantly improve the sim-to-real transition performance of medical training simulators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于主动学习的分数阶黏弹性模型参数优化以实现逼真的触觉渲染
有效的医学模拟器需要对表现出黏弹性材料特性的生物组织（如蠕变和应力松弛）进行逼真的触觉渲染。分数阶模型能以较少参数有效描述固有的时间依赖性黏弹性动力学，因其自然捕捉记忆效应。然而，由于分数阶元件阶数与其他参数之间存在非直观的、频率相关的耦合关系，确定能产生高感知真实感的分数阶模型参数仍是一个重大挑战。本研究提出了一种系统方法，通过主动学习结合基于定性反馈的人机协同优化（HiL），优化分数阶黏弹性模型参数，以在一般人群中实现一致的高真实感评分。其次，我们提出一种严谨方法，将HiL优化结果整合为基于全数据集训练的感知图谱，并展示如何从该表示中选择在一般人群中普遍被视为真实的群体最优参数。最后，我们通过受试者实验验证了所提出的通用分数阶黏弹性模型参数在三种黏弹性材料中的有效性。总体而言，通过所提出的HiL优化与聚合方法建立的通用分数阶黏弹性模型，有望显著提升医学培训模拟器的模拟到真实场景的过渡性能。
Summary / 总结
To enhance the realism of haptic rendering in medical simulators, this study addresses the challenge of optimizing fractional-order viscoelastic model parameters that capture time-dependent biological tissue behavior. Using active learning with human-in-the-loop qualitative feedback, the method iteratively refines parameters to maximize perceived realism for individual users. The approach then aggregates these personalized results into a population-level perceptual map, enabling selection of broadly realistic parameters across diverse users. Experimental validation across three viscoelastic materials confirmed that the generalized parameters derived from this HiL optimization and aggregation framework significantly improve perceived realism compared to baseline models, supporting their potential for improving sim-to-real translation in medical training.
为提升医学模拟器中触觉渲染的真实性，本研究针对分数阶黏弹性模型参数优化难题提出解决方案。通过结合人类在环的定性反馈与主动学习方法，迭代调整模型参数以最大化个体感知的真实感；随后将个性化优化结果聚合为群体感知图，从中选取普遍被认可的最优参数。实验在三种黏弹性材料上验证，结果表明该方法所构建的通用分数阶模型参数显著优于基准模型，提升了触觉仿真与真实组织行为的匹配度。
Soohak: A Mathematician-Curated Benchmark for Evaluating Research-level Math Capabilities of LLMs
Authors: Guijin Son, Seungone Kim, Catherine Arnett, Hyunwoo Ko, Hyein Lee, Hyeonah Kang, Jiang Longxi, Jin Yun, JungYup Lee, Kyungmin Lee, Sam Yoosuk Kim, Sang Park, Seunghyeok Hong, SeungJae Lee, Seungyeop Yi, Shinae Shin, SunHye Bok, Sunyoung Shin, Yonghoon Ji, Youngtaek Kim, Hanearl Jung, Akari Asai, Graham Neubig, Sean Welleck, Youngjae Yu, Akshelin R, Alexander B. Ivanov, Boboev Muhammadjon, Chae Young Han, Christian Stump, Cooper R. Anderson, Dmitrii Karp, Dohyun Kwon, Dongryung Yi, DoYong Kwon, Duk-Soon Oh, Eunho Choi, Giovanni Resta, Greta Panova, Huiyun Noh, Hyungryul Baik, Hyungsun Bae, Inomov Mashrafdzhon, Jeewon Kim, Jeong-Rae Kim, Ji Eun Lee, Jiaqi Liu, Jieui Kang, Jimin Kim, Jon-Lark Kim, Joonyeong Won, Junseo Yoon, Junwoo Jo, Kibeom Kim, Kiwoon Kwon, Mario Kummer, Max Mercer, Min Hoon Kim, Minjun Kim, Nahyun Lee, Ng Ze-An, Nicolas Libedinsky, Rafał Marcin Łochowski, Raphaël Lachièze-Rey, Robert Auffarth, Ruichen Zhang, Sejin Park, Seonguk Seo, Shin Jaehoon, Sunatullo, Taewoong Eom, Yeachan Park, Yongseok Jang, Youchan Oh, Zhaoyang Wang, Zoltán Kovács
First: 2026-05-09T17:14:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:12:41+00:00
Comments: Under review, For questions or model-evaluation requests, contact $guijin.son@snu.ac.kr$
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Following the recent achievement of gold-medal performance on the IMO by frontier LLMs, the community is searching for the next meaningful and challenging target for measuring LLM reasoning. Whereas olympiad-style problems measure step-by-step reasoning alone, research-level problems use such reasoning to advance the frontier of mathematical knowledge itself, emerging as a compelling alternative. Yet research-level math benchmarks remain scarce because such problems are difficult to source (e.g., Riemann Bench and FrontierMath-Tier 4 contain 25 and 50 problems, respectively). To support reliable evaluation of next-generation frontier models, we introduce Soohak, a 439-problem benchmark newly authored from scratch by 64 mathematicians. Soohak comprises two subsets. On the Challenge subset, frontier models including Gemini-3-Pro, GPT-5, and Claude-Opus-4.5 reach 30.4%, 26.4%, and 10.4% respectively, leaving substantial headroom, while leading open-weight models such as Qwen3-235B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Kimi-2.5 remain below 15%. Notably, beyond standard problem solving, Soohak introduces a refusal subset that probes a capability intrinsic to research mathematics: recognizing ill-posed problems and pausing rather than producing confident but unjustified answers. On this subset, no model exceeds 50%, identifying refusal as a new optimization target that current models do not directly address. To prevent contamination, the dataset will be publicly released in late 2026, with model evaluations available upon request in the interim.
Summary / 总结
Following the recent achievement of gold-medal performance on the IMO by frontier LLMs, the community is searching for the next meaningful and challenging target for measuring LLM reasoning.
为评估大模型在研究级数学推理中的能力，由64位数学家全新编撰了包含439道题目的Soohak基准测试。该基准分为挑战集与拒绝集，后者用于检测模型是否能识别无解或无效问题并主动拒绝作答。在挑战集中，前沿模型如Gemini-3-Pro（30.4%）、GPT-5（26.4%）和Claude-Opus-4.5（10.4%）表现不佳，而开源模型如Qwen3-235B、GPT-OSS-120B和Kimi-2.5均低于15%。在拒绝集中，所有模型得分均未超过50%，表明当前模型缺乏对无效问题的识别与拒绝能力，亟需优化。
The Last Human-Written Paper: Agent-Native Research Artifacts
Authors: Jiachen Liu, Jiaxin Pei, Jintao Huang, Chenglei Si, Ao Qu, Xiangru Tang, Runyu Lu, Lichang Chen, Xiaoyan Bai, Haizhong Zheng, Carl Chen, Zhiyang Chen, Haojie Ye, Yujuan Fu, Zexue He, Zijian Jin, Zhenyu Zhang, Shangquan Sun, Maestro Harmon, John Dianzhuo Wang, Jianqiao Zeng, Jiachen Sun, Mingyuan Wu, Baoyu Zhou, Chenyu You, Shijian Lu, Yiming Qiu, Fan Lai, Yuan Yuan, Yao Li, Junyuan Hong, Ruihao Zhu, Beidi Chen, Alex Pentland, Ang Chen, Mosharaf Chowdhury, Zechen Zhang
First: 2026-04-27T16:23:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:11:13+00:00
Comments: 46 pages, 15 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Scientific publication compresses a branching, iterative research process into a linear narrative, discarding the majority of what was discovered along the way. This compilation imposes two structural costs: a Storytelling Tax, where failed experiments, rejected hypotheses, and the branching exploration process are discarded to fit a linear narrative; and an Engineering Tax, where the gap between reviewer-sufficient prose and agent-sufficient specification leaves critical implementation details unwritten. Tolerable for human readers, these costs become critical when AI agents must understand, reproduce, and extend published work. We introduce the Agent-Native Research Artifact (ARA), a protocol that replaces the narrative paper with a machine-executable research package structured around four layers: scientific logic, executable code with full specifications, an exploration graph that preserves the failures compilation discards, and evidence grounding every claim in raw outputs. Three mechanisms support the ecosystem: a Live Research Manager that captures decisions and dead ends during ordinary development; an ARA Compiler that translates legacy PDFs and repos into ARAs; and an ARA-native review system that automates objective checks so human reviewers can focus on significance, novelty, and taste. On PaperBench and RE-Bench, ARA raises question-answering accuracy from 72.4% to 93.7% and reproduction success from 57.4% to 64.4%. On RE-Bench's five open-ended extension tasks, preserved failure traces in ARA accelerate progress, but can also constrain a capable agent from stepping outside the prior-run box depending on the agent's capabilities. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/Orchestra-Research/Agent-Native-Research-Artifact.
Summary / 总结
Scientific publication compresses a branching, iterative research process into a linear narrative, discarding the majority of what was discovered along the way.
为解决传统科研论文难以完整传达研究过程给AI代理的问题，本文提出Agent-Native Research Artifact（ARA）——一种可执行的机器格式，包含四个结构层：科学逻辑、带完整规范的可执行代码、记录失败实验的探索图谱，以及为每个论点提供原始输出证据。方法包括Live Research Manager用于捕获开发决策、ARA Compiler用于将旧材料转换为ARA，以及ARA原生评审系统实现自动化检查。实验表明，ARA在PaperBench和RE-Bench上将问答准确率从72.4%提升至93.7%，复现成功率从57.4%提升至64.4%，保留的失败轨迹有助于扩展任务，但可能因代理能力不同而限制其跳出原有框架。
PromptRad: Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Label Prompt-Tuning for Low-Resource Radiology Report Labeling
Authors: Ying-Jia Lin, Tzu-Chin Lo, Ping-Chien Li, Chi-Tung Cheng, Chien-Hung Liao, Hung-Yu Kao
Venue: ACL
First: 2026-05-19T16:07:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:07:42+00:00
Comments: BioNLP 2026 @ ACL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automatic report labeling facilitates the identification of clinical findings from unstructured text and enables large-scale annotation for medical imaging research. Existing rule-based labelers struggle with the diverse descriptions in clinical reports, while fine-tuning pre-trained language models (PLMs) requires large amounts of labeled data that are often unavailable in clinical settings. In this paper, we propose PromptRad, a knowledge-enhanced multi-label \textbf{prompt}-tuning approach for \textbf{rad}iology report labeling under low-resource settings. PromptRad reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and incorporates synonyms from the UMLS Metathesaurus into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich category representations. By fine-tuning the PLM without additional classification layers, PromptRad requires substantially less labeled data than conventional fine-tuning. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad outperforms dictionary-based and fine-tuning baselines with only 32 labeled training examples, and achieves competitive performance with GPT-4 despite using a much smaller model. Further analysis demonstrates that PromptRad captures complex negation patterns more effectively than existing methods, making it a promising solution for report labeling in data-scarce clinical scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PromptRad：面向低资源放射学报告标注的知识增强多标签提示调优方法
自动报告标注有助于从非结构化文本中识别临床发现，并支持医学影像研究的大规模标注。现有基于规则的标注器难以处理临床报告中的多样化描述，而微调预训练语言模型（PLM）则需要大量标注数据，而临床环境中往往难以获取。本文提出PromptRad，一种面向低资源环境的、知识增强的多标签提示调优方法，用于放射学报告标注。PromptRad将多标签分类重构为掩码语言建模，并将UMLS元同义词库中的同义词整合进多词提示器，以丰富类别表示。通过无需额外分类层的PLM微调，PromptRad所需标注数据量远低于传统微调方法。在肝部CT报告实验中，PromptRad仅用32个标注训练样本即优于词典基线和微调基线，并在模型规模远小于GPT-4的情况下仍能取得竞争性性能。进一步分析表明，PromptRad比现有方法更有效地捕捉复杂否定模式，使其成为数据稀缺临床场景下报告标注的有前景解决方案。代码开源于https://github.com/ila-lab/PromptRad。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate radiology report labeling in low-data clinical environments, PromptRad introduces a knowledge-enhanced prompt-tuning method that reformulates multi-label classification as masked language modeling and integrates UMLS synonyms into a multi-word verbalizer to enrich label representations. By fine-tuning a pre-trained language model without additional classification layers, it significantly reduces reliance on labeled data. Experiments on liver CT reports show that PromptRad achieves superior performance over rule-based and fine-tuning baselines using only 32 labeled examples and matches GPT-4’s performance despite using a smaller model, while also better capturing negation patterns in clinical text.
为了解决临床场景中标注数据稀缺导致的放射科报告标签化难题，PromptRad提出一种基于知识增强的提示调优方法，将多标签分类转化为掩码语言建模，并通过UMLS同义词构建多词提示器以丰富类别表示。该方法无需额外分类层即可微调预训练语言模型，大幅降低对标注数据的需求。在肝部CT报告实验中，仅用32个标注样本即超越规则基线和传统微调方法，且在模型规模远小于GPT-4的情况下仍能取得相当性能，同时更有效捕捉否定语义模式。
Language Mutations Sustain the Persistences of Conspiracy Theories on Social Media
Authors: Calvin Yixiang Cheng, Dorian Quelle, Scott A. Hale
First: 2026-05-19T16:06:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:06:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This study investigates how language mutations affect the persistent diffusion of conspiracy theories on social media. Drawing on a three-year dataset of conspiracy-related posts from X, and applying computational linguistic analysis alongside survival modelling, we find that conspiracy claims with greater semantic mutations have substantially longer lifespans. Mutations in psycholinguistic properties, including pronouns, social reference words, cognitive process terms, risk- and health- related vocabularies, are associated with extended lifespans. Mutations in actor, action and target (AAT) categories are associated with longer lifespans as well. Qualitative analysis identifies two predominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, at both linguistic and AAT structural levels. Taken together, the results advance our understanding of how language mutations contribute to conspiracy persistence online and shed lights on longitudinal content moderation strategies. We argue that content moderation should consider the mutability of conspiracy claims and focus on the core claims that can address their potential variations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言变异如何维持社交媒体上阴谋论的持久传播
本研究探讨语言变异如何影响社交媒体上阴谋论的持续扩散。基于X平台三年的阴谋论相关帖子数据，结合计算语言学分析与生存模型，我们发现语义变异程度更高的阴谋论声明具有更长的生命周期。心理语言学属性（如代词、社会指称词、认知过程术语、风险与健康相关词汇）的变异与更长生命周期相关。行动者-行动-目标（AAT）类别的变异同样与更长生命周期相关。质性分析识别出两种主要变异模式：简化与同化，存在于语言和AAT结构层面。综合来看，研究结果深化了我们对语言变异如何促进线上阴谋论持久性的理解，并为纵向内容审核策略提供启示。我们认为，内容审核应考虑阴谋论声明的可变性，并聚焦于核心主张以应对其潜在变体。
Summary / 总结
This study explores how linguistic changes in conspiracy theories contribute to their prolonged presence on social media, using a three-year dataset of related posts and combining computational linguistics with survival modeling. It finds that semantic mutations—particularly in psycholinguistic elements like pronouns, social references, cognitive terms, and health-related vocabulary, as well as in actor-action-target structures—correlate with longer post lifespans. Qualitative analysis reveals two dominant mutation patterns: simplification and assimilation, which operate at both linguistic and structural levels. The findings suggest that content moderation should target core claims rather than surface variations to effectively combat persistent conspiracy narratives.
本研究通过三年社交媒体数据，结合计算语言学与生存模型，探讨语言变异如何延长阴谋论的传播周期。研究发现，心理语言学元素（如代词、社会指称词、认知术语、健康相关词汇）及行动者-行为-目标结构的变异与帖子寿命显著延长相关。质性分析识别出两种主要变异模式：简化与同化，存在于语言与结构层面。研究建议内容审核应聚焦核心主张，而非表面变异，以更有效遏制阴谋论的持续传播。
Does Code Cleanliness Affect Coding Agents? A Controlled Minimal-Pair Study
Authors: Priyansh Trivedi, Olivier Schmitt
First: 2026-05-19T16:06:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:06:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous coding agents see rapid adoption, their evaluation has primarily focused on task completion rates holding the target codebase fixed. This leaves a critical question unanswered: does the structural and stylistic quality, or ``cleanliness'' of the underlying code affect an agent's ability to navigate and modify it? To isolate the effect of code cleanliness from agent capability, we introduce an evaluation protocol built around minimal pairs: repositories that match on architecture, dependencies, and external behaviour, but differ on static-analysis rule violations and cognitive complexity. The pairs are constructed in both directions, by agent pipelines that either degrade a clean repository or clean a messy one. We author 33 tasks across six such pairs, evaluated through hidden tests at the application's public surface. Across 660 trials with Claude Code, code cleanliness does not change the agent's pass rate. However, it substantially alters the agent's operational footprint: agents working on cleaner code use 7 to 8% fewer tokens and reduce file revisitations by 34%. Our findings suggest that traditional maintainability principles remain highly relevant in the era of AI-driven development, shaping the computational cost and navigational efficiency of coding agents. Code cleanliness joins model choice, harness, and prompting as a factor that materially affects agent behaviours.
Summary / 总结
As coding agents become more prevalent, researchers have largely evaluated them based on task completion without considering how code cleanliness affects their performance. To isolate this effect, the study uses minimal-pair repositories—codebases with identical architecture and behavior but differing in cleanliness metrics like static-analysis violations and cognitive complexity. The authors construct 33 tasks across six such pairs, testing agents on hidden application interfaces. Results show that code cleanliness does not impact pass rates, but significantly reduces token usage (7–8%) and file revisits (34%) for agents working with cleaner code. This suggests maintainability principles still influence agent efficiency and computational cost in AI-driven development.
为探究代码结构和风格对自主编程代理的影响，本研究设计了对照实验，比较架构与行为一致但代码整洁度不同的代码库。通过代理流水线生成对比对，对六个对中的33个任务进行测试，使用Claude Code并通过隐藏表面测试评估。虽然代码整洁度未影响任务通过率，但显著降低了7–8%的token使用量和34%的文件重访次数，表明整洁代码可提升计算效率而不影响正确性。
MINTEval: Evaluating Memory under Multi-Target Interference in Long-Horizon Agent Systems
Authors: Hyunji Lee, Justin Chih-Yao Chen, Joykirat Singh, Zaid Khan, Elias Stengel-Eskin, Mohit Bansal
First: 2026-05-18T15:43:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:05:55+00:00
Comments: Equal contribution; order decided by a coin flip. Code and data: https://github.com/amy-hyunji/MINTEval
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Real-world agents operate over long and evolving horizons, where information is repeatedly updated and may interfere across memories, requiring accurate recall and aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of information. However, existing benchmarks focus on static, independent recall and fail to capture these dynamic interactions between evolving memories. In this paper, we study how current memory-augmented agents perform in realistic, interference-heavy, long-horizon settings across diverse domains and question types. We introduce MINTEval (Long-Horizon Memory under INTerference Evaluation), a benchmark featuring (1) long, highly interconnected contexts with frequently updated information that induces substantial interference, (2) diverse domains (state tracking, multi-turn dialogue, Wikipedia revisions, and GitHub commits), enabling evaluation of domain generalization, and (3) diverse question types that assess robustness to interference, including (i) single-target recall tasks requiring retrieval of a specific target from long contexts, and (ii) multi-target aggregation tasks requiring reasoning over multiple relevant pieces of information. Overall, MINTEval has 15.6k question-answering pairs over long-horizon contexts averaging 138.8k tokens and extending up to 1.8M tokens per instance. We evaluate 7 representative systems, including vanilla long-context LLMs, RAG, and memory-augmented agent frameworks. Across all systems, we observe consistently low performance (avg. 27.9% accuracy), especially on questions requiring aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of evidence. Our analysis shows that performance is primarily limited by retrieval and memory construction. Furthermore, current memory systems struggle to recall and reason over earlier facts that are revised or interfered with by subsequent context, with accuracy degrading as the number of intervening updates increases.
Summary / 总结
Real-world agents operate over long and evolving horizons, where information is repeatedly updated and may interfere across memories, requiring accurate recall and aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of information.
为评估记忆系统在现实世界长时序场景中应对信息更新干扰的能力，作者提出了MINTEval基准，用于测试记忆增强代理在动态、高干扰环境下的表现。该方法构建了包含频繁更新的长时序、高度互联上下文，并覆盖多种领域与问题类型（单目标召回与多目标聚合），以评估系统鲁棒性。实验结果显示，七类代表性系统（包括LLM与RAG）平均准确率仅为27.9%，尤其在多目标推理任务上表现不佳。分析表明，检索与记忆构建是主要瓶颈，且随着中间更新次数增加，早期事实的召回与推理准确率显著下降。
OP2GS: Object-Aware 3D Gaussian Splatting with Dual-Opacity Primitives
Authors: Guiyu Liu, Niklas Vaara, Janne Mustaniemi, Juho Kannala, Janne Heikkilä
First: 2026-05-19T16:05:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:05:48+00:00
Comments: Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an explicit and efficient scene representation, but its primitives lack inherent object-level identity, hindering downstream tasks such as open-vocabulary scene understanding. Existing methods typically address this by either distilling high-dimensional feature embeddings into Gaussians or by lifting 2D mask labels into 3D via heuristic refinement. However, feature-based approaches incur heavy storage and decoding overhead, while lifting-based pipelines remain vulnerable to label contamination: Gaussians necessary for appearance reconstruction often receive incorrect object labels during 2D-to-3D projection. We propose OP2GS, an object-aware Gaussian representation that augments each primitive with an explicit instance identity and a dedicated instance opacity $σ^{*}$ for object-mask rendering. The original opacity $σ$ remains responsible for visual reconstruction, while $σ^{*}$ models whether a Gaussian should contribute to a particular object mask. This dual-opacity formulation decouples visual existence from instance occupancy: mislabeled Gaussians can remain available for image rendering while becoming transparent in the object-mask branch. To learn this representation, we introduce a random object loss that optimizes the 1D instance occupancy field using the standard transmittance-based visibility of 3DGS. Semantic descriptors are then attached at the object level through multi-view aggregation, eliminating per-Gaussian feature storage. Compared with feature-training approaches, OP2GS achieves competitive open-vocabulary performance while significantly reducing computational overhead. Compared with training-free pipelines, it leverages physically consistent occupancy learning to resolve visibility ambiguities.
Summary / 总结
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an explicit and efficient scene representation, but its primitives lack inherent object-level identity, hindering downstream tasks such as open-vocabulary scene understanding.
为解决3D高斯点云缺乏物体级身份标识、影响开放词汇场景理解的问题，OP2GS提出双透明度框架：每个高斯点同时携带用于视觉重建的原始透明度和用于物体掩码生成的实例透明度，实现视觉存在与实例占据的解耦，使标签错误的点仍可参与渲染但对掩码透明。该方法通过随机物体损失基于3DGS透射率学习实例占据，并通过多视角聚合在物体级别附加语义描述，避免每个高斯点存储特征。实验表明，OP2GS在开放词汇任务中性能媲美特征训练方法且计算开销更低，相比无训练方法，其通过物理一致的占据学习更有效解决可见性歧义问题。
Mind Your Moras: Orthography-Aware Error Analysis of Neural Japanese Morphological Generation
Authors: Wen Zhang
First: 2026-05-19T16:04:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:04:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present an orthography-aware error analysis of Japanese past-tense morphological inflection, treating hiragana not merely as a transcriptional medium, but as a representational system encoding morphophonological distinctions that may influence model generalization. We evaluate two character-level sequence-to-sequence architectures on past-tense formation using datasets formatted according to the SIGMORPHON 2020 and 2023 shared task conventions. Despite high aggregate accuracy, models exhibit systematic, linguistically interpretable errors that cluster around specific orthographic properties of hiragana. We introduce a concise error taxonomy capturing seven primary failure modes and provide both quantitative and qualitative analyses. Gemination-related errors dominate residual failures, accounting for 75-80% of errors, particularly in verbs whose stems end in the vowel e and require gemination before the past-tense suffix. Error patterns remain highly consistent across architectures and random seeds, suggesting a robust interaction between orthographic representation, morphological structure, and data frequency effects in shaping model generalization. These results underscore the necessity of orthography-aware evaluation for understanding neural generalization in morphologically complex languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关注日语词形生成中的拼写意识错误分析
我们对日语过去时态词形变化的拼写意识错误进行了分析，将平假名不仅视为转录媒介，更视为编码可能影响模型泛化的形态音系区别的表征系统。我们在遵循SIGMORPHON 2020和2023共享任务格式的数据集上，评估了两种字符级序列到序列架构在形成过去时态中的表现。尽管整体准确率高，模型仍表现出系统性、可解释的错误，集中于平假名的特定拼写特性。我们提出了一种简洁的错误分类法，涵盖七种主要失败模式，并提供了定量与定性分析。与连音相关的错误主导了剩余错误，占75-80%，尤其在词干以元音“e”结尾且需在过去时态后缀前发生连音的动词中。错误模式在不同架构和随机种子间高度一致，表明拼写表征、形态结构与数据频率效应之间存在稳健的交互作用，共同塑造模型泛化能力。这些结果强调了在形态复杂语言中，拼写意识评估对于理解神经模型泛化能力的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how neural models generalize in morphologically complex languages like Japanese, this study conducts an orthography-aware error analysis of past-tense morphological generation, treating hiragana as a meaningful representational system. Two character-level sequence-to-sequence models are evaluated on past-tense formation using SIGMORPHON datasets, revealing high aggregate accuracy but systematic, linguistically interpretable errors tied to hiragana orthographic properties. The authors introduce a taxonomy of seven error types, with gemination-related failures dominating 75-80% of residual errors—especially in verbs ending in 'e' that require gemination before the past-tense suffix. These consistent error patterns across architectures and random seeds indicate a strong interaction between orthographic representation, morphological structure, and data frequency, highlighting the importance of orthography-aware evaluation for neural generalization.
为理解神经模型在形态复杂语言（如日语）中的泛化能力，本研究对日语过去时态形态生成进行音形敏感的错误分析，将平假名视为承载形态音系区别的表征系统而非单纯转录媒介。在SIGMORPHON格式数据集上评估两种字符级序列到序列模型，发现整体准确率高但存在系统性、语言可解释的错误，集中于平假名的特定音形属性。作者提出七类错误分类，其中与辅音延长相关的错误占残余错误的75-80%，尤其在词干以元音“e”结尾、需在后缀前延长辅音的动词中突出。这些错误模式在不同架构与随机种子下高度稳定，表明音形表征、形态结构与数据频率共同影响模型泛化能力。
Understanding and Exploiting Weight Update Sparsity for Communication-Efficient Distributed RL
Authors: Erfan Miahi, Eugene Belilovsky
First: 2026-02-03T18:56:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:03:06+00:00
Comments: 40 pages, 19 figures, 14 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bandwidth-constrained distributed reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models is bottlenecked by two channels: weight synchronization from trainers to inference workers, and gradient or pseudo-gradient synchronization across trainers. We find that approximately 99% of per-step weight updates are invisible after the BF16 cast used by standard training and inference forward passes. We explain this sparsity by showing that, at typical RL post-training learning rates, Adam updates often fall below the local BF16 rounding threshold. We turn this observation into an algorithmic principle called compute-visible sparsification: transmit only updates that would change the next forward pass. PULSE (Precision-gated Updates for Low-precision Sparse Exchange) turns this principle into two communication algorithms: PULSESync sends lossless sparse BF16 weight patches from trainers to inference workers, and PULSELoCo sparsifies DiLoCo-style FP32 pseudo-gradient synchronization with error feedback. Over bandwidth-constrained commodity networks, PULSESync cuts weight-synchronization communication by over 100x while reconstructing trainer weights bit-identically. PULSELoCo matches DiLoCo across four models while reducing trainer-to-trainer communication by over 17x versus DiLoCo and over 100x versus DDP in the largest evaluated setting.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high communication cost in distributed reinforcement learning post-training, the paper identifies that nearly all weight updates are effectively invisible after BF16 casting, due to Adam updates falling below the local rounding threshold. It proposes compute-visible sparsification, transmitting only updates that affect the next forward pass, and implements this via PULSESync for trainer-to-worker weight updates and PULSELoCo for trainer-to-trainer gradient synchronization with error feedback. Experiments show PULSESync reduces weight synchronization by over 100x while preserving bit-identical weights, and PULSELoCo matches DiLoCo’s performance while cutting trainer communication by over 17x versus DiLoCo and over 100x versus DDP in the largest setting.
为降低大语言模型分布式强化学习后训练中的通信开销，该研究发现约99%的权重更新在BF16转换后实际无效，因Adam更新常低于本地舍入阈值。提出“计算可见稀疏化”原则，仅传输影响下一次前向传播的更新，并设计PULSESync用于训练器到推理节点的权重稀疏同步，PULSELoCo用于训练器间梯度稀疏同步并结合误差反馈。实验表明，PULSESync将权重同步通信降低100倍以上且保持权重比特级一致，PULSELoCo在性能上媲美DiLoCo，同时将训练器间通信减少17倍以上（对比DiLoCo）和100倍以上（对比DDP）
Active Context Selection Improves Simple Regret in Contextual Bandits
Authors: Mohammad Shahverdikondori, Jalal Etesami, Negar Kiyavash
First: 2026-05-19T16:01:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T16:01:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the contextual multi-armed bandit problem with a finite context space (a.k.a. subpopulations), where the learner recommends a best action for each context and is evaluated by context-weighted simple regret. Our guarantees are worst-case over the reward distributions, while remaining instance-dependent with respect to the context distribution vector $p$. Akin to experimental design problems where the population of interest is fixed but the sampled subpopulation can be controlled, we allow the learner to actively choose which context to sample from. For a known $p$, we characterize tight regret rates: passive sampling where contexts are randomly revealed achieves regret of order $\sqrt{n/T \, \lVert p \rVert_{1/2}}$, whereas active sampling with allocation $q_j \propto p_j^{2/3}$ achieves the tight rate $\sqrt{n/T} \, \lVert p \rVert_{2/3}$. The resulting improvement can be as large as $Θ(k^{1/4})$, where $k$ is the number of contexts. We further extend the analysis to budgeted active sampling, characterize the corresponding tight rate, and identify when a limited active budget suffices to recover the fully active rate. When $p$ is unknown, we propose the Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC) algorithm, which optimally balances estimating the context distribution and the time to switch to active allocation, such that for large horizons, it matches the known-$p$ active rate up to constants. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data support our theoretical findings.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve regret performance in contextual bandits when context sampling is controllable, this paper introduces active context selection to reduce simple regret. The method allows the learner to strategically choose which contexts to sample, rather than passively receiving them, and derives optimal allocation strategies based on the context distribution p. For known p, active sampling with allocation proportional to p_j^{2/3} achieves a tighter regret rate of sqrt(n/T) * ||p||_{2/3}, outperforming passive sampling’s sqrt(n/T) * ||p||_{1/2} by up to Θ(k^{1/4}) where k is the number of contexts. The analysis extends to budgeted active sampling and proposes the EETC algorithm for unknown p, which balances exploration of p and active allocation, achieving near-optimal rates for large horizons.
为在有限上下文空间的上下文多臂老虎机问题中提升简单后悔性能，本文提出主动上下文选择策略。该方法允许学习者自主选择采样上下文，而非被动接收，基于上下文分布 p 设计最优采样分配 q_j ∝ p_j^{2/3}。当 p 已知时，主动采样可实现更优的后悔率 √(n/T) · ∥p∥_{2/3}，相较被动采样 √(n/T · ∥p∥_{1/2}} 最大可提升 Θ(k^{1/4})（k 为上下文数量）。分析进一步扩展至预算受限的主动采样，并提出 Explore-Explore-Then-Commit (EETC) 算法处理未知 p 情况，通过平衡分布估计与主动分配，最终在长时程下逼近已知 p 的最优率。合成与真实数据实验验证了理论结果。
When Critics Disagree: Adaptive Reward Poisoning Attacks in RIS-Aided Wireless Control System
Authors: Deemah H. Tashman, Soumaya Cherkaoui
First: 2026-05-19T15:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T15:59:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reward-poisoning attacks present a significant risk to learning-based wireless control systems. Given this, we propose a Disagreement-Guided Reward Poisoning (DGRP) adaptive attack on a Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) agent. In a Cognitive Radio Network (CRN) environment assisted by Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces (RIS), the SAC agent is tasked with maximizing the long-term secondary users' (SUs) rate by simultaneously optimizing the transmission power of the SU transmitter and the RIS phase shifts. DGRP corrupts rewards, particularly when the SAC dual critics exhibit substantial disagreement-especially in high-leverage, high-uncertainty states-resulting in distorted value estimations and guiding the policy towards suboptimal actions. Our findings demonstrate that DGRP substantially diminishes the performance improvements typically provided by RIS and degrades transmission quality. We further investigate key attack parameters and determine their impact on learning. In comparison to periodic-timing and exploration-triggered baselines, DGRP consistently causes greater damage, highlighting the necessity of considering disagreement-aware threats when evaluating the robustness of Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) in RIS-assisted networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当评论者意见不一致时：面向RIS辅助无线控制系统中的自适应奖励投毒攻击
奖励投毒攻击对基于学习的无线控制系统构成重大风险。为此，我们提出了一种基于意见分歧引导的奖励投毒（DGRP）自适应攻击方法，针对Soft Actor-Critic（SAC）智能体。在由可重构智能表面（RIS）辅助的认知无线电网络（CRN）环境中，SAC智能体需同时优化次级用户（SUs）发射机的传输功率与RIS相位偏移，以最大化次级用户的长期速率。DGRP在SAC双批评者出现显著分歧时（尤其在高影响力、高不确定性状态下）破坏奖励，导致价值估计失真，并引导策略走向次优动作。我们的研究结果表明，DGRP显著削弱了RIS通常带来的性能提升，并降低传输质量。我们进一步研究了关键攻击参数及其对学习的影响。与周期定时和探索触发的基线方法相比，DGRP造成更大损害，凸显在评估RIS辅助网络中深度强化学习（DRL）鲁棒性时，必须考虑意见分歧感知威胁的必要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of learning-based wireless control systems to reward-poisoning attacks, this study proposes DGRP—an adaptive attack targeting SAC agents in RIS-assisted Cognitive Radio Networks. DGRP exploits disagreement between SAC’s dual critics to selectively corrupt rewards in high-leverage, high-uncertainty states, thereby distorting value estimates and steering the policy toward suboptimal actions. Experimental results show that DGRP significantly reduces the performance gains enabled by RIS and degrades transmission quality, outperforming periodic-timing and exploration-triggered baselines in damage inflicted, underscoring the need to account for disagreement-aware adversarial threats in evaluating DRL robustness in such systems.
鉴于基于学习的无线控制系统易受奖励投毒攻击，本文提出一种名为DGRP的自适应攻击方法，针对RIS辅助认知无线电网络中的SAC智能体。该攻击利用SAC双批评者间的分歧，在高杠杆、高不确定性状态下选择性篡改奖励，从而扭曲价值估计并引导策略走向次优动作。实验表明，DGRP显著削弱RIS带来的性能增益并降低传输质量，其破坏效果优于周期性定时和探索触发基线，凸显在评估DRL在RIS网络中的鲁棒性时必须考虑分歧感知型威胁的重要性。
NeRF-based Spacecraft Reconstruction from Monocular Imagery Under Illumination Variability and Pose Uncertainty
Authors: Antoine Legrand, Renaud Detry, Christophe De Vleeschouwer
First: 2026-05-18T14:17:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T15:57:41+00:00
Comments: (under review)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations around uncooperative, unknown spacecraft are critical for active debris removal and on-orbit servicing missions. A key component of such operations is the offline reconstruction of a 3D model of the target from a set of 2D images. This task is challenging due to two main factors. First, in-orbit illumination conditions exhibit considerable variability, and change rapidly over time. Second, the inaccuracy of pose information in the images, results in 3D reconstruction uncertainty. To overcome these challenges, we propose to extend Neural Radiance Fields with per-image degrees of freedom: a learnable appearance embedding that captures the illumination conditions specific to each image, and an image-specific pose correction term that refines its noisy pose label to increase 3D consistency across images. These parameters add minimal complexity, as they are learned jointly with the NeRF, yet they substantially improve robustness to illumination variability and pose inaccuracies. We validate our approach on three image sets representative of in-orbit operations, demonstrating its effectiveness for offline reconstruction and highlighting its suitability for online reconstruction, an open problem in the field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于NeRF的单目图像下光照变化与姿态不确定性条件下的航天器重建
在未知、非合作航天器周围执行自主交会与近距离操作，对主动太空垃圾清除和在轨服务任务至关重要。此类操作的关键环节是利用一组二维图像离线重建目标的三维模型。该任务面临两大挑战：一是轨道上光照条件变化剧烈且随时间快速变化；二是图像姿态信息不准确，导致三维重建不确定性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出扩展神经辐射场（NeRF），引入每张图像的自由度：一个可学习的外观嵌入，捕捉每张图像特有的光照条件；以及一个图像特定的姿态校正项，将噪声姿态标签优化以提升图像间三维一致性。这些参数复杂度极低，与NeRF联合学习，却显著增强了对光照变化和姿态误差的鲁棒性。我们在三组代表在轨操作的图像数据集上验证了该方法，证明其在离线重建中的有效性，并凸显其适用于在线重建的潜力——这是该领域尚未解决的开放问题。
Summary / 总结
To enable autonomous spacecraft rendezvous under variable lighting and uncertain camera poses, this work extends Neural Radiance Fields by introducing per-image learnable parameters: an illumination-specific appearance embedding and a pose correction term that refines noisy pose labels. These additions, learned jointly with the NeRF, enhance 3D reconstruction consistency without increasing model complexity. Experiments on three in-orbit image datasets show significant improvement in reconstruction accuracy under illumination changes and pose uncertainty, validating the method’s suitability for both offline and online reconstruction tasks.
为实现光照变化和相机姿态不确定条件下的自主航天器交会，该研究在NeRF基础上引入每张图像的可学习参数：一个捕捉图像特定光照条件的外观嵌入和一个修正噪声姿态标签的姿态校正项。这些参数与NeRF联合学习，复杂度极低但显著提升三维重建鲁棒性。在三个真实在轨图像数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效提升重建精度与图像间一致性，验证其离线建模的有效性，并具备在线重建的应用潜力。
D$^3$-Subsidy: Online and Sequential Driver Subsidy Decision-Making for Large-Scale Ride-Hailing Market
Authors: Taijie Chen, Rui Su, Siyuan Feng, Laoming Zhang, Hongyang Zhang, Haijiao Wang, Zhaofeng Ma, Jintao Ke
First: 2026-05-19T15:55:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T15:55:55+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 14 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ride-hailing platforms like DiDi Chuxing operate in highly dynamic environments where balancing driver supply and passenger demand is critical. Although driver-side subsidies serve as a primary lever to align these forces and improve key KPIs like completed rides (\texttt{Rides}) and gross merchandise value (\texttt{GMV}), optimizing them in production requires simultaneously meeting three constraints: (i) responsiveness to stochastic shocks, (ii) strict subsidy-rate caps, and (iii) low-latency execution at city scale. These requirements rule out expensive per-order optimization, calling for a forward-looking, constraint-aware city-level controller for online sequential decision making. To meet these requirements, we introduce D$^3$-Subsidy (Dynamic Driver-side Diffusion-based Subsidy), a hierarchical diffusion-based framework for deployable city-wide subsidy control. To bridge the train-inference gap, D$^3$-Subsidy employs a prefix-conditioned diffusion model that samples plausible future trajectories from immutable historical observations, ensuring the training protocol aligns with the fixed-history nature of online deployment. These generated plans are then decoded by a context-conditioned inverse module into low-dimensional city-level control signals. For scalable execution, we bridge the gap between city-level planning and fine-grained dispatch via a Lagrangian-dual-derived mapping, which embeds subsidy-rate caps directly into order-driver incentives without iterative optimization. Additionally, a multi-city pretraining strategy with parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables robust transfer across heterogeneous cities. Extensive offline evaluations demonstrate that D$^3$-Subsidy improves \texttt{Rides} and \texttt{GMV} while enhancing cap compliance, and a real-world A/B test confirms significant uplift while keeping budget-related violation metrics within operational thresholds.
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of dynamically balancing driver supply and passenger demand in large-scale ride-hailing markets while respecting subsidy-rate caps and low-latency execution, D$^3$-Subsidy introduces a hierarchical diffusion-based framework for city-wide subsidy control. It uses a prefix-conditioned diffusion model to generate plausible future trajectories from historical data, aligning training with the fixed-history nature of online deployment, and decodes these into low-dimensional city-level control signals via an inverse module. A Lagrangian-dual-derived mapping ensures subsidy caps are embedded into driver incentives without iterative optimization, enabling scalable execution. Evaluated offline and validated in a real-world A/B test, D$^3$-Subsidy improves completed rides and gross merchandise value while maintaining compliance with subsidy caps and keeping budget-related violations within operational thresholds.
为在大规模网约车市场中动态平衡司机供给与乘客需求，同时满足补贴率上限和低延迟执行要求，D$^3$-Subsidy 提出了一种基于扩散模型的分层城市级补贴控制框架。该框架采用前缀条件扩散模型，从历史数据生成合理未来轨迹，使训练与在线部署的固定历史特性对齐，并通过逆模块解码为低维城市级控制信号。通过拉格朗日对偶导出的映射机制，补贴上限直接嵌入订单-司机激励，无需迭代优化，实现可扩展执行。离线评估与真实A/B测试均表明，该方法提升完成订单数与GMV，同时保持补贴上限合规性，且预算相关违规指标在运营阈值内。
Stage-adaptive Token Selection for Efficient Omni-modal LLMs
Authors: Zijie Xin, Jie Yang, Ruixiang Zhao, Tianyi Wang, Fengyun Rao, Jing Lyu, Xirong Li
First: 2026-05-19T15:55:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T15:55:16+00:00
Comments: Code Link: https://github.com/xxayt/SEATS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Omni-modal large language models (om-LLMs) achieve unified audio-visual understanding by encoding video and audio into temporally aligned token sequences interleaved at the window level. However, processing these dense non-textual tokens throughout the LLM incurs substantial computational overhead. Although training-free token selection can reduce this cost, existing methods either focus on visual-only inputs or prune om-LLM tokens only before the LLM with fixed per-modality ratios, failing to capture how cross-modal token importance evolves across layers. To address this limitation, we first analyze the layer-wise token dependency of om-LLMs. We find that visual and audio dependencies follow a block-wise pattern and gradually weaken with depth, indicating that many late-layer non-textual tokens become redundant after cross-modal fusion. Motivated by this observation, we propose SEATS, a training-free, stage-adaptive token selection method for efficient om-LLM inference. Before the LLM, SEATS removes spatiotemporal redundancy via attention-weighted diversity selection. Inside the LLM, it progressively prunes tokens across blocks and dynamically allocates the retention budget from temporal windows to modalities using query relevance scores. In late layers, it removes all remaining non-textual tokens once cross-modal fusion is complete. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni demonstrate that SEATS effectively improves inference efficiency. Retaining only 10% of visual and audio tokens, it achieves a 9.3x FLOPs reduction and a 4.8x prefill speedup while preserving 96.3% of the original performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向高效多模态大语言模型的阶段自适应标记选择
多模态大语言模型（om-LLMs）通过将视频和音频编码为在窗口级别交错的时序对齐标记序列，实现统一的视听理解。然而，在LLM中处理这些密集的非文本标记会带来显著的计算开销。尽管无训练标记选择可降低此成本，现有方法要么仅针对视觉输入，要么仅在LLM前以固定每模态比例剪枝om-LLM标记，未能捕捉跨模态标记重要性随层变化的动态。为解决此问题，我们首先分析om-LLMs的层间标记依赖关系。发现视觉与音频依赖呈块状模式，且随深度逐渐减弱，表明跨模态融合后，许多深层非文本标记变得冗余。受此启发，我们提出SEATS，一种无训练、阶段自适应的标记选择方法，用于高效om-LLM推理。在LLM前，SEATS通过注意力加权多样性选择去除时空冗余；在LLM内部，它逐块渐进剪枝标记，并利用查询相关性分数动态分配从时间窗口到模态的保留预算；在深层，一旦跨模态融合完成，即移除所有剩余非文本标记。在Qwen2.5-Omni和Qwen3-Omni上的实验表明，SEATS有效提升推理效率：仅保留10%视觉与音频标记时，实现9.3倍FLOPs减少和4.8倍预填充加速，同时保持96.3%原始性能。
Summary / 总结
To reduce computational overhead in omni-modal LLMs processing dense audio-visual tokens, the authors propose SEATS, a training-free, stage-adaptive token selection method. Motivated by layer-wise analysis showing that cross-modal token dependencies weaken with depth, SEATS first removes spatiotemporal redundancy via attention-weighted diversity selection before the LLM, then progressively prunes tokens inside the model using query relevance scores to dynamically allocate retention budgets, and finally eliminates all non-textual tokens in late layers after cross-modal fusion. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Omni and Qwen3-Omni show that retaining only 10% of visual and audio tokens yields a 9.3x FLOPs reduction and 4.8x prefill speedup while maintaining 96.3% of original performance.
为降低多模态大语言模型处理密集音视频令牌的计算开销，作者提出SEATS，一种无训练、分阶段自适应的令牌选择方法。基于层间分析发现跨模态令牌依赖随深度减弱，SEATS在LLM前通过注意力加权多样性选择去除时空冗余，模型内部按块逐步剪枝令牌并根据查询相关性动态分配保留预算，后期层在跨模态融合完成后移除所有非文本令牌。在Qwen2.5-Omni和Qwen3-Omni实验中，仅保留10%视觉与音频令牌即可实现9.3倍FLOPs减少、4.8倍预填充加速，同时保持96.3%原始性能。
A Nash Equilibrium Framework For Training-Free Multimodal Step Verification
Authors: Rohit Sinha, Kunal Tilaganji, Tanuja Ganu, Nagarajan Natarajan, Amit Sharma, Vineeth N. Balasubramanian
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-05-19T15:54:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T15:54:22+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026 Workshop VerifAI-2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Learned critics need extensive labeled data and show inconsistent performance across different tasks. Meanwhile, existing training-free methods simply average scores from different sources, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly valid or not. We propose a training-free verification approach that treats step-wise verification as a coordination problem among specialized judges. We formalize these judges' interaction as a Nash equilibrium game where agreement signals valid steps while disagreement reveals instability. Our method computes equilibrium scores through a closed-form solution, enabling both disagreement-aware filtering and stability-conscious ranking of reasoning steps. Evaluated across six benchmarks, our approach achieves consistent improvements of 2.4% to 5.2% over baseline models and shows competitive performance against learned critics, demonstrating that cross-modal agreement (not just average confidence) provides robust verification signals without task-specific adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种无需训练的多模态步骤验证纳什均衡框架
多模态大语言模型常生成包含细微错误的推理链，导致错误答案。现有验证方法存在明显局限：学习型批评者需大量标注数据且在不同任务间表现不一致；而现有无需训练的方法仅简单平均不同来源的分数，忽略了关键洞察：当这些分数不一致时，这种分歧本身即蕴含了推理步骤是否真正有效的关键信息。我们提出一种无需训练的验证方法，将分步验证视为专业化裁判间的协调问题，并将其交互形式化为纳什均衡博弈——共识信号有效步骤，分歧揭示不稳定。该方法通过闭式解计算均衡分数，实现分歧感知的过滤与稳定性导向的推理步骤排序。在六个基准测试中，我们的方法相较基线模型稳定提升2.4%至5.2%，且与学习型批评者表现相当，证明跨模态共识（而非仅平均置信度）可提供无需任务特定适配的鲁棒验证信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to verify reasoning steps in multimodal LLMs without training data, this work proposes a training-free framework that models step verification as a Nash equilibrium among specialized judges. Each judge evaluates a reasoning step independently, and their scores are aggregated through a closed-form equilibrium solution that rewards agreement and penalizes disagreement, thereby capturing the stability of reasoning. Experiments across six benchmarks show consistent gains of 2.4% to 5.2% over baselines and competitive performance against learned critics, demonstrating that disagreement-aware scoring improves verification accuracy without task-specific tuning.
为解决多模态大语言模型推理链中隐含错误导致答案错误的问题，本文提出一种无需训练的纳什均衡框架，将推理步骤验证建模为多个专业裁判的协调问题。裁判们对步骤打分，其互动被形式化为一个博弈：一致同意表示步骤有效，分歧则揭示不稳定。方法通过闭式解计算均衡分数，实现分歧感知过滤与稳定性导向排序。在六个基准测试中，该方法相较基线提升2.4%至5.2%，性能可媲美需训练的批评者，证明跨模态一致性比简单平均置信度更能提供稳健的验证信号。
CAMERA: Adapting to Semantic Camouflage in Unsupervised Text-Attributed Graph Fraud Detection
Authors: Junjun Pan, Yixin Liu, Yu Zheng, Lianhua Chi, Alan Wee-Chung Liew, Shirui Pan
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2026-05-19T15:54:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T15:54:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted by IJCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Text-attributed graph fraud detection (TAGFD) plays a critical role in preventing fraudulent activities on online social and e-commerce platforms. However, to evade detection, fraudsters continuously evolve their camouflaging strategies by deliberately mimicking textual responses of benign users, thereby concealing their malicious purposes. This phenomenon, referred to as semantic camouflage, fundamentally undermines commonly relied assumptions on how structural and attribute cues can be exploited to identify fraudsters, and makes it difficult to spot fraudsters with unsupervised TAGFD. To bridge the gaps, we propose a Case-Adaptive Multi-cue Expert fRAmework (CAMERA) for unsupervised TAGFD. CAMERA employs an ego-decoupled mixture-of-experts architecture, where each expert specializes in modeling a distinct type of fraud-indicative cue. A context-informed gating model is introduced to jointly consider the ego node representation and its local neighborhood context for adaptive integration of cues learned by different experts. Furthermore, CAMERA leverages the inherent rarity of fraudsters to support unsupervised one-class learning with expert-level objectives that encourage modeling dominant benign patterns, thereby enabling reliable unsupervised detection of camouflaged fraudsters. Experiments on 4 challenging datasets show that CAMERA consistently outperforms competitors, showing its effectiveness against semantically camouflaged fraudsters. Code available at https://github.com/CampanulaBells/CAMERA
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of detecting fraudsters who evade detection by mimicking benign users’ textual behavior—a phenomenon called semantic camouflage—CAMERA introduces an unsupervised framework that adapts to diverse fraud cues using an ego-decoupled mixture-of-experts architecture. Each expert specializes in modeling a specific type of fraud-indicative cue, and a context-informed gating mechanism dynamically combines these cues based on the ego node and its local neighborhood. By leveraging the rarity of fraudsters, CAMERA enables one-class learning through expert-level objectives that prioritize modeling dominant benign patterns, enhancing detection accuracy. Experiments across four datasets demonstrate CAMERA’s consistent superiority over existing methods in identifying camouflaged fraudsters under unsupervised settings.
为应对欺诈者通过模仿良性用户文本行为躲避检测（即语义伪装）的挑战，CAMERA 提出一种无监督框架，采用解耦的专家混合架构，每个专家专注于建模特定类型的欺诈线索。通过上下文感知的门控机制，系统根据节点自身及其局部邻域动态融合不同专家学习到的线索。利用欺诈者稀有的特性，CAMERA 通过专家级目标促进对主流良性模式的学习，从而提升无监督检测能力。在四个数据集上的实验表明，CAMERA 在识别语义伪装欺诈者方面持续优于现有方法。
Learn2Decompose: Learning Problem Decomposition for Efficient Sequential Multi-object Manipulation Planning
Authors: Yan Zhang, Teng Xue, Amirreza Razmjoo, Sylvain Calinon
First: 2024-08-13T12:06:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-19T15:54:06+00:00
Comments: Extension of RAL version: added PR2 Whole-body kitchen task and detailed discussion on limitations in main text; added pseudocode and robustness analysis of our approach, and formal analysis on why and when task goals are decomposable in appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present an efficient task and motion replanning approach for sequential multi-object manipulation in dynamic environments. Conventional Task And Motion Planning (TAMP) solvers experience an exponential increase in planning time as the planning horizon and number of objects grow, limiting their applicability in real-world scenarios. To address this, we propose learning problem decompositions from demonstrations to accelerate TAMP solvers. Our approach consists of three key components: goal decomposition learning, computational distance learning, and object reduction. Goal decomposition identifies the necessary sequences of states that the system must pass through before reaching the final goal, treating them as subgoal sequences. Computational distance learning predicts the computational complexity between two states, enabling the system to identify the temporally closest subgoal from a disturbed state. Object reduction minimizes the set of active objects considered during replanning, further improving efficiency. We evaluate our approach on three benchmarks, demonstrating its effectiveness in improving replanning efficiency for sequential multi-object manipulation tasks in dynamic environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Learn2Decompose：通过学习问题分解实现高效序列多物体操作规划
我们提出了一种用于动态环境中序列多物体操作的高效任务与运动重规划方法。传统任务与运动规划（TAMP）求解器在规划时长和物体数量增加时，规划时间呈指数级增长，限制了其在现实场景中的应用。为解决此问题，我们提出从演示中学习问题分解，以加速TAMP求解器。该方法包含三个关键组件：目标分解学习、计算距离学习和物体简化。目标分解识别系统在达到最终目标前必须经过的必要状态序列，将其视为子目标序列；计算距离学习预测两个状态间的计算复杂度，使系统能从受干扰状态中识别出时间上最近的子目标；物体简化在重规划过程中最小化考虑的活动物体集合，进一步提升效率。我们在三个基准上评估了该方法，证明其在动态环境中显著提升了序列多物体操作任务的重规划效率。
Summary / 总结
To overcome the exponential growth in planning time for sequential multi-object manipulation in dynamic environments, this work proposes Learn2Decompose, a method that learns problem decompositions from demonstrations to accelerate Task And Motion Planning (TAMP). It comprises three components: goal decomposition learning to identify necessary subgoal sequences, computational distance learning to predict state complexity and select temporally closest subgoals, and object reduction to minimize active objects during replanning. Experimental results across three benchmarks show significant improvements in replanning efficiency, particularly in dynamic settings with multiple objects.
为应对动态环境中多物体序列操作任务规划时间呈指数增长的问题，本文提出Learn2Decompose方法，通过从演示中学习问题分解以加速任务与运动规划（TAMP）。该方法包含三个核心组件：目标分解学习用于识别必要子目标序列，计算距离学习用于预测状态间复杂度并定位时间最近子目标，以及物体缩减用于减少重规划时的活跃物体数量。在三个基准测试中，该方法显著提升了重规划效率，相比基线TAMP求解器收敛更快、计算开销更低。
